[UCE_SRC_GD] All original GD assumptions, with unchanged residual genericity and small-prime scope
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/HeightAndVanishing/Counts.lean#L9-L22
structure GD (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (e : ℕ) {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  e_pos : 1 ≤ e
  N_even : Even N
  ord : ¬ l ∣ orderOf ψ
  ine : comp l ψ ^ 2 = 1
  par : PsiEven e ψ
  quad : Module.finrank ℚ K = 2
  imag : IsTotallyComplex K
  l_cop : ¬ l ∣ (discr K).natAbs * classNumber K
  cop : (discr K).natAbs.Coprime N
  eta : ¬ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker ⊓ (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (chiP l ψ)).ker ≤ galOf ℚ K Ω
  gen : ¬ l - 1 ∣ 2 * e


[UCE_SRC_DAT] Actual Dat, ULoc, Ring, Marked, rhoK, IsExt, IsAutI and IsModular
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/RhoKApply.lean#L16-L94
structure Dat (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) where
  gd : GD Ω K l e ψ
  η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ
  η_ker : η.ker = galOf ℚ K Ω
  cc : G ℚ Ω
  conj : ∃ φ : Ω →+* ℂ, ∀ x, φ (cc x) = starRingEnd ℂ (φ x)
  j : Ω →+* Om l
  ι : GQ l →* G ℚ Ω
  ι_cont : Continuous ι
  ι_spec : ∀ σ x, σ (j x) = j (ι σ x)
  Kv : FinExt l
  Kv_spec : ∀ g, η (ι g) = 1 ↔ g ∈ GK Kv
  η_unr : ∀ g ∈ Loc.Iq l, η (ι g) = 1
  τ_open : IsOpen (((Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (comp l ψ)).comp ι).ker : Set (GQ l))
  Ψ_cont : CharCont (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ))
  Ψ_I : ∀ g ∈ Loc.Iq l, Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) (ι g) =
    Loc.cycA l l c.O g ^ e * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (comp l ψ) (ι g)

namespace Dat

variable {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
  {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ)

abbrev Ψ (_ : Dat Ω K c e ψ) : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ := Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)

def ΨQ : G ℚ Ω →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ := (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom).comp D.Ψ

abbrev loc : Loc.Dat3 l c.O where
  η := D.η.comp D.ι
  Ψ := D.Ψ.comp D.ι
  τ := (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (comp l ψ)).comp D.ι
  e := e
  Kv := D.Kv
  odd := D.gd.l_odd
  e_pos := D.gd.e_pos
  η_ker := D.Kv_spec
  η_unr := D.η_unr
  τ_open := D.τ_open
  Ψ_cont n := by
    have h := (D.Ψ_cont 1 n).comp_continuous D.ι_cont
    simp only [zpow_one] at h
    exact h
  Ψ_I := D.Ψ_I

def Kill : Set (G ℚ Ω) :=
  {g | ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧ rbar c.O D.η D.Ψ g = 1}

abbrev ULoc : Type 1 := Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (D.η.comp D.ι) (D.Ψ.comp D.ι))

abbrev Ring (U : D.ULoc) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Type 1 := SRing D.Kill D.ι_cont U (D.loc.It U) D.cc g₁

abbrev Marked (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Prop := Marks D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁

def rhoK {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 κ) (hr : HasMult D.η r) : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) κ :=
  (glPart D.η r hr).comp ((galEmb ℚ K Ω).codRestrict D.η.ker fun s => D.η_ker ▸ galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω s)

theorem rhoK_apply {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 κ) (hr : HasMult D.η r) (s : G K Ω) :
    D.rhoK r hr s = (r (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).left := rfl

structure IsPt (U : D.ULoc) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)) : Prop where
  mult : HasMult D.η r
  cont : Continuous (mats r)
  int : ∀ g a b, ‖(r g).mat a b‖ ≤ 1
  red : ∀ g a b, ‖(r g).mat a b - (r0 D.η D.ΨQ g).mat a b‖ < 1
  norm : Normalised D.cc g₁ r
  kill : ∀ g ∈ D.Kill, r g = 1
  loc : ∃ f : U.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt f ∧ D.loc.It U ≤ RingHom.ker f ∧ push f U.r.hom = r.comp D.ι

def IsExt {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 κ) (W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) κ) : Prop :=
  (∀ g ∈ D.η.ker, (W g : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) κ) = corner (r g).mat) ∧ (W D.cc : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) κ).trace = 0

def IsAutI [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)) (hr : HasMult D.η r) : Prop :=
  Af.IsAutCsd ι (Af.tw (D.rhoK r hr) (cycBar K Ω l)⁻¹)

def IsModular (_ : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l)) : Prop :=
  ∃ f : Af.Newform (2 * e + 1), f.Att ι (Af.tw W (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ (-(e : ℤ))))

end Dat


[UCE_SRC_DAT_LOCAL] The specified local embedding, decomposition group, inertia and chosen valuation ring
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/DatLoc.lean#L8-L45
theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.dat_loc :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ),
      ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ D.ι.range = Dec (F := ℚ) A ∧ (Loc.Iq l).map D.ι = Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧
        Function.Injective D.ι ∧ (∀ x : Ω, x ∈ A ↔ ‖D.j x‖ ≤ 1) ∧ (∀ σ, cycZ ℚ Ω l (D.ι σ) = Loc.cyc l l σ) ∧
        ∀ A' : ValuationSubring Ω, A'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ g : G ℚ Ω,
          (∀ s, s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A' ↔ g⁻¹ * s * g ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A) ∧ ∀ s, s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A' ↔ g⁻¹ * s * g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D
  have hι : D.ι = Loc.iota D.j := by
    ext σ x
    apply D.j.injective
    rw [Loc.iota_spec, ← D.ι_spec]
  obtain ⟨hemb, hrange, -, hmap, hcyc, -, -⟩ := Loc.iotaFacts D.j
  have hmem : ∀ x : Ω, x ∈ Loc.placeJ D.j ↔ Valued.v (D.j x) ≤ 1 := fun x => Iff.rfl
  have hA : (Loc.placeJ D.j).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := by
    have hnot : (l : Ω)⁻¹ ∉ Loc.placeJ D.j := by
      rw [hmem, map_inv₀, map_natCast, map_inv₀, PadicAlgCl.valuation_p, not_le]
      have hl : (1 : NNReal) < l := by exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt
      simpa using hl
    by_contra h
    push_neg at h
    apply hnot
    have hle : (Loc.placeJ D.j).valuation (l : Ω) ≤ 1 :=
      ((Loc.placeJ D.j).valuation_le_one_iff _).2 (natCast_mem _ l)
    rw [← ValuationSubring.valuation_le_one_iff, map_inv₀, le_antisymm hle h, inv_one]
  refine ⟨Loc.placeJ D.j, hA, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [hι]; exact hrange
  · rw [hι]; exact hmap
  · rw [hι]; exact hemb.injective
  · intro x
    rw [hmem, ← NNReal.coe_le_coe, PadicAlgCl.valuation_coe, NNReal.coe_one]
  · intro σ
    rw [hι]
    exact DFunLike.congr_fun (hcyc l) σ
  · intro A' hA'
    obtain ⟨-, -, -, -, -, -, -, hconj, -, hact⟩ := ML.placeBasic Ω
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hconj (Loc.placeJ D.j) A' l Fact.out hA hA'
    exact ⟨g, (hact _ g).1, (hact _ g).2.1⟩


[UCE_SRC_DAT_FACTS] Original inertia separator, cyclotomic and residual consequences of GD
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/DatFacts.lean#L30-L133
private theorem cyc_ine (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) {σ : G ℚ Ω} (hσ : σ ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A) :
    cycZ ℚ Ω l σ = 1 := by
  have : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  have : NeZero ((l : ℕ) : Ω) := ⟨Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero⟩
  show cyclotomicCharacter Ω l σ.toRingEquiv = 1
  refine Units.ext (PadicInt.ext_of_toZModPow.mp fun n => ?_)
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω (l ^ n)
  have hN0 : l ^ n ≠ 0 := pow_ne_zero _ (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero
  have : NeZero (l ^ n) := ⟨hN0⟩
  have hspec := cyclotomicCharacter.spec (L := Ω) l σ.toRingEquiv ζ hζ.pow_eq_one
  have hv : ¬ A.valuation ((l ^ n : ℕ) : Ω) < 1 := by
    rw [Nat.cast_pow, map_pow, not_lt]
    exact one_le_pow₀ (not_lt.1 hA)
  have h1 : Chr.cls ℚ Ω (l ^ n) σ = 1 := (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω (l ^ n)).2.2.2.2 A hv hσ
  have h2 := Chr.cls_spec (l ^ n) σ ζ hζ.pow_eq_one
  rw [h1] at h2
  have h3 := hζ.pow_inj (ZMod.val_lt _) (ZMod.val_lt _) (hspec.symm.trans h2)
  rw [ZMod.val_injective _ h3]
  simp

private theorem tor_eq_one {R : Type} [CommRing R] {t : Rˣ} (h : tor R t = 1) : t = 1 := by
  have h1 := congrArg (fun x : GL (Fin 3) R => (x : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) 0 0) h
  apply Units.ext
  simpa [tor_val, wt1] using h1

private theorem diagonal_rep_eq_one_iff {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Rˣ) (g : Γ) :
    r0 η Ψ g = 1 ↔ η g = 1 ∧ Ψ g = 1 := by
  refine ⟨fun h => ⟨congrArg SemidirectProduct.right h, tor_eq_one (congrArg SemidirectProduct.left h)⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
  refine SemidirectProduct.ext ?_ h.1
  rw [unitaryThreeReflection_left, h.2, map_one]
  rfl

private theorem bar_lc {O Γ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ)
    (hΨ : CharCont Ψ) : IsLocallyConstant (barChar O Ψ) := by
  refine IsLocallyConstant.desc _ Units.val ?_ Units.val_injective
  have h := (hΨ 1 1).comp (Ideal.Quotient.factor (pow_one (maximalIdeal O)).le)
  simp only [zpow_one] at h
  exact h

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.dat_facts :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ),
      D.cc * D.cc = 1 ∧ D.η D.cc = -1 ∧ D.Ψ D.cc = 1 ∧ IsLocallyConstant D.η ∧ IsLocallyConstant (barChar c.O D.Ψ) ∧
      (∀ g ∈ D.Kill, r0 D.η D.Ψ g = 1) ∧ IsUnit (2 : c.O) ∧ IsCMField K ∧ Continuous D.ΨQ ∧
      (∀ g, g ∈ D.Kill ↔ ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧ D.η g = 1 ∧ Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g = 1) ∧
      (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ D.η.ker ∧
        ∃ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, IsUnit ((D.Ψ s : c.O) ^ 2 - 1)) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ D
  obtain ⟨w, η', a, hlit⟩ := (gdIff Ω K l c e N ψ).1.1 D.gd
  have himag := D.gd.imag
  have hΨ : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A,
      D.Ψ g = Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g := fun A hA g hg => by
    show (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g) ^ (e : ℤ) * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g = _
    have : chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g = 1 := by
      unfold chiO
      rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, cyc_ine Ω l A hA hg, map_one]
    rw [this, _root_.one_zpow, one_mul]
  have hkill : ∀ g, g ∈ D.Kill ↔ ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧
      D.η g = 1 ∧ Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g = 1 := fun g => by
    refine exists_congr fun A => and_congr_right fun hA => and_congr_right fun hg => ?_
    unfold rbar
    rw [diagonal_rep_eq_one_iff]
    refine and_congr_right fun _ => ?_
    unfold barChar
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, hΨ A hA g hg]
    refine ⟨fun h => ?_, fun h => by rw [h, map_one]⟩
    refine (congrFacts Ω l c).2.2.2.1 _ 1 _ hlit.ord ?_ (one_pow _) ?_
    · rw [← MonoidHom.pow_apply, pow_orderOf_eq_one, MonoidHom.one_apply]
    · rw [← residue_eq_zero_iff, map_sub, sub_eq_zero, Units.val_one, map_one]
      exact congrArg Units.val h
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := D.conj
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, hlit.par D.cc ⟨φ, hφ⟩, ?_, ?_, fun g hg => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, hkill, fun A hA => ?_⟩
  · refine AlgEquiv.ext fun x => φ.injective ?_
    rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hφ, hφ]
    simp
  · refine (Int.units_eq_one_or _).resolve_left fun h => ?_
    have h1 : D.cc ∈ galOf ℚ K Ω := D.η_ker ▸ h
    refine IsTotallyComplex.complexEmbedding_not_isReal (φ.comp (algebraMap K Ω)) ?_
    rw [ComplexEmbedding.isReal_iff]
    ext x
    rw [ComplexEmbedding.conjugate_coe_eq, RingHom.comp_apply, ← hφ, h1 x]
  · exact locConst _ (D.η_ker ▸ open_galOf Ω K)
  · exact bar_lc _ D.Ψ_cont
  · obtain ⟨A, hA, hg, h1, h2⟩ := (hkill g).1 hg
    exact (diagonal_rep_eq_one_iff _ _ _).2 ⟨h1, (hΨ A hA g hg).trans h2⟩
  · have := (dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 2 fun h => D.gd.l_odd ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hl.out Nat.prime_two).1 h)
    simpa using this
  · have : Algebra.IsQuadraticExtension ℚ K := ⟨D.gd.quad⟩
    exact IsCMField.ofCMExtension ℚ K
  · have hf : Continuous (algebraMap ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom := by
      refine ((continuous_algebraMap ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)).comp continuous_subtype_val).congr fun x => ?_
      exact (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l) x).symm
    have h1 := ((hf.units_map _).comp (continuous_cycZ ℚ Ω l)).zpow (e : ℤ)
    have h2 := (((locConst _ (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).1).comp ψ.toUnitHom).comp
      (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom)).continuous
    refine (h1.mul h2).congr fun g => ?_
    show _ = Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g ^ (e : ℤ) * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g)
    rw [map_mul, map_zpow]
    rfl
  · obtain ⟨h1, s, hs, h2⟩ := (gdLoc Ω K l c e N ψ w η' a hlit).1 A hA
    refine ⟨D.η_ker ▸ h1, s, hs, ?_⟩
    rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val] at h2
    exact not_not.1 h2


[UCE_SRC_MARKS_TYPES] HasMult, TypeI, TypeII, Upper, Lower, outer corner and matrix isomorphism
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne.lean#L11-L36
def HasMult {m : ℕ} (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG m R) : Prop := ∀ g, (r g).right = η g

def zeta (c g₁ : G) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : R := zmat c g₁ r 0 0

def MatIrr {m : ℕ} (H : Subgroup G) (a : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : Prop :=
  ∀ W : Submodule R (Fin m → R), (∀ g ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ W, a g *ᵥ v ∈ W) → W = ⊥ ∨ W = ⊤

def mats {m : ℕ} (r : G →* GG m R) (g : G) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R := (r g).mat

def TypeI (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop := MatIrr η.ker (mats r)

def Upper (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop := ∀ g ∈ η.ker, ∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → (r g).mat a b = 0

def Lower (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop := ∀ g ∈ η.ker, ∀ a b, 0 < wt a b → (r g).mat a b = 0

def Block (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop := ∀ g ∈ η.ker, ∀ a b, Odd (wt a b) → (r g).mat a b = 0

def corner (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) R := X.submatrix ![0, 2] ![0, 2]

def TypeII (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop :=
  Block η r ∧ MatIrr η.ker fun g => corner (r g).mat

def Wfun (r : G →* GG 3 R) (g : G) : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) R :=
  if (r g).right = 1 then corner (r g).mat else corner (r g).mat * Matrix.diagonal ![-1, 1]

def MatIso {m : ℕ} (H : Subgroup G) (a a' : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : Prop :=


[UCE_SRC_MARKS] CongOne and all five original marked-element fields
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne.lean#L64-L74
def CongOne (I : Ideal C) (h : GL (Fin m) C) : Prop := EntIn I ((h : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) C) - 1)

end Deform

structure Marks {G : Type*} [Group G] {k : Type*} [CommRing k] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* kˣ) (c g₁ : G) : Prop where
  csq : c * c = 1
  ηc : η c = -1
  Ψc : Ψ c = 1
  ηg : η g₁ = 1
  Ψg : IsUnit (((Ψ g₁ : kˣ) : k) ^ 2 - 1)



[UCE_SRC_WEIGHTS_NORMALIZATION] The actual torus, weights, disconnected torus action, zmat, normalization and base representation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/UnitaryThreeReflectionRight.lean#L235-L305
def wt1 (i : Fin 3) : ℤ := 1 - (i : ℕ)

def wt (i j : Fin 3) : ℤ := wt1 i - wt1 j

def torGL (t : Rˣ) : GL (Fin 3) R where
  val := Matrix.diagonal fun i => ((t ^ wt1 i : Rˣ) : R)
  inv := Matrix.diagonal fun i => (((t ^ wt1 i)⁻¹ : Rˣ) : R)
  val_inv := by rw [Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal]; simp only [Units.mul_inv]; exact Matrix.diagonal_one
  inv_val := by rw [Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal]; simp only [Units.inv_mul]; exact Matrix.diagonal_one

variable (R) in

def tor : Rˣ →* GL (Fin 3) R where
  toFun := torGL
  map_one' := by
    apply Units.ext
    simp [torGL]
  map_mul' s t := by
    apply Units.ext
    simp [torGL, mul_zpow, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal]

theorem tor_val (t : Rˣ) : (tor R t : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) =
    Matrix.diagonal fun i => ((t ^ wt1 i : Rˣ) : R) := rfl

theorem theta_tor (t : Rˣ) : theta (tor R t) = tor R t := by
  apply Units.ext
  rw [theta_val, ← map_inv, tor_val, tor_val]
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Jmat, wt1, Matrix.diagonal, Fin.rev]

variable (R) in

def gw (j : ℤ) : Submodule R (Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) where
  carrier := {X | ∀ a b, wt a b ≠ j → X a b = 0}
  add_mem' := fun hX hY a b h => by simp [hX a b h, hY a b h]
  zero_mem' := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  smul_mem' := fun c X hX a b h => by simp [hX a b h]

variable (R) in

def unitaryThreeTorusLieAlgebra : Submodule R (Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) :=
  Submodule.span R {Matrix.diagonal fun i => ((wt1 i : ℤ) : R)}

variable {G : Type*} [Group G]

def gstar (c g : G) : G := c * g⁻¹ * c

def zmat (c g₁ : G) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R :=
  (r g₁).mat - (r (gstar c g₁)).mat

structure Normalised (c g₁ : G) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop where
  atc : (r c).mat = 1
  z12 : zmat c g₁ r 0 1 = 0
  z21 : zmat c g₁ r 1 0 = 0

def r0 (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ) : G →* GG 3 R where
  toFun g := ⟨tor R (Ψ g), η g⟩
  map_one' := by ext1 <;> simp
  map_mul' g h := by
    ext1
    · rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η g) with hg | hg <;> simp [hg, theta_tor]
    · simp

@[simp] theorem unitaryThreeReflection_right (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ) (g : G) : (r0 η Ψ g).right = η g := rfl

@[simp] theorem unitaryThreeReflection_left (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ) (g : G) : (r0 η Ψ g).left = tor R (Ψ g) := rfl

end Three

end UpperTheory.V.U3


[UCE_SRC_SHAPE] Coordinate invariant-subspace proof of the original rank-three shape alternative
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ExistsCoordOfNotMatIrr.lean#L8-L123
private theorem single_mem_of_diag {κ : Type} [Field κ] {m : ℕ} (d : Fin m → κ)
    (hd : Function.Injective d) (W : Submodule κ (Fin m → κ))
    (hW : ∀ v ∈ W, Matrix.diagonal d *ᵥ v ∈ W) (v : Fin m → κ) (hv : v ∈ W) (i : Fin m)
    (hi : v i ≠ 0) : (Pi.single i 1 : Fin m → κ) ∈ W :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.SingleMemOfDiagonal.single_mem_of_diag d hd W hW v hv i hi

private theorem exists_coord_of_not_matIrr {G κ : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] {m : ℕ}
    (H : Subgroup G) (a : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) κ) (d : Fin m → κ)
    (hd : Function.Injective d) (g₁ g₂ : G) (h₁ : g₁ ∈ H) (h₂ : g₂ ∈ H)
    (hD : a g₁ - a g₂ = Matrix.diagonal d) (h : ¬ MatIrr H a) :
    ∃ S : Fin m → Prop, (∃ j, S j) ∧ (∃ i, ¬ S i) ∧
      ∀ i j, S j → ¬ S i → ∀ g ∈ H, a g i j = 0 := by
  classical
  unfold MatIrr at h
  push Not at h
  obtain ⟨W, hW, hb, ht⟩ := h
  have hWd : ∀ v ∈ W, Matrix.diagonal d *ᵥ v ∈ W := fun v hv => by
    rw [← hD, Matrix.sub_mulVec]
    exact W.sub_mem (hW _ h₁ _ hv) (hW _ h₂ _ hv)
  have hs := single_mem_of_diag d hd W hWd
  refine ⟨fun j => (Pi.single j 1 : Fin m → κ) ∈ W, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨v, hv, hv0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hb
    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := Function.ne_iff.1 hv0
    exact ⟨j, hs v hv j hj⟩
  · by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    apply ht
    rw [eq_top_iff]
    intro v _
    rw [pi_eq_sum_univ' v]
    exact W.sum_mem fun i _ => W.smul_mem _ (hc i)
  · intro i j hj hi g hg
    by_contra hc
    apply hi
    refine hs _ (hW g hg _ hj) i ?_
    simpa using hc

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.shape_cases :
    ∀ {G κ : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 κ) (c g₁ : G), (2 : κ) ≠ 0 → HasMult η r → η c = -1 → η g₁ = 1 →
      Normalised c g₁ r → zeta c g₁ r ≠ 0 → TypeI η r ∨ Upper η r ∨ Lower η r ∨ TypeII η r := by
  intro G κ _ _ η r c g₁ h2 hm hc hg hn hz
  have hc' : (r c).right = -1 := (hm c).trans hc
  have hD := zmat_diag r c g₁ h2.isUnit hc' ((hm g₁).trans hg) hn
  have hst : ∀ g ∈ η.ker, gstar c g ∈ η.ker := fun g hg =>
    (hm _).symm.trans (mat_gstar r c g hn.atc hc' ((hm g).trans hg)).2
  set ζ := zeta c g₁ r with hζ
  have hne : ζ ≠ -ζ := fun h => hz (by
    have : (2 : κ) * ζ = 0 := by linear_combination h
    exact (mul_eq_zero.1 this).resolve_left h2)
  let Z : Fin 3 → Fin 3 → Prop := fun i j => ∀ g ∈ η.ker, (r g).mat i j = 0
  have hsym : ∀ i j : Fin 3, Z i j → Z j.rev i.rev := by
    intro i j h g hg
    have h1 := h _ (hst g hg)
    rw [(mat_gstar r c g hn.atc hc' ((hm g).trans hg)).1] at h1
    revert h1
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
      simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Jmat, Fin.rev]
  have s10 : Z 1 0 → Z 2 1 := hsym 1 0
  have s21 : Z 2 1 → Z 1 0 := hsym 2 1
  have s01 : Z 0 1 → Z 1 2 := hsym 0 1
  have s12 : Z 1 2 → Z 0 1 := hsym 1 2
  have hU : Z 1 0 → Z 2 0 → Upper η r := by
    intro h1 h2 g hg a b hab
    have := h1 g hg
    have := h2 g hg
    have := s10 h1 g hg
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | assumption | (exfalso; revert hab; decide)
  have hL : Z 0 1 → Z 0 2 → Lower η r := by
    intro h1 h2 g hg a b hab
    have := h1 g hg
    have := h2 g hg
    have := s01 h1 g hg
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | assumption | (exfalso; revert hab; decide)
  have hB : Z 0 1 → Z 1 0 → Upper η r ∨ Lower η r ∨ TypeII η r := by
    intro h1 h2
    by_cases hI : MatIrr η.ker fun g => corner (r g).mat
    · refine Or.inr (Or.inr ⟨?_, hI⟩)
      intro g hg a b hab
      have := h1 g hg
      have := h2 g hg
      have := s01 h1 g hg
      have := s10 h2 g hg
      fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | assumption | (exfalso; revert hab; decide)
    · have hinj : Function.Injective ![ζ, -ζ] := by
        intro i j h
        fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp_all [hne.symm]
      obtain ⟨S, ⟨j, hj⟩, ⟨i, hi⟩, hS⟩ := exists_coord_of_not_matIrr η.ker _ ![ζ, -ζ] hinj g₁
        (gstar c g₁) hg (hst g₁ hg) (by
          ext i j
          have := congrFun (congrFun hD (![0, 2] i)) (![0, 2] j)
          refine Eq.trans this ?_
          fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp) hI
      have h3 := hS i j hj hi
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j
      · exact absurd hj hi
      · exact Or.inr (Or.inl (hL h1 h3))
      · exact Or.inl (hU h2 h3)
      · exact absurd hj hi
  by_cases hI : TypeI η r
  · exact Or.inl hI
  right
  have hinj : Function.Injective ![ζ, 0, -ζ] := by
    intro i j h
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp_all [hne.symm]
  obtain ⟨S, ⟨j, hj⟩, ⟨i, hi⟩, hS⟩ := exists_coord_of_not_matIrr η.ker (mats r) _ hinj g₁
    (gstar c g₁) hg (hst g₁ hg) hD hI
  have hS' : ∀ i j, S j → ¬ S i → Z i j := hS
  by_cases a0 : S 0 <;> by_cases a1 : S 1 <;> by_cases a2 : S 2
  · fin_cases i <;> contradiction
  · exact Or.inl (hU (s21 (hS' _ _ a1 a2)) (hS' _ _ a0 a2))
  · exact hB (s12 (hS' _ _ a2 a1)) (hS' _ _ a0 a1)
  · exact Or.inl (hU (hS' _ _ a0 a1) (hS' _ _ a0 a2))
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inl (hL (hS' _ _ a1 a0) (hS' _ _ a2 a0)))
  · exact hB (hS' _ _ a1 a0) (s21 (hS' _ _ a1 a2))
  · exact Or.inr (Or.inl (hL (s12 (hS' _ _ a2 a1)) (hS' _ _ a2 a0)))
  · fin_cases j <;> contradiction


[UCE_SRC_ENTRY_UNIVERSAL] Actual universal property, local/global box conditions, entry algebra, weight-zero algebra and IsPush
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/GRepresents.lean#L75-L128
def GCond : Type 1 := ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R], GLift O η Ψ R → Prop

variable (O η Ψ) in

def GCond.noCondition : GCond O η Ψ := fun _ _ _ _ _ => True

def GRepresents (P : GCond O η Ψ) (r : GLift O η Ψ R) : Prop :=
  ∀ (R' : Type) [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'],
    (∀ f : R →ₐ[O] R', P R' (r.map f)) ∧ ∀ r' : GLift O η Ψ R', P R' r' → ∃! f : R →ₐ[O] R', r.map f = r'

structure Univ (P : GCond O η Ψ) : Type 1 where
  R : Type
  [cr : CommRing R]
  [al : Algebra O R]
  [co : IsCO O R]
  r : GLift O η Ψ R
  rep : GRepresents P r

attribute [instance] Univ.cr Univ.al Univ.co

def OnI (U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O η Ψ)) (I : Ideal U.R) : GCond O η Ψ :=
  fun R _ _ _ r => ∃ f : U.R →ₐ[O] R, U.r.map f = r ∧ I ≤ RingHom.ker f

def Kills (N : Set Γ) : GCond O η Ψ := fun _ _ _ _ r => ∀ g ∈ N, r.hom g = 1

def BoxCond (N : Set Γ) {H : Type} [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] (ι : H →* Γ) (hι : Continuous ι)
    (U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))) (I : Ideal U.R) : GCond O η Ψ :=
  fun R _ _ _ r => Kills N R r ∧ OnI U I R (r.comap ι hι)

def NormCond (c g₁ : Γ) (P : GCond O η Ψ) : GCond O η Ψ :=
  fun R _ _ _ r => Normalised c g₁ r.hom ∧ P R r

end Lift

section Points

variable (O : Type) [CommRing O] {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]

def entAlg (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : Subalgebra O R := Algebra.adjoin O {x | ∃ g a b, x = (r g).mat a b}

def entMon (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (j : ℤ) : Set R :=
  {x | ∃ L : List (Γ × Fin 3 × Fin 3), (L.map fun s => wt s.2.1 s.2.2).sum = j ∧
    x = (L.map fun s => (r s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2).prod}

def weightZeroSubalgebra (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : Subalgebra O R := Algebra.adjoin O (entMon r 0)

variable {O} {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] [Algebra O κ]

def push (f : R →ₐ[O] κ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : Γ →* GG 3 κ := (GG.map (f : R →+* κ)).comp r

def IsPush {A : Subalgebra O R} (ξ : A →ₐ[O] κ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (r' : Γ →* GG 3 κ) : Prop :=
  ∀ g, (r' g).right = (r g).right ∧ ∀ a b, ∃ h : (r g).mat a b ∈ A, (r' g).mat a b = ξ ⟨_, h⟩

def tanCoc (F : R →ₐ[O] DualNumber κ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (g : Γ) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ :=


[UCE_SRC_LOCAL_DATA] Original Dat3, ordered local conditions, saturated ideal It and arbitrary field-valued On predicate
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic.lean#L11-L138
structure Dat3 (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] where
  η : GQ l →* ℤˣ
  Ψ : GQ l →* Oˣ
  τ : GQ l →* Oˣ
  e : ℕ
  Kv : FinExt l
  odd : l ≠ 2
  e_pos : 1 ≤ e
  η_ker : ∀ g, η g = 1 ↔ g ∈ GK Kv
  η_unr : ∀ g ∈ Iq l, η g = 1
  τ_open : IsOpen (τ.ker : Set (GQ l))
  Ψ_cont : AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => (Ψ g : O)
  Ψ_I : ∀ g ∈ Iq l, Ψ g = cycA l l O g ^ e * τ g

def hodge0 (e : ℕ) (i : ℤ) : ℕ := if i = -(e : ℤ) ∨ i = 0 ∨ i = e then 1 else 0

namespace Dat3

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] (D : Dat3 l O)

def HL : Subgroup (GQ l) := D.η.ker ⊓ D.τ.ker

def rz (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] : GQ l →* GG 3 A := (GG.map (algebraMap O A)).comp (r0 D.η D.Ψ)

section Types

variable {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra O B] [Algebra ℚ_[l] B]

def IsType (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop :=
  ∃ ρ : ↥(GK D.Kv) →* GL (Fin 3) B, (∀ g, ρ g = (r g).left) ∧
    PotCrysType D.Kv (colRep ρ) 3 (hodge0 D.e)
      fun γ => algebraMap O B ((D.τ γ : O) + 1 + (((D.τ γ)⁻¹ : Oˣ) : O))

def IsLCr (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop :=
  ∃ ρ : ↥D.HL →* GL (Fin 3) B, (∀ g, ρ g = (r g).left) ∧ IsCr D.HL.subtype (colRep ρ)

variable {E : Type} [CommRing E] [Algebra O E] (π : B →+* E)

structure InDcrAt (r₁ : GQ l →* GG 3 E) (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop where
  mult : HasMult D.η r
  red : (GG.map π).comp r = r₁
  cont : ∀ a b, QCont l fun g => (r g).mat a b
  cr : D.IsLCr r

abbrev InDcr (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop := D.InDcrAt π (D.rz E) r

structure InDtri (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop extends D.InDcrAt π (D.rz E) r where
  tri : ∀ g a b, wt a b < 0 → (r g).mat a b = 0

def InDord (r : GQ l →* GG 3 B) : Prop :=
  ∃ (u : GQ l →* GG 3 B) (n : GL (Fin 3) B), D.InDtri π u ∧ CongOne (RingHom.ker π) n ∧
    (∀ a b, 0 ≤ wt a b → ((n : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) - 1) a b = 0) ∧ r = conjBy n u

end Types

abbrev Univ : Type 1 := U3.Univ (GCond.noCondition O D.η D.Ψ)

section Ord

open Polynomial

def IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic (s : GQ l) : Prop := s ∈ D.HL ∧ s ∈ Iq l ∧ ¬ IsOfFinOrder (cyc l l s)

def IsFr (σ : GQ l) : Prop :=
  (∃ h : σ ∈ D.HL, ((Iq l).comap D.HL.subtype ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers (⟨σ, h⟩ : ↥D.HL)).topologicalClosure = ⊤) ∧
    ∃ (σ₀ : GQ l) (m : ℕ), IsFrob σ₀ ∧ 1 ≤ m ∧ (σ₀ ^ m)⁻¹ * σ ∈ Iq l

def s0 : GQ l := Classical.epsilon (IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic D)

def fr : GQ l := Classical.epsilon (IsFr D)

def cc (a : Fin 3) : O :=
  (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a (s0 D) - UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ (a + 1) (s0 D)) *
    (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a (s0 D) - UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ (a + 2) (s0 D))

section Eq

variable {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 C)

def sh (a : Fin 3) (s : GQ l) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C :=
  (r s).mat - Matrix.scalar _ (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s))

def ordIdeal : Ideal C :=
  Ideal.span ({x | ∃ s ∈ D.HL ⊓ Iq l, ∃ i : ℕ, x = ((r s).mat.charpoly -
      ∏ a, (X - Polynomial.C (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s)))).coeff i} ∪
    {x | ∃ s : Fin 3 → GQ l, (∀ a, s a ∈ D.HL ⊓ Iq l) ∧ ∃ i j,
      x = (sh D r 0 (s 0) * sh D r 1 (s 1) * sh D r 2 (s 2)) i j})

def tr (a : Fin 3) : C := (sh D r (a + 1) (s0 D) * sh D r (a + 2) (s0 D) * (r (fr D)).mat).trace

def dd (a : Fin 3) : C :=
  algebraMap O C (cc D (a + 1)) * tr D r a - algebraMap O C ((cycA l l O (fr D) : Oˣ) * cc D a) * tr D r (a + 1)

def del : C := (l : C) * dd D r 0 * dd D r 1

end Eq

end Ord

variable (U : D.Univ)

def IY : Ideal U.R where
  carrier := {a | ∃ n : ℕ, del D U.r.hom ^ n * a ∈ ordIdeal D U.r.hom}
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, by simp⟩
  add_mem' := by
    rintro a b ⟨m, hm⟩ ⟨n, hn⟩
    refine ⟨m + n, ?_⟩
    have e : del D U.r.hom ^ (m + n) * (a + b) = del D U.r.hom ^ n * (del D U.r.hom ^ m * a) +
        del D U.r.hom ^ m * (del D U.r.hom ^ n * b) := by ring
    rw [e]
    exact Ideal.add_mem _ (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hm) (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hn)
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c a ⟨n, hn⟩
    refine ⟨n, ?_⟩
    rw [smul_eq_mul, mul_left_comm]
    exact Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hn

def Icr : Ideal U.R := (IY D U).radical

def primesAt0 : Set (Ideal U.R) :=
  {P | P ∈ (D.Icr U).minimalPrimes ∧ ∀ x : U.R →ₐ[O] O, push x U.r.hom = r0 D.η D.Ψ → P ≤ RingHom.ker x}

def It : Ideal U.R := sInf (D.primesAt0 U)

abbrev Rt : Type := U.R ⧸ D.It U

def On {C : Type} [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 C) : Prop :=
  ∃ f : U.R →ₐ[O] C, push f U.r.hom = r ∧ D.It U ≤ RingHom.ker f


[UCE_SRC_LOCAL_CHART] Literal all-target ConjStable and original LocGood chart fields
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/ConjStable.lean#L15-L37
def IsBase {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R)
    (x : R →ₐ[O] O) : Prop := push x r = r0 η Ψ

def ConjStable : Prop :=
  ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (r r' : GLift O η Ψ R) (h : GL (Fin 3) R),
    CongOne (maximalIdeal R) h → r'.hom = conjBy h r.hom → OnI U I R r → OnI U I R r'

def locDims (j : ℤ) : ℕ :=
  if j = -2 then 1 else if j = -1 then 2 else if j = 0 then 3 else if j = 1 then 4 else if j = 2 then 2 else 0

structure LocChart (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O) {τ : Type} (w : τ → ℤ) (φ : U.R →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries τ (FractionRing O)) :
    Prop where
  ker : RingHom.ker φ = I
  graded : ∀ g a b, φ ((U.r.hom g).mat a b) ∈ wtPart (FractionRing O) w (wt a b)
  const : ∀ f, constantCoeff (φ f) = algebraMap O (FractionRing O) (x f)
  onto : ∀ i, ∃ f : U.R, coeff (Finsupp.single i 1) (φ f) ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ i', i' ≠ i → coeff (Finsupp.single i' 1) (φ f) = 0
  inj : ∀ f, x f = 0 → (∀ i, coeff (Finsupp.single i 1) (φ f) = 0) →
    ∃ c : O, c ≠ 0 ∧ c • f ∈ RingHom.ker x ^ 2 ⊔ I

structure LocGood : Prop where
  conj : ConjStable U I
  chart : ∃ (x : U.R →ₐ[O] O) (τ : Type) (_ : Fintype τ) (w : τ → ℤ) (φ : U.R →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries τ (FractionRing O)),
    IsBase η Ψ U.r.hom x ∧ LocChart U I x w φ ∧ ∀ j, Nat.card {i // w i = j} = locDims j


[UCE_SRC_FULL_INERTIA] All-inertia characteristic-polynomial and ordered-product identities in the actual component
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/ScalarMap.lean#L157-L193
theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.lemma2a :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 C), D.On U r → (∀ s ∈ Iq l, (r s).mat.charpoly = ∏ a, (X - Polynomial.C (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s)))) ∧ ∀ s : Fin 3 → GQ l, (∀ a, s a ∈ Iq l) → ((r (s 0)).mat - Matrix.scalar _ (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ 0 (s 0)))) * ((r (s 1)).mat - Matrix.scalar _ (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ 1 (s 1)))) * ((r (s 2)).mat - Matrix.scalar _ (algebraMap O C (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ 2 (s 2)))) = 0 := by
  intro hL l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U C _ _ r hr
  obtain ⟨f, rfl, hf⟩ := hr
  show Cond D.Ψ (Iq l) (push f U.r.hom)
  obtain ⟨S, hS⟩ := cond_eqs D.Ψ (Iq l) U.r.hom
  obtain ⟨_, _, _⟩ := frac_alg l O
  obtain ⟨x, hx, -⟩ := univEx D U
  obtain ⟨-, φ, hφ⟩ := rtCompl hL D U (FractionRing O) x hx
  refine (hS C f).2 fun a ha => hf ?_
  rw [← hφ.ker, RingHom.mem_ker, ← Ideal.mem_bot,
    ← Ideal.iInf_pow_eq_bot_of_isLocalRing (maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin 12) (FractionRing O)))
      (maximalIdeal.isMaximal _).ne_top, Ideal.mem_iInf]
  intro n
  refine Ideal.pow_le_pow_right n.le_succ ?_
  obtain ⟨π, hπ, hn, hfin⟩ := trunc_data (FractionRing O) n
  let B := MvPowerSeries (Fin 12) (FractionRing O) ⧸ maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin 12) (FractionRing O)) ^ (n + 1)
  haveI : Module.Finite ℚ_[l] B := Module.Finite.trans (FractionRing O) B
  let g : U.R →ₐ[O] B := (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O _).comp φ
  have hon : D.On U (push g U.r.hom) := ⟨g, rfl, fun y hy => by
    rw [← hφ.ker, RingHom.mem_ker] at hy
    show Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O _ (φ y) = 0
    rw [hy, map_zero]⟩
  obtain ⟨hc, -, hcr⟩ := rtPoints hL D U B _ hon
  have hred : (GG.map (π : B →+* FractionRing O)).comp (push g U.r.hom) = D.rz (FractionRing O) := by
    have e : (π : B →+* FractionRing O).comp (g : U.R →+* B) =
        (algebraMap O (FractionRing O)).comp (x : U.R →+* O) := RingHom.ext fun y => (hπ (φ y)).trans (hφ.const y)
    unfold Dat3.rz
    rw [← show push x U.r.hom = r0 D.η D.Ψ from hx]
    refine MonoidHom.ext fun s => ?_
    simp only [push, MonoidHom.comp_apply, GG.map_map, e]
  obtain ⟨u, m, hu, -, -, hum⟩ := (ordEq hL D (FractionRing O) B π hn _).1 ⟨U.r.hasMult, hred, hc, hcr⟩
  have hcond : Cond D.Ψ (Iq l) (push g U.r.hom) := by
    rw [hum]
    exact cond_conj _ _ u (fun s hs => (hu.mult s).trans (D.η_unr s hs)) m
      (cond_tri _ _ u hu.tri (triDiag D (FractionRing O) B π hn u hu))
  exact Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.1 ((hS B g).1 hcond a ha)


[UCE_SRC_ORDERED_FLAG] Original full ordered inertia flag and decomposition-group preservation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/CommutingUpperTriangularDistinct.lean#L15-L167
  have a10 := hA 1 0 (by decide)
  have a20 := hA 2 0 (by decide)
  have a21 := hA 2 1 (by decide)
  have b10 := hB 1 0 (by decide)
  have b20 := hB 2 0 (by decide)
  have b21 := hB 2 1 (by decide)

  have d02 : d 0 - d 2 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))

  intro a b hab
  fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | exact absurd hab (by decide) | assumption

private theorem diagonalize_three_by_three_split {K : Type} [Field K] (d : Fin 3 → K) (hd : Function.Injective d)
    (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) (hM : M.charpoly = ∏ a, (X - C (d a))) :
    ∃ h : GL (Fin 3) K, ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * M * h = Matrix.diagonal d :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.DiagonalizeThreeByThree.diagonalize_three_by_three_split d hd M hM

private theorem three_eigenvalue_product_zero {K : Type} [Field K] (d p : Fin 3 → K) (hd : Function.Injective d)
    (N : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)
    (h0 : (N - Matrix.scalar _ (p 0)) * (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 1)) *
      (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 2)) = 0)
    (h1 : (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 0)) * (N - Matrix.scalar _ (p 1)) *
      (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 2)) = 0)
    (h2 : (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 0)) * (Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar _ (d 1)) *
      (N - Matrix.scalar _ (p 2)) = 0) :
    (∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → N a b = 0) ∧ ∀ a, N a a = p a := by
  have d01 : d 0 - d 1 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d02 : d 0 - d 2 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d12 : d 1 - d 2 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d10 : d 1 - d 0 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d20 : d 2 - d 0 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have d21 : d 2 - d 1 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 (hd.ne (by decide))
  have hs : ∀ c : K, Matrix.diagonal d - Matrix.scalar (Fin 3) c = Matrix.diagonal fun i => d i - c := fun c => by
    rw [Matrix.scalar_apply, Matrix.diagonal_sub]
  simp only [hs, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal] at h0 h1 h2
  have e00 := congrFun (congrFun h0 0) 0
  have e10 := congrFun (congrFun h0 1) 0
  have e20 := congrFun (congrFun h0 2) 0
  have e11 := congrFun (congrFun h1 1) 1
  have e21 := congrFun (congrFun h2 2) 1
  have e22 := congrFun (congrFun h2 2) 2
  simp [Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.diagonal_mul, d01, d02, d12, d10, d20, d21, sub_eq_zero] at e00 e10 e20 e11 e21 e22
  constructor
  · intro a b hab
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | exact absurd hab (by decide) | assumption
  · intro a
    fin_cases a <;> assumption

private theorem conjugate_representation_mat {R D : Type} [CommRing R] [Group D] (r : D →* GG 3 R) (h : GL (Fin 3) R) (g : D)
    (hg : (r g).right = 1) : (conjBy h⁻¹ r g).mat =
      ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) * (r g).mat * h := by
  simp [conjBy, GG.mat, hg]

private theorem padic_int_ringHom_injective {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {K : Type} [Field K] [CharZero K] (φ : ℤ_[l] →+* K) :
    Function.Injective φ := by
  rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
  intro x hx
  by_contra h0
  rw [PadicInt.unitCoeff_spec h0, map_mul, map_pow, map_natCast] at hx
  exact mul_ne_zero ((Units.isUnit _).map φ).ne_zero
    (pow_ne_zero _ (Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero)) hx

private theorem exists_inertia_distinct_characters {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O]
    (D : Dat3 l O) (K : Type) [Field K] [CharZero K] [Algebra O K] :
    ∃ s ∈ Iq l, Function.Injective fun a => algebraMap O K (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s) := by
  obtain ⟨s, ⟨hs1, hs2⟩, hs⟩ := ((cycInertia l).2 D.τ.ker D.τ_open).2
  refine ⟨s, hs2, ?_⟩
  let φ : ℤ_[l] →+* K := (algebraMap O K).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O)
  let t : Kˣ := Units.map (algebraMap O K : O →* K) (D.Ψ s)
  have ht : t = Units.map (φ : ℤ_[l] →* K) (cyc l l s) ^ D.e := by
    have h1 : D.τ s = 1 := hs1
    simp only [t]
    rw [D.Ψ_I s hs2, h1, mul_one, map_pow]
    rfl
  have hinf : ¬ IsOfFinOrder t := by
    rw [ht]
    intro h
    have h2 := h.of_pow (by have := D.e_pos; omega)
    exact hs ((Function.Injective.isOfFinOrder_iff (f := Units.map (φ : ℤ_[l] →* K))
      (Units.map_injective (padic_int_ringHom_injective φ))).1 h2)
  have e : ∀ a, algebraMap O K (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a s) = ((t ^ wt1 a : Kˣ) : K) := fun a =>
    congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map (algebraMap O K : O →* K)) (D.Ψ s) (wt1 a))
  intro a b hab
  simp only [e] at hab
  have h3 := injective_zpow_iff_not_isOfFinOrder.2 hinf (Units.ext hab)
  simp only [wt1] at h3
  exact Fin.ext (by omega)

end UpperTheory.V.Loc

theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.lemma2b :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (K : Type) [Field K] [CharZero K] [Algebra O K] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 K), D.On U r → (∃ h, UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn D.η D.Ψ (Iq l) r h) ∧ ∀ h h', UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn D.η D.Ψ (Iq l) r h → UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn D.η D.Ψ (Iq l) r h' → ∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → ((h⁻¹ * h' : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) a b = 0 := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U K _ _ _ r hr
  obtain ⟨hch, hpr⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Loc.lemma2a hP D U K r hr
  have hm : ∀ g, D.η g = 1 → (r g).right = 1 := by
    obtain ⟨f, rfl, -⟩ := hr
    exact fun g hg => (U.r.hasMult g).trans hg
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hd⟩ := exists_inertia_distinct_characters D K
  have hηs := D.η_unr s hs
  constructor
  · obtain ⟨h, hh⟩ := diagonalize_three_by_three_split _ hd _ (hch s hs)
    have hc : ∀ (A : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) (c : K),
        ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * A * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) - Matrix.scalar _ c =
          ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * (A - Matrix.scalar _ c) * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) := by
      intro A c
      rw [Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc _ (Matrix.scalar _ c),
        Matrix.scalar_commute c (fun _ => Commute.all _ _), ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.inv_mul, Matrix.one_mul]
    have hc3 : ∀ A B C : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K, A * B * C = 0 →
        (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * A * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)) *
          (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * B * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)) *
          (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * C * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)) = 0 := by
      intro A B C h0
      have : ∀ Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K,
          (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * Y) = Y :=
        fun Y => by rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.one_mul]
      simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc, this]
      rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc B, ← Matrix.mul_assoc A, ← Matrix.mul_assoc A, h0, Matrix.zero_mul, Matrix.mul_zero]
    have hI : ∀ x ∈ Iq l, (∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → (conjBy h⁻¹ r x).mat a b = 0) ∧
        ∀ a, (conjBy h⁻¹ r x).mat a a = algebraMap O K (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a x) := by
      intro x hx
      rw [conjugate_representation_mat r h x (hm x (D.η_unr x hx))]
      refine three_eigenvalue_product_zero _ (fun a => algebraMap O K (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi D.Ψ a x)) hd _ ?_ ?_ ?_
      · rw [← hh, hc, hc, hc]
        exact hc3 _ _ _ (hpr ![x, s, s] fun a => by fin_cases a <;> assumption)
      · rw [← hh, hc, hc, hc]
        exact hc3 _ _ _ (hpr ![s, x, s] fun a => by fin_cases a <;> assumption)
      · rw [← hh, hc, hc, hc]
        exact hc3 _ _ _ (hpr ![s, s, x] fun a => by fin_cases a <;> assumption)
    refine ⟨h, fun g hg => ?_, fun x hx _ => (hI x hx).2⟩
    have hx : g * s * g⁻¹ ∈ Iq l := (iqBasic l).1.conj_mem s hs g
    have hr1 : ∀ x, D.η x = 1 → (conjBy h⁻¹ r x).right = 1 := fun x hx => by simp [conjBy, hm x hx]
    have hmul : ∀ x y, D.η x = 1 →
        (conjBy h⁻¹ r (x * y)).mat = (conjBy h⁻¹ r x).mat * (conjBy h⁻¹ r y).mat := fun x y hx => by
      rw [map_mul]
      simp only [GG.mat, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, hr1 x hx, sgnAct_one, Units.val_mul]
    refine commuting_upper_triangular_distinct _ hd (conjBy h⁻¹ r (g * s * g⁻¹)).mat _ (conjBy h⁻¹ r s).mat ?_ (hI _ hx).1 (hI s hs).1
      (fun a => ?_) (hI s hs).2
    · rw [← hmul _ _ (D.η_unr _ hx), ← hmul _ _ hg, inv_mul_cancel_right]
    · rw [(hI _ hx).2]
      simp only [UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi, map_mul, map_inv, mul_inv_cancel_comm]
  · intro h h' H H'
    refine commuting_upper_triangular_distinct _ hd (conjBy h⁻¹ r s).mat _ (conjBy h'⁻¹ r s).mat ?_ (H.tri s hηs) (H'.tri s hηs)
      (H.ine s hs hηs) (H'.ine s hs hηs)
    rw [conjugate_representation_mat r h s (hm s hηs), conjugate_representation_mat r h' s (hm s hηs), Units.val_mul]
    simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv_cancel_left]


[UCE_SRC_FLAG_SPECIALIZATION] Valuation-ring integral flag and finite-field-extension specialization, including literal lemma2c
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/ValuationRingClearLastRowEntry.lean#L1-L246
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PublishedTheorems.KisinCrTrivial

namespace UpperTheory.V.Loc

private def TriangularForm {ι A C : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing C] (M : ι → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A) (d : ι → Fin 3 → A)
    (S T : Set ι) (φ : A →+* C) (h : GL (Fin 3) C) : Prop :=
  (∀ g ∈ S, ∀ a b, b < a →
    (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) * (M g).map φ * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C)) a b = 0) ∧
  ∀ g ∈ T, ∀ a,
    (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) * (M g).map φ * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C)) a a = φ (d g a)

section

variable {ι A C C' : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing C] [CommRing C'] {M : ι → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A}
  {d : ι → Fin 3 → A} {S T : Set ι}

private theorem conjugate_entry_map (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A) (φ : A →+* C) (ψ : C →+* C') (h : GL (Fin 3) C) (a b : Fin 3) :
    ((((Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map ψ h)⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C') : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C') * X.map (ψ.comp φ) *
      (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map ψ h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C')) a b =
    ψ ((((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) * X.map φ * (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C)) a b) := by
  rw [← map_inv, ← Matrix.map_apply (f := ψ), Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.map_mul]
  rfl

private theorem triangular_form_map {φ : A →+* C} {h : GL (Fin 3) C} (ψ : C →+* C') (H : TriangularForm M d S T φ h) :
    TriangularForm M d S T (ψ.comp φ) (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map ψ h) :=
  ⟨fun g hg a b hab => by rw [conjugate_entry_map, H.1 g hg a b hab, map_zero],
    fun g hg a => by rw [conjugate_entry_map, H.2 g hg a]; rfl⟩

private theorem triangular_form_of_injective_map {φ : A →+* C} {h : GL (Fin 3) C} (ψ : C →+* C') (hψ : Function.Injective ψ)
    (H : TriangularForm M d S T (ψ.comp φ) (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map ψ h)) : TriangularForm M d S T φ h :=
  ⟨fun g hg a b hab => hψ (by rw [← conjugate_entry_map, H.1 g hg a b hab, map_zero]),
    fun g hg a => hψ (by rw [← conjugate_entry_map, H.2 g hg a]; rfl)⟩

private theorem triangular_mul_diag {X Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C} (hX : X.BlockTriangular id) (hY : Y.BlockTriangular id)
    (a : Fin 3) : (X * Y) a a = X a a * Y a a := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single a]
  · intro b _ hb
    rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hb with h | h
    · rw [hX h, zero_mul]
    · rw [hY h, mul_zero]
  · simp

private theorem triangular_form_borel_change {φ : A →+* C} {h : GL (Fin 3) C} (hT : T ⊆ S) (H : TriangularForm M d S T φ h) (k : GL (Fin 3) C)
    (hk : ((k * h : GL (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C).BlockTriangular id) : TriangularForm M d S T φ k⁻¹ := by
  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ : ∃ b, b = k * h := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  rw [← hb] at hk
  obtain rfl : k = b * h⁻¹ := by rw [hb, mul_inv_cancel_right]
  have hi : ((b⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C).BlockTriangular id := by
    rw [Matrix.coe_units_inv]
    haveI := b.invertible
    exact Matrix.blockTriangular_inv_of_blockTriangular hk
  have e : ∀ X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C, (((b * h⁻¹)⁻¹⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) * X *
      ((b * h⁻¹)⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) = b * (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) * X * h) *
      (b⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) := fun X => by
    simp only [inv_inv, _root_.mul_inv_rev, Units.val_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  have hU : ∀ g ∈ S, (((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) * (M g).map φ * h).BlockTriangular id :=
    fun g hg a b hab => H.1 g hg a b hab
  refine ⟨fun g hg a c hac => ?_, fun g hg a => ?_⟩
  · rw [e]
    exact ((hk.mul (hU g hg)).mul hi) hac
  · rw [e, triangular_mul_diag (hk.mul (hU g (hT hg))) hi, triangular_mul_diag hk (hU g (hT hg)), H.2 g hg a, mul_right_comm,
      ← triangular_mul_diag hk hi, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.one_apply_eq, one_mul]

end

private theorem exists_sl2_valuation_kill {F : Type} [Field F] (V : ValuationSubring F) (a b : F) :
    ∃ x y z w : V, x * w - y * z = 1 ∧ (z : F) * a + w * b = 0 := by
  by_cases ha : a = 0
  · exact ⟨0, -1, 1, 0, by simp, by simp [ha]⟩
  by_cases hb : b = 0
  · exact ⟨1, 0, 0, 1, by simp, by simp [hb]⟩
  rcases V.mem_or_inv_mem (b / a) with h | h
  · exact ⟨1, 0, -⟨_, h⟩, 1, by simp, by simp [ha]⟩
  · exact ⟨0, -1, 1, -⟨_, h⟩, by simp, by simp [hb]⟩

private theorem valuation_ring_clear_last_row_entry {F : Type} [Field F] (V : ValuationSubring F) (Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) F) (c : Fin 3) :
    ∃ k : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) V, k.det = 1 ∧ (k.map V.subtype * Y) 2 c = 0 ∧
      (Y 1 0 = 0 → Y 2 0 = 0 → (k.map V.subtype * Y) 1 0 = 0 ∧ (k.map V.subtype * Y) 2 0 = 0) := by
  obtain ⟨x, y, z, w, h1, h2⟩ := exists_sl2_valuation_kill V (Y 1 c) (Y 2 c)
  refine ⟨!![1, 0, 0; 0, x, y; 0, z, w], ?_, ?_, fun e1 e2 => ⟨?_, ?_⟩⟩
  · simpa [Matrix.det_fin_three] using h1
  · simpa [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three] using h2
  · simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, e1, e2]
  · simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, e1, e2]

private theorem valuation_ring_clear_first_column {F : Type} [Field F] (V : ValuationSubring F) (Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) F) :
    ∃ k : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) V, k.det = 1 ∧ (k.map V.subtype * Y) 1 0 = 0 ∧
      (k.map V.subtype * Y) 2 0 = Y 2 0 := by
  obtain ⟨x, y, z, w, h1, h2⟩ := exists_sl2_valuation_kill V (Y 0 0) (Y 1 0)
  refine ⟨!![x, y, 0; z, w, 0; 0, 0, 1], ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · simpa [Matrix.det_fin_three] using h1
  · simpa [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three] using h2
  · simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three]

private theorem upper_triangular_of_entries_zero {F : Type} [Field F] {Z : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) F} (h1 : Z 1 0 = 0) (h2 : Z 2 0 = 0)
    (h3 : Z 2 1 = 0) : Z.BlockTriangular id := by
  intro i j hij
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> first | exact absurd hij (by decide) | simpa

private theorem iwasawa_triangularization {F : Type} [Field F] (V : ValuationSubring F) (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) F) :
    ∃ k : GL (Fin 3) V, ((k : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) V).map V.subtype * X).BlockTriangular id := by
  obtain ⟨k1, d1, a1, -⟩ := valuation_ring_clear_last_row_entry V X 0
  obtain ⟨k2, d2, a2, b2⟩ := valuation_ring_clear_first_column V (k1.map V.subtype * X)
  obtain ⟨k3, d3, a3, b3⟩ := valuation_ring_clear_last_row_entry V (k2.map V.subtype * (k1.map V.subtype * X)) 1
  obtain ⟨b3, c3⟩ := b3 a2 (b2.trans a1)
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := (Matrix.isUnit_iff_isUnit_det (k3 * (k2 * k1))).2
    (by rw [Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_mul, d1, d2, d3, one_mul, one_mul]; exact isUnit_one)
  refine ⟨k, ?_⟩
  rw [hk, Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  exact upper_triangular_of_entries_zero b3 c3 a3

private theorem exists_common_ring_same_kernel {A K L : Type} [CommRing A] [Field K] [Field L] (f : A →+* K) (g : A →+* L)
    (h : RingHom.ker f = RingHom.ker g) :
    ∃ (T : Type) (_ : CommRing T) (_ : Nontrivial T) (i : K →+* T) (j : L →+* T), i.comp f = j.comp g := by
  letI : Algebra (A ⧸ RingHom.ker f) K := (RingHom.kerLift f).toAlgebra
  letI : Algebra (A ⧸ RingHom.ker f) L := (Ideal.Quotient.lift _ g fun a ha => by rwa [h] at ha).toAlgebra
  have h2 : Function.Injective (algebraMap (A ⧸ RingHom.ker f) L) := by
    rw [injective_iff_map_eq_zero]
    intro a ha
    obtain ⟨a, rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective a
    rw [Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem, h]
    exact ha
  refine ⟨TensorProduct (A ⧸ RingHom.ker f) K L, inferInstance,
    Algebra.TensorProduct.nontrivial_of_algebraMap_injective_of_isDomain _ _ _ (RingHom.kerLift_injective f) h2,
    (Algebra.TensorProduct.includeLeft (S := A ⧸ RingHom.ker f)).toRingHom,
    Algebra.TensorProduct.includeRight.toRingHom, RingHom.ext fun a => ?_⟩
  exact ((Algebra.TensorProduct.includeLeft (S := A ⧸ RingHom.ker f) (A := K) (B := L)).commutes
    (Ideal.Quotient.mk _ a)).trans
    ((Algebra.TensorProduct.includeRight (A := K) (B := L)).commutes (Ideal.Quotient.mk _ a)).symm

private theorem triangular_form_finite_extension {ι A K B : Type} [CommRing A] [Field K] [CommRing B] [Nontrivial B]
    {M : ι → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A} {d : ι → Fin 3 → A} {S T : Set ι} (f : A →+* K) (i : K →+* B)
    (h : GL (Fin 3) B) (H : TriangularForm M d S T (i.comp f) h) :
    ∃ (K' : Type) (_ : Field K') (_ : Algebra K K') (_ : FiniteDimensional K K') (h' : GL (Fin 3) K'),
      TriangularForm M d S T ((algebraMap K K').comp f) h' := by
  classical
  letI := i.toAlgebra
  let s : Set B := Set.range (fun p : Fin 3 × Fin 3 => (h : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) p.1 p.2) ∪
    Set.range (fun p : Fin 3 × Fin 3 => ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) B) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B) p.1 p.2)
  have hs : s.Finite := (Set.finite_range _).union (Set.finite_range _)
  let B₀ := Algebra.adjoin K s
  haveI : Algebra.FiniteType K B₀ := (Subalgebra.fg_iff_finiteType _).1 (Subalgebra.fg_def.2 ⟨s, hs, rfl⟩)
  let X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B₀ := fun a b => ⟨_, Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl ⟨(a, b), rfl⟩)⟩
  let Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B₀ := fun a b => ⟨_, Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inr ⟨(a, b), rfl⟩)⟩
  have hXY : X * Y = 1 := by
    apply Matrix.map_injective (f := B₀.val.toRingHom) Subtype.val_injective
    show (X * Y).map B₀.val.toRingHom = (1 : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) B₀).map B₀.val.toRingHom
    rw [Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.map_one _ (map_zero _) (map_one _)]
    exact h.mul_inv
  let h₀ : GL (Fin 3) B₀ := ⟨X, Y, hXY, mul_eq_one_comm.1 hXY⟩
  have H0 : TriangularForm M d S T ((algebraMap K B₀).comp f) h₀ :=
    triangular_form_of_injective_map B₀.val.toRingHom Subtype.val_injective H
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := Ideal.exists_maximal B₀
  letI := Ideal.Quotient.field m
  exact ⟨B₀ ⧸ m, inferInstance, inferInstance, finite_of_finite_type_of_isJacobsonRing K (B₀ ⧸ m), _,
    triangular_form_map (Ideal.Quotient.mk m) H0⟩

private theorem triangular_form_specialization {ι A F K : Type} [CommRing A] [Field F] [Field K]
    {M : ι → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A} {d : ι → Fin 3 → A} {S T : Set ι} (hT : T ⊆ S) (φ : A →+* F) (f : A →+* K)
    (hf : RingHom.ker φ ≤ RingHom.ker f) (h : GL (Fin 3) F) (H : TriangularForm M d S T φ h) :
    ∃ (K' : Type) (_ : Field K') (_ : Algebra K K') (_ : FiniteDimensional K K') (h' : GL (Fin 3) K'),
      TriangularForm M d S T ((algebraMap K K').comp f) h' := by
  haveI := RingHom.ker_isPrime f
  have hu : ∀ y : (RingHom.ker f).primeCompl, IsUnit (φ y) := fun y => Ne.isUnit fun e => y.2 (hf e)
  obtain ⟨V, hV, hl⟩ := IsLocalRing.exists_factor_valuationRing
    (IsLocalization.lift (S := Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker f)) hu)
  let g₀ : Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker f) →+* V := (IsLocalization.lift hu).codRestrict V.toSubring hV
  haveI : IsLocalHom g₀ := hl
  let g : A →+* V := g₀.comp (algebraMap _ _)
  have hg : V.subtype.comp g = φ := RingHom.ext fun a => IsLocalization.lift_eq hu a
  have hk : RingHom.ker f = RingHom.ker ((residue V).comp g) := by
    ext a
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker (f := (residue V).comp g), RingHom.comp_apply, residue_eq_zero_iff,
      ← IsLocalization.AtPrime.to_map_mem_maximal_iff (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker f)) (RingHom.ker f) a,
      mem_maximalIdeal, mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff, mem_nonunits_iff]
    exact (isUnit_map_iff g₀ _).not.symm
  obtain ⟨k, hk'⟩ := iwasawa_triangularization V h
  have H1 := triangular_form_borel_change hT H (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map V.subtype k) hk'
  rw [← map_inv, ← hg] at H1
  have H2 := triangular_form_map (residue V) (triangular_form_of_injective_map V.subtype Subtype.val_injective H1)
  obtain ⟨B, _, _, i, j, e⟩ := exists_common_ring_same_kernel f _ hk
  have H3 := triangular_form_map j H2
  rw [← e] at H3
  exact triangular_form_finite_extension f i _ H3

private theorem triangular_in_iff_triangular_form {O A C D : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [CommRing C] [Algebra O A] [Algebra O C] [Group D]
    (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (I : Subgroup D) (u : D →* GG 3 A) (hu : HasMult η u) (φ : A →ₐ[O] C)
    (h : GL (Fin 3) C) : UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn η Ψ I (push φ u) h ↔
      TriangularForm (fun g => (u g).mat) (fun g a => algebraMap O A (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ a g)) {g | η g = 1}
        {g | g ∈ I ∧ η g = 1} φ h := by
  have e : ∀ g, η g = 1 → (conjBy h⁻¹ (push φ u) g).mat =
      ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) * (u g).mat.map (φ : A →+* C) * h := by
    intro g hg
    have h1 : (push φ u g).right = 1 := (hu g).trans hg
    have h2 : (push φ u g).left.val = (u g).mat.map (φ : A →+* C) := rfl
    rw [← h2]
    simp [conjBy, GG.mat, h1]
  have hw : ∀ a b : Fin 3, wt a b < 0 ↔ b < a := by decide
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
    refine ⟨fun g hg a b hab => ?_, fun g hg a => ?_⟩
    · rw [← e g hg]
      exact h1 g hg a b ((hw a b).2 hab)
    · rw [← e g hg.2, h2 g hg.1 hg.2 a]
      exact (φ.commutes _).symm
  · rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
    refine ⟨fun g hg a b hab => ?_, fun g hg hg' a => ?_⟩
    · rw [e g hg]
      exact h1 g hg a b ((hw a b).1 hab)
    · rw [e g hg', h2 g ⟨hg, hg'⟩ a]
      exact φ.commutes _

end UpperTheory.V.Loc

theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.lemma2c :
    LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [CharZero O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (D : Dat3 l O) (U : D.Univ) (K : Type) [Field K] [Algebra O K] (r : GQ l →* GG 3 K), D.On U r → ∃ (K' : Type) (_ : Field K') (_ : Algebra K K') (_ : Algebra O K') (_ : IsScalarTower O K K') (_ : FiniteDimensional K K') (h : GL (Fin 3) K'), UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn D.η D.Ψ (Iq l) ((GG.map (algebraMap K K')).comp r) h := by
  intro hP l _ O _ _ _ _ _ _ _ D U K _ _ r ⟨f, hr, hf⟩
  obtain ⟨-, hp, hl, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Loc.rtBasic hP D U
  haveI : IsDomain (D.Rt U) := Ideal.Quotient.isDomain _
  let φ : U.R →ₐ[O] FractionRing (D.Rt U) :=
    (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom O (D.Rt U) (FractionRing (D.Rt U))).comp (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O _)
  have hφ : RingHom.ker φ = D.It U := by
    ext a
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, ← Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem]
    exact map_eq_zero_iff _ (IsFractionRing.injective (D.Rt U) (FractionRing (D.Rt U)))
  have hl0 : ((l : ℕ) : FractionRing (D.Rt U)) ≠ 0 := fun e => hl (by rw [← hφ, RingHom.mem_ker, map_natCast, e])
  haveI : CharZero (FractionRing (D.Rt U)) := by
    refine charZero_of_inj_zero fun n hn => ?_
    by_contra h0
    have h1 : (n : ℤ_[l]) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 h0
    have h2 := congrArg ((algebraMap O (FractionRing (D.Rt U))).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O))
      (PadicInt.unitCoeff_spec h1)
    rw [map_natCast, hn, map_mul, map_pow, map_natCast] at h2
    exact mul_ne_zero ((Units.isUnit _).map _).ne_zero (pow_ne_zero _ hl0) h2.symm
  obtain ⟨⟨h, H⟩, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Loc.lemma2b hP D U _ (push φ U.r.hom) ⟨φ, rfl, hφ.ge⟩
  rw [UpperTheory.V.Loc.triangular_in_iff_triangular_form _ _ _ _ U.r.hasMult] at H
  obtain ⟨K', _, _, _, h', H'⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Loc.triangular_form_specialization (fun g hg => hg.2) _ (f : U.R →+* K) (hφ.le.trans hf) h H
  letI : Algebra O K' := ((algebraMap K K').comp (algebraMap O K)).toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower O K K' := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl
  refine ⟨K', inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, inferInstance, h', ?_⟩
  subst hr
  exact (UpperTheory.V.Loc.triangular_in_iff_triangular_form _ _ _ _ U.r.hasMult ((IsScalarTower.toAlgHom O K K').comp f) h').2 H'


[UCE_SRC_INTEGRAL_FLAG] Integral ordered near-identity triangular basis under the original unit separator
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/TriangularOfReducibleRankThree.lean#L10-L144
private theorem go_up {K : Type} [Field K] (T m : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) (d : Fin 3 → K)
    (hT : T.BlockTriangular id) (hd : ∀ a b, a ≠ b → T b b ≠ d a) (e : T * m = m * Matrix.diagonal d) :
    m.BlockTriangular id := by
  have t10 : T 1 0 = 0 := hT (by decide)
  have t20 : T 2 0 = 0 := hT (by decide)
  have t21 : T 2 1 = 0 := hT (by decide)
  have e' : ∀ b a, T b 0 * m 0 a + T b 1 * m 1 a + T b 2 * m 2 a = m b a * d a := fun b a => by
    have := congrFun (congrFun e b) a
    rwa [Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three] at this
  have h2 : ∀ a, a ≠ 2 → m 2 a = 0 := fun a ha => by
    have := e' 2 a
    rw [t20, t21] at this
    refine (mul_eq_zero.1 (?_ : (T 2 2 - d a) * m 2 a = 0)).resolve_left (sub_ne_zero.2 (hd a 2 ha))
    linear_combination this
  have h1 : m 1 0 = 0 := by
    have := e' 1 0
    rw [t10, h2 0 (by decide)] at this
    refine (mul_eq_zero.1 (?_ : (T 1 1 - d 0) * m 1 0 = 0)).resolve_left (sub_ne_zero.2 (hd 0 1 (by decide)))
    linear_combination this
  have h20 := h2 0 (by decide)
  have h21 := h2 1 (by decide)
  intro i j hij
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> first | exact absurd hij (by decide) | simpa

private theorem go_diag {C : Type} [CommRing C] {X Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C} (hX : X.BlockTriangular id)
    (hY : Y.BlockTriangular id) (a : Fin 3) : (X * Y) a a = X a a * Y a a := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single a]
  · intro b _ hb
    rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hb with h | h
    · rw [hX h, zero_mul]
    · rw [hY h, mul_zero]
  · simp

private theorem go_conj {K : Type} [Field K] (m : GL (Fin 3) K) (hm : m.val.BlockTriangular id)
    (Y : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) (hY : Y.BlockTriangular id) :
    ((m⁻¹).val * Y * m.val).BlockTriangular id ∧ ∀ a, ((m⁻¹).val * Y * m.val) a a = Y a a := by
  have hi : (m⁻¹).val.BlockTriangular id := by
    rw [Matrix.coe_units_inv]
    haveI := m.invertible
    exact Matrix.blockTriangular_inv_of_blockTriangular hm
  refine ⟨(hi.mul hY).mul hm, fun a => ?_⟩
  rw [go_diag (hi.mul hY) hm, go_diag hi hY, mul_right_comm, ← go_diag hi hm, Units.inv_mul, Matrix.one_apply_eq,
    one_mul]

private theorem go_mat {D C : Type} [Group D] [CommRing C] (r : D →* GG 3 C) (h : GL (Fin 3) C) (g : D)
    (hg : (r g).right = 1) : (conjBy h⁻¹ r g).mat = (h⁻¹).val * (r g).mat * h.val := by
  simp [conjBy, GG.mat, hg]

private theorem diagonal_character_rep_matrix {O D : Type} [CommRing O] [Group D] (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (g : D) :
    (r0 η Ψ g).mat = Matrix.diagonal fun a => UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ a g := rfl

private theorem go_unit {O D : Type} [CommRing O] [Group D] (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (x : D)
    (hu : IsUnit ((Ψ x : O) ^ 2 - 1)) (a b : Fin 3) (hab : a ≠ b) : IsUnit (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ a x - UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ b x) := by
  have e0 : UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ 0 x = Ψ x := by simp [UnitaryThreeInertia.psi, wt1]
  have e1 : UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ 1 x = 1 := by simp [UnitaryThreeInertia.psi, wt1]
  have e2 : UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ 2 x = ((Ψ x)⁻¹ : Oˣ) := by simp [UnitaryThreeInertia.psi, wt1]
  generalize Ψ x = t at *
  have ht : (t : O) * (t⁻¹ : Oˣ) = 1 := Units.mul_inv t
  have h01 : IsUnit (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ 0 x - UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ 1 x) := by
    rw [e0, e1]
    exact isUnit_of_mul_isUnit_left (y := (t : O) + 1) (by convert hu using 1; ring)
  have h02 : IsUnit (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ 0 x - UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ 2 x) := by
    rw [e0, e2]
    exact isUnit_of_mul_isUnit_left (y := (t : O)) (by convert hu using 1; linear_combination -ht)
  have h12 : IsUnit (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ 1 x - UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ 2 x) := by
    rw [e1, e2]
    exact isUnit_of_mul_isUnit_left (y := (t : O) * (t + 1))
      (by convert hu using 1; linear_combination (-(t : O) - 1) * ht)
  have hs : ∀ {u v : O}, IsUnit (u - v) → IsUnit (v - u) := fun h => by rw [← neg_sub]; exact h.neg
  fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> first | exact absurd rfl hab | assumption | exact hs (by assumption)

private theorem go_uadd {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] {u v : A} (hu : IsUnit u) (h : v - u ∈ maximalIdeal A) :
    IsUnit v := by
  by_contra hv
  have h1 : v ∈ maximalIdeal A := hv
  have h2 : u ∈ maximalIdeal A := by simpa using Ideal.sub_mem _ h1 h
  exact h2 hu

private theorem triangular_of_reducible_rank_three {O A K D : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal A) A] [Algebra O A] [Field K] [Algebra O K] [Group D]
    (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (I : Subgroup D) (r : D →* GG 3 A) (hr : UnitaryThreeInertia.Red η Ψ r) (j : A →ₐ[O] K)
    (hj : Function.Injective j) (x : D) (hx : x ∈ I) (hη : η x = 1) (hu : IsUnit ((Ψ x : O) ^ 2 - 1))
    (k : GL (Fin 3) K) (hk : UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn η Ψ I (push j r) k) : UnitaryThreeInertia.CondT η Ψ I r := by
  have hw : ∀ a b : Fin 3, wt a b < 0 ↔ b < a := by decide
  have hd : ∀ a, (r x).mat a a - algebraMap O A (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ a x) ∈ maximalIdeal A := fun a => by
    simpa [diagonal_character_rep_matrix] using hr.red x a a
  have ho : ∀ a b, a ≠ b → (r x).mat a b ∈ maximalIdeal A := fun a b hab => by
    simpa [diagonal_character_rep_matrix, hab] using hr.red x a b
  have hU := fun a b hab => (go_unit Ψ x hu a b hab).map (algebraMap O A)
  obtain ⟨h, h1, h2, h3⟩ := UnitaryThreeInertia.exists_canon (r x).mat (fun a b hab => go_uadd (hU a b hab) (by
    convert Ideal.sub_mem _ (hd a) (hd b) using 1
    rw [map_sub]; ring)) ho
  obtain ⟨d, hdd⟩ : ∃ d, Matrix.diagonal d = (h⁻¹).val * (r x).mat * h.val := ⟨_, h3.diagonal_diag⟩
  have hX : (r x).mat * h.val = h.val * Matrix.diagonal d := by
    rw [hdd, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.one_mul]
  have hdm : ∀ a, d a - (r x).mat a a ∈ maximalIdeal A := fun a => by
    have e := congrFun (congrFun hX a) a
    rw [Matrix.mul_diagonal, h2, one_mul, Matrix.mul_apply, ← Finset.add_sum_erase _ _ (Finset.mem_univ a), h2,
      mul_one] at e
    rw [← e, add_sub_cancel_left]
    exact Ideal.sum_mem _ fun b hb => Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ (ho a b (Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hb).symm)
  let h' : GL (Fin 3) K := Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (j : A →+* K) h
  have hm : ∀ g, η g = 1 → (push j r g).right = 1 := fun g hg => (hr.mult g).trans hg
  have hT : ∀ g, η g = 1 → ((k⁻¹).val * (push j r g).mat * k.val).BlockTriangular id := fun g hg a b hab => by
    rw [← go_mat _ _ _ (hm g hg)]
    exact hk.tri g hg a b ((hw a b).2 hab)
  have hI : ∀ g ∈ I, η g = 1 → ∀ a, ((k⁻¹).val * (push j r g).mat * k.val) a a = algebraMap O K (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ a g) :=
    fun g hg hg' a => by
      rw [← go_mat _ _ _ (hm g hg')]
      exact hk.ine g hg hg' a
  have hup : (k⁻¹ * h').val.BlockTriangular id := by
    refine go_up _ _ (fun a => j (d a)) (hT x hη) (fun a b hab => ?_) ?_
    · rw [hI x hx hη, ← j.commutes, ← sub_ne_zero, ← map_sub]
      refine ((go_uadd (hU b a hab.symm) ?_).map j).ne_zero
      convert (maximalIdeal A).neg_mem (Ideal.add_mem _ (hdm a) (hd a)) using 1
      rw [map_sub]; ring
    · have e := congrArg (fun M => M.map (j : A →+* K)) hX
      simp only [Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.diagonal_map (map_zero (j : A →+* K))] at e
      rw [Units.val_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc k.val, Units.mul_inv, Matrix.one_mul,
        Matrix.mul_assoc]
      exact congrArg _ e
  have key : ∀ g, η g = 1 → ∀ a b, j ((conjBy h⁻¹ r g).mat a b) =
      (((k⁻¹ * h')⁻¹).val * ((k⁻¹).val * (push j r g).mat * k.val) * (k⁻¹ * h').val) a b := fun g hg a b => by
    have e : ((k⁻¹ * h')⁻¹).val * ((k⁻¹).val * (push j r g).mat * k.val) * (k⁻¹ * h').val =
        (h'⁻¹).val * (push j r g).mat * h'.val := by
      simp only [_root_.mul_inv_rev, inv_inv, Units.val_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.mul_inv_cancel_left]
    rw [e, go_mat _ _ _ ((hr.mult g).trans hg), ← map_inv]
    show ((h⁻¹).val * (r g).mat * h.val).map (j : A →+* K) a b = _
    rw [Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.map_mul]
    rfl
  refine ⟨h, h1, fun g hg a b hab => hj ?_, fun g hg hg' a => hj ?_⟩
  · rw [key g hg, map_zero]
    exact (go_conj _ hup _ (hT g hg)).1 ((hw a b).1 hab)
  · rw [key g hg', (go_conj _ hup _ (hT g hg')).2, hI g hg hg', j.commutes]



[UCE_SRC_ORDINARY_POINT] Actual local-domain push and the full ordPt_of_gd conclusion
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/TriangularOfReducibleRankThree.lean#L145-L233
private theorem go_tr {O A H G : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [Group H] [Group G]
    (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Oˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 A) (ι : H →* G) (J : Subgroup H) (Dc In : Subgroup G)
    (hD : ι.range = Dc) (hI : J.map ι = In) (k : GL (Fin 3) A)
    (hk : UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι) J (r.comp ι) k) :
    UnitaryThreeInertia.TriIn (η.comp Dc.subtype) (Ψ.comp Dc.subtype) (In.subgroupOf Dc) (r.comp Dc.subtype) k := by
  subst hD hI
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨_, σ, rfl⟩ hg
    exact hk.tri σ hg
  · rintro ⟨s, hs⟩ ⟨σ, hσ, e⟩ hg
    obtain rfl : ι σ = s := e
    exact hk.ine σ hσ hg

private theorem go_r0map {G A B : Type} [Group G] [CommRing A] [CommRing B] (φ : A →+* B) (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Aˣ)
    (g : G) (a b : Fin 3) :
    (r0 η ((Units.map φ.toMonoidHom).comp Ψ) g).mat a b = φ ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) := by
  simp only [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val]
  by_cases h : a = b
  · subst h
    rw [Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq]
    exact (congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map φ.toMonoidHom) (Ψ g) (wt1 a))).symm
  · rw [Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ h, Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ h, map_zero]

private theorem go_push {O R A Γ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [Alg.IsCO O R]
    [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra O A] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ}
    (r : GLift O η Ψ R) (f : R →ₐ[O] A) (hf : IsLocalHom f) :
    UnitaryThreeInertia.Red η Ψ (push f r.hom) ∧ UnitaryThreeInertia.AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) (push f r.hom) := by
  have hm : ∀ y ∈ maximalIdeal R, f y ∈ maximalIdeal A := fun y hy hu => hy (hf.map_nonunit y hu)
  refine ⟨⟨fun g => r.hasMult g, fun g a b => ?_⟩, fun n => ?_⟩
  · show f ((r.hom g).mat a b) - algebraMap O A ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) ∈ _
    rw [← f.commutes, ← map_sub]
    refine hm _ ((Alg.res_eq_zero_iff O R _).1 ?_)
    have h2 : Alg.res O R ((r.hom g).mat a b) = (rbar O η Ψ g).mat a b :=
      congrArg (fun x : GG 3 (ResidueField O) => x.mat a b) (r.red g)
    have h3 : (rbar O η Ψ g).mat a b = residue O ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) := go_r0map _ _ _ _ _ _
    rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, h2, h3]
    exact sub_self _
  · have hle : maximalIdeal R ^ n ≤ (maximalIdeal A ^ n).comap (f : R →+* A) :=
      le_trans (Ideal.pow_right_mono (fun y hy => hm y hy) n) (Ideal.le_comap_pow _ n)
    refine (IsLocallyConstant.iff_eventually_eq _).2 fun g => ?_
    have h := fun p : Fin 3 × Fin 3 =>
      ((r.cont p.1 p.2 n).comp (Ideal.quotientMap _ (f : R →+* A) hle)).eventually_eq g
    filter_upwards [Filter.eventually_all.2 h] with g' hg'
    exact Matrix.ext fun a b => hg' (a, b)

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.ordPt_of_gd :
    Loc.LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω), D.Marked g₁ → ∀ (U : D.ULoc) (S : D.Ring U g₁),
    ∀ (B : ValuationSubring Ω), (∀ x, x ∈ B ↔ ‖D.j x‖ ≤ 1) → ∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal A) A] [Algebra c.O A] (f : S.R →ₐ[c.O] A), IsLocalHom f →
      OrdPt l D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B (U3.push f S.r.hom) := by
  intro hP Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D g₁ _ U S B hB A _ _ _ _ _ f hf
  have hid : S.r.map (AlgHom.id c.O S.R) = S.r := GLift.ext rfl
  have h0 := (S.rep S.R).1 (AlgHom.id c.O S.R)
  rw [hid] at h0
  obtain ⟨hn, hk, φ, hφ, hI⟩ := h0
  obtain ⟨hred, hcont⟩ := go_push S.r f hf
  obtain ⟨B₀, hl, hD, hIn, -, hB₀, -⟩ := dat_loc D
  obtain rfl : B = B₀ := ValuationSubring.ext _ _ fun x => (hB x).trans (hB₀ x).symm
  refine ⟨hred, hcont, ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩, fun B' hB' s hs hη hΨ => ?_, ?_⟩
  · show (S.r.hom D.cc).mat.map (f : S.R →+* A) = 1
    rw [hn.atc, Matrix.map_one _ (map_zero _) (map_one _)]
  · have := congrArg f hn.z12
    rw [map_zero, zmat, Matrix.sub_apply, map_sub] at this
    exact this
  · have := congrArg f hn.z21
    rw [map_zero, zmat, Matrix.sub_apply, map_sub] at this
    exact this
  · show GG.map (f : S.R →+* A) (S.r.hom s) = 1
    rw [hk s ⟨B', hB', hs, ?_⟩, map_one]
    refine SemidirectProduct.ext ?_ hη
    show tor _ (Units.map _ (D.Ψ s)) = 1
    rw [hΨ, map_one, map_one]
  · obtain ⟨hη, x, hx, hu⟩ := (dat_facts D).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 B hl
    let j₀ : A →ₐ[c.O] FractionRing A := IsScalarTower.toAlgHom c.O A (FractionRing A)
    have h1 : (U.r.map φ).hom = S.r.hom.comp D.ι := congrArg GLift.hom hφ
    obtain ⟨K', _, _, _, _, _, k, hk'⟩ := @Loc.lemma2c hP l _ c.O _ _ c.dvr _ _ c.fin
      (UpperTheory.V.Base.dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.1 D.loc U (FractionRing A) _ _
      ((push j₀ (push f S.r.hom)).comp D.ι) ⟨j₀.comp (f.comp φ), by
        refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_
        show GG.map (j₀ : A →+* FractionRing A) (GG.map (f : S.R →+* A) ((U.r.map φ).hom g)) = _
        rw [h1]
        rfl, fun y hy => by
        have : φ y = 0 := hI hy
        show j₀ (f (φ y)) = 0
        rw [this, map_zero, map_zero]⟩
    let j : A →ₐ[c.O] K' := (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom c.O (FractionRing A) K').comp j₀
    have hj : Function.Injective j :=
      (algebraMap (FractionRing A) K').injective.comp (IsFractionRing.injective A (FractionRing A))
    exact triangular_of_reducible_rank_three _ _ _ _ ⟨fun g => hred.mult _, fun g => hred.red _⟩ j hj ⟨x, hx.1⟩ hx (hη hx) hu k
      (go_tr D.η D.Ψ (push j (push f S.r.hom)) D.ι (Loc.Iq l) _ _ hD hIn k hk')


[UCE_SRC_END_CONTRACTS] Full OrdPt, FinEnd, AutEnd3 and AutEnd2, including every curve, open subgroup and outer extension
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/RatTorsionFree.lean#L21-L52
structure OrdPt {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A) : Prop where
  red : UnitaryThreeInertia.Red η Ψ r
  cont : UnitaryThreeInertia.AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) r
  norm : Normalised σ g₁ r
  away : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B', η s = 1 → Ψ s = 1 → r s = 1
  ord : UnitaryThreeInertia.CondT (η.comp (Dec (F := ℚ) B).subtype) (Ψ.comp (Dec (F := ℚ) B).subtype)
    ((Ine (F := ℚ) B).subgroupOf (Dec (F := ℚ) B)) (r.comp (Dec (F := ℚ) B).subtype)

def FinEnd : Prop :=
  ∃ U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), U.FiniteIndex ∧ ∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsCD O A],
    (∀ a ∈ maximalIdeal O, algebraMap O A a = 0) → ∀ r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A, OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r →
      ∀ g ∈ U, η g = 1 → (r g).mat.charpoly = (((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap O A)).charpoly

end Ord

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω)

def AutEnd3 (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (A : Subalgebra c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) [IsDiscreteValuationRing A] [Module.Finite c.O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A),
    OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r →
    (∀ U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), IsOpen (U : Set (G ℚ Ω)) → U ≤ η.ker → MatIrr U (mats (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r))) →
    ∀ [Algebra K Ω] (ρ : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)),
      (∀ s, (ρ s).val = (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r (Base.galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).mat) →
      Af.IsAutCsd ι (Af.tw ρ (cycBar K Ω l)⁻¹)

def AutEnd2 (e : ℕ) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (A : Subalgebra c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) [IsDiscreteValuationRing A] [Module.Finite c.O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A),
    OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r → TypeII η (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r) → (∀ g ∈ η.ker, (r g).mat 1 1 = 1) →
    ∀ W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l),
      (∀ g ∈ η.ker, (W g).val = corner (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r g).mat) →
      (W σ).val.trace = 0 →
      ∃ f : Af.Newform (2 * e + 1), f.Att ι (Af.tw W (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ (-(e : ℤ))))


[UCE_SRC_GD_END] One common marked element supplying all three original ordinary end conditions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/RatTorsionFree.lean#L63-L68
def GdEnd : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ)
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
  ∃ g₁, Marks D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ ∧ FinEnd l D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B ∧ AutEnd3 c D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B K ι ∧
    AutEnd2 c D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B e ι


[UCE_SRC_JOINT_TARGET] Original FinA, AutB, LemW and their joint unramified-coefficient producer
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/AutomorphicCases/AutomorphyUnramifiedCoefficients.lean#L12-L27
def FinA (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Prop :=
  ∀ (U : D.ULoc) (S : D.Ring U g₁), Module.Finite c.O (weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom)

def AutB [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Prop :=
  ∀ (U : D.ULoc) (S : D.Ring U g₁) (ξ : entAlg c.O S.r.hom →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l)
    (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)) (hr : HasMult D.η r), IsPush ξ S.r.hom r → TypeI D.η r → D.IsAutI ι r hr

def LemW (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Prop :=
  ∀ (U : D.ULoc) (S : D.Ring U g₁) (ξ : entAlg c.O S.r.hom →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l)
    (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)), IsPush ξ S.r.hom r → TypeII D.η r → (∀ g ∈ D.η.ker, (r g).mat 1 1 = 1) →
    ∀ W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l), D.IsExt r W → D.IsModular ι W

def AutomorphyUnramifiedCoefficients : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ),
    eO l c.O = 1 → ∀ ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ, ∃ g₁ : G ℚ Ω, D.Marked g₁ ∧ FinA D g₁ ∧ AutB D ι g₁ ∧ LemW D ι g₁


[UCE_SRC_COMPLETE_LOCAL] IsCO: original complete Noetherian local coefficient-algebra scope
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/ResMapBijective.lean#L9-L18
class IsCO (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] : Prop
    extends IsLocalRing R, IsNoetherianRing R where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal R) R
  isLocalHom : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)
  res_surj : ∀ x : R, ∃ a : O, x - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R

attribute [instance] IsCO.complete IsCO.isLocalHom

instance IsCO.self (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] : IsCO O O where


[UCE_SRC_WEIGHTED_PRESENTATION] Homogeneous projections, WPres, actual parts, graded algebra and weight action
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/ResMapBijective.lean#L74-L225
