def AdicCont {Γ R : Type} [TopologicalSpace Γ] [CommRing R] (I : Ideal R) (f : Γ → R) : Prop :=
  ∀ N : ℕ, IsLocallyConstant fun g => Ideal.Quotient.mk (I ^ N) (f g)

section Weights

variable (O : Type) [CommRing O] {σ X : Type} [AddCommGroup X] (wt : σ → X)

def wtPart (j : X) : Submodule O (MvPowerSeries σ O) where
  carrier := {f | ∀ n, Finsupp.weight wt n ≠ j → coeff n f = 0}
  add_mem' ha hb n hn := by rw [map_add, ha n hn, hb n hn, add_zero]
  zero_mem' n _ := map_zero _
  smul_mem' c f hf n hn := by rw [map_smul, hf n hn, smul_zero]

open Classical in

def wcomp (j : X) : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₗ[O] MvPowerSeries σ O where
  toFun f := fun n => if Finsupp.weight wt n = j then coeff n f else 0
  map_add' f g := funext fun n => by
    show (if _ then _ else _) = (if _ then _ else _) + (if _ then _ else (0 : O))
    split_ifs <;> simp
  map_smul' c f := funext fun n => by
    show (if _ then _ else _) = c • (if _ then _ else (0 : O))
    split_ifs <;> simp

open Classical in
theorem coeff_wcomp (j : X) (f : MvPowerSeries σ O) (n : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    coeff n (wcomp O wt j f) = if Finsupp.weight wt n = j then coeff n f else 0 := rfl

theorem wcomp_mem (j : X) (f : MvPowerSeries σ O) : wcomp O wt j f ∈ wtPart O wt j :=
  fun n hn => by rw [coeff_wcomp, if_neg hn]

theorem mul_mem_wtPart {i j : X} {f g : MvPowerSeries σ O} (hf : f ∈ wtPart O wt i) (hg : g ∈ wtPart O wt j) :
    f * g ∈ wtPart O wt (i + j) := fun n hn => by
  classical
  rw [coeff_mul]
  refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun p hp => ?_
  by_cases h1 : Finsupp.weight wt p.1 = i
  · have h2 : Finsupp.weight wt p.2 ≠ j := fun h2 => hn (by
      rw [← Finset.HasAntidiagonal.mem_antidiagonal.1 hp, map_add, h1, h2])
    rw [hg _ h2, mul_zero]
  · rw [hf _ h1, zero_mul]

theorem one_mem_wtPart : (1 : MvPowerSeries σ O) ∈ wtPart O wt 0 := fun n hn => by
  classical
  rw [coeff_one, if_neg]
  rintro rfl
  exact hn (map_zero _)

theorem X_mem_wtPart (i : σ) : (MvPowerSeries.X i : MvPowerSeries σ O) ∈ wtPart O wt (wt i) := fun n hn => by
  classical
  rw [coeff_X, if_neg]
  rintro rfl
  exact hn (by simp [Finsupp.weight_single])

instance : SetLike.GradedMonoid (wtPart O wt) where
  one_mem := one_mem_wtPart O wt
  mul_mem _ _ _ _ := mul_mem_wtPart O wt

def IsHomog (J : Ideal (MvPowerSeries σ O)) : Prop := ∀ f ∈ J, ∀ j, wcomp O wt j f ∈ J

end Weights

def gradedSub {O R X : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [AddCommGroup X] (𝒜 : X → Submodule O R)
    [SetLike.GradedMonoid 𝒜] : Subalgebra O R :=
  (⨆ j, 𝒜 j).toSubalgebra (le_iSup 𝒜 0 (SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem)) fun x y hx hy => by
    refine Submodule.iSup_induction 𝒜 (motive := fun x => x * y ∈ ⨆ j, 𝒜 j) hx (fun i a ha => ?_) (by simp)
      (fun a b ha hb => by rw [add_mul]; exact add_mem ha hb)
    exact Submodule.iSup_induction 𝒜 (motive := fun y => a * y ∈ ⨆ j, 𝒜 j) hy
      (fun j b hb => le_iSup 𝒜 (i + j) (SetLike.GradedMul.mul_mem ha hb)) (by simp)
      (fun b c hb hc => by rw [mul_add]; exact add_mem hb hc)

structure WPres (O X R : Type) [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] where
  σ : Type
  [fin : Fintype σ]
  wt : σ → X
  π : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] R
  surj : Function.Surjective π
  homog : IsHomog O wt (RingHom.ker π)

attribute [instance] WPres.fin

namespace WPres

variable {O X R : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (W : WPres O X R)

def free (O : Type) [CommRing O] {σ : Type} [Fintype σ] (wt : σ → X) : WPres O X (MvPowerSeries σ O) where
  σ := σ
  wt := wt
  π := AlgHom.id O _
  surj := Function.surjective_id
  homog f hf j := by
    have : f = 0 := hf
    subst this
    simp

def z (i : W.σ) : R := W.π (MvPowerSeries.X i)

def part (j : X) : Submodule O R := (wtPart O W.wt j).map W.π.toLinearMap

instance : SetLike.GradedMonoid W.part where
  one_mem := ⟨1, one_mem_wtPart O W.wt, map_one W.π⟩
  mul_mem := by
    rintro i j _ _ ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩ ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨f * g, mul_mem_wtPart O W.wt hf hg, map_mul W.π f g⟩

theorem z_mem (i : W.σ) : W.z i ∈ W.part (W.wt i) := ⟨_, X_mem_wtPart O W.wt i, rfl⟩

def A : Subalgebra O R := gradedSub W.part

def gr (j : X) : Submodule O W.A := (W.part j).comap W.A.val.toLinearMap

instance : SetLike.GradedMonoid W.gr where
  one_mem := (SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem : (1 : R) ∈ W.part 0)
  mul_mem _ _ _ _ ha hb := (SetLike.GradedMul.mul_mem ha hb : _ ∈ W.part _)

theorem part_le_A {j : X} {x : R} (hx : x ∈ W.part j) : x ∈ W.A := le_iSup W.part j hx

def zA (i : W.σ) : W.A := ⟨W.z i, W.part_le_A (W.z_mem i)⟩

def HasOrigin : Prop := ∀ f, W.π f = 0 → constantCoeff f = 0

def origin (h : W.HasOrigin) : R →ₐ[O] O :=
  (Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ (RingHom.ker W.π)
    ({ (constantCoeff : MvPowerSeries W.σ O →+* O) with commutes' := fun a => by simp [MvPowerSeries.algebraMap_apply] } : MvPowerSeries W.σ O →ₐ[O] O)
    h).comp (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective W.surj).symm.toAlgHom

theorem origin_π (h : W.HasOrigin) (f : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) : W.origin h (W.π f) = constantCoeff f := by
  have : (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective W.surj).symm (W.π f) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ f := by
    rw [AlgEquiv.symm_apply_eq]; rfl
  simp only [origin, AlgHom.comp_apply, AlgEquiv.coe_algHom]
  erw [this]
  rfl

end WPres

section Points

variable {O A X K : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [AddCommGroup X] [DecidableEq X]
  (𝒜 : X → Submodule O A) [GradedAlgebra 𝒜] [CommRing K] [Algebra O K]

def act (t : Multiplicative X →* Kˣ) (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) : A →ₐ[O] K :=
  (DirectSum.toAlgebra O (fun j => 𝒜 j)
    (fun j => ((t (Multiplicative.ofAdd j) : Kˣ) : K) • (ξ.toLinearMap ∘ₗ (𝒜 j).subtype))
    (by simp) (fun {i j} a b => by
      simp only [LinearMap.smul_apply, LinearMap.comp_apply, Submodule.subtype_apply, AlgHom.toLinearMap_apply,
        SetLike.coe_gMul, map_mul, ofAdd_add, Units.val_mul, smul_eq_mul]
      ring)).comp (DirectSum.decomposeAlgEquiv 𝒜).toAlgHom

theorem act_of_mem (t : Multiplicative X →* Kˣ) (ξ : A →ₐ[O] K) {j : X} {f : A} (hf : f ∈ 𝒜 j) :
    act 𝒜 t ξ f = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd j) * ξ f := by
  have h : DirectSum.decompose 𝒜 f = DirectSum.of (fun j => 𝒜 j) j ⟨f, hf⟩ := DirectSum.decompose_of_mem 𝒜 hf
  simp [act, h, DirectSum.toAlgebra]


[UCE_SRC_ENTRY_GRADING] Integral entry-coordinate grading from the actual universal torus action
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/CoordGrading.lean#L12-L93
private theorem map_mem_max {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    [IsCO O R] {R' : Type} [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f : R →ₐ[O] R') {x : R}
    (hx : x ∈ maximalIdeal R) : f x ∈ maximalIdeal R' := by
  rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, res_map, res_eq_zero_iff]
  exact hx

private theorem ext_X {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] {σ : Type} [Finite σ] {R' : Type} [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R']
    [IsCO O R'] (F G : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] R') (hm : ∀ s, F (X s) ∈ maximalIdeal R')
    (h : ∀ s, F (X s) = G (X s)) : F = G := by
  have hC : ∀ (H : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] R') (x : O), H (C x) = Algebra.ofId O R' x := fun H x => by
    have := H.commutes x
    rw [MvPowerSeries.algebraMap_apply] at this
    exact this
  exact (co_ev O O R' σ (Algebra.ofId O R') (fun s => F (X s)) hm).unique ⟨hC F, fun _ => rfl⟩
    ⟨hC G, fun s => (h s).symm⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.Alg.coord_grading :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (X : Type) [AddCommGroup X] [Module.Free ℤ X] [Module.Finite ℤ X] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (S : Type) (e : S → R) (wt : S → X), (∀ s, e s ∈ maximalIdeal R) →
      (∀ (R' : Type) [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f f' : R →ₐ[O] R'), (∀ s, f (e s) = f' (e s)) → f = f') →
      (∀ (R' : Type) [CommRing R'] [Algebra O R'] [IsCO O R'] (f : R →ₐ[O] R') (t : Multiplicative X →* R'ˣ), (∀ j, res O R' (t j) = 1) →
        ∃ f' : R →ₐ[O] R', ∀ s, f' (e s) = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (wt s)) * f (e s)) →
      ∃ (W : WPres O X R) (u : W.σ → S), (∀ i, W.z i = e (u i) ∧ W.wt i = wt (u i)) ∧ ∀ s, e s ∈ W.part (wt s) := by
  intro O _ _ _ _ X _ _ _ R _ _ _ S e wt hem hgen hst
  classical
  have hspan : maximalIdeal R ≤ Ideal.span (Set.range e) ⊔ (maximalIdeal O).map (Algebra.ofId O R) ⊔
      maximalIdeal R ^ 2 := by
    refine co_span O R O (Algebra.ofId O R) (Set.range e) (by rintro _ ⟨s, rfl⟩; exact hem s) ?_
    intro f f' _ h
    haveI := (co_res O).2
    exact hgen _ f f' fun s => h _ ⟨s, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨T, hT, hTs⟩ := (Submodule.fg_span_iff_fg_span_finset_subset (R := R) (Set.range e)).1
    (IsNoetherian.noetherian _)
  choose u hu using fun i : T => hT i.2
  obtain ⟨π, ⟨hπC, hπX⟩, -⟩ := co_ev O O R T (Algebra.ofId O R) (fun i => e (u i)) fun i => hem _
  have hsurj : Function.Surjective π := by
    haveI := co_mvps O O T
    refine co_surj O (MvPowerSeries T O) R π (hspan.trans (sup_le_sup_right (sup_le ?_ ?_) _))
    · show Submodule.span R (Set.range e) ≤ _
      rw [hTs, Submodule.span_le]
      intro x hx
      have h1 : x = π (MvPowerSeries.X ⟨x, hx⟩) := by rw [hπX, hu]
      rw [h1]
      refine Ideal.mem_map_of_mem π fun hU => ?_
      have := hU.map π
      rw [hπX] at this
      exact hem _ this
    · rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
      intro a ha
      rw [Ideal.mem_comap, ← hπC]
      refine Ideal.mem_map_of_mem π fun hU => ?_
      have := hU.map (constantCoeff : MvPowerSeries T O →+* O)
      rw [constantCoeff_C] at this
      exact ha this
  have hπm : ∀ i, π (MvPowerSeries.X i) ∈ maximalIdeal R := fun i => by rw [hπX]; exact hem _
  have hhom : IsHomog O (fun i : T => wt (u i)) (RingHom.ker π) := by
    refine homog_of_stable O X R T _ π hπm ?_
    intro R' _ _ _ f t ht hf f' hf' x hx
    let g : R →ₐ[O] R' := (Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ (RingHom.ker π) f hf).comp
      (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective hsurj).symm.toAlgHom
    have hg : ∀ y, g (π y) = f y := fun y => by
      have : (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective hsurj).symm (π y) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ y := by
        rw [AlgEquiv.symm_apply_eq]; rfl
      show Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ (RingHom.ker π) f hf ((Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective hsurj).symm (π y)) = _
      rw [this]
      rfl
    obtain ⟨g', hg'⟩ := hst R' g t ht
    have : g'.comp π = f' := ext_X _ _ (fun i => map_mem_max g' (hπm i)) fun i => by
      rw [AlgHom.comp_apply, hf', ← hg, hπX, hg']
    rw [← this, AlgHom.comp_apply, hx, map_zero]
  let W : WPres O X R := { σ := T, wt := fun i => wt (u i), π := π, surj := hsurj, homog := hhom }
  refine ⟨W, u, fun i => ⟨hπX i, rfl⟩, fun s => eigen_mem O X R W hπm (e s) (wt s) ?_⟩
  intro R' _ _ _ f f' t ht h
  obtain ⟨g', hg'⟩ := hst R' f t ht
  have h1 : g'.comp π = f'.comp π := ext_X _ _ (fun i => map_mem_max g' (hπm i)) fun i => by
    have h2 : f' (π (MvPowerSeries.X i)) = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (wt (u i))) * f (π (MvPowerSeries.X i)) := h i
    rw [AlgHom.comp_apply, AlgHom.comp_apply, h2, hπX, hg']
  have h3 : f' (e s) = g' (e s) := by
    obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hsurj (e s)
    rw [← hy]
    exact (DFunLike.congr_fun h1 y).symm
  rw [h3, hg']


[UCE_SRC_FINITE_MONOMIALS] Finite fixed-weight monomial spanning in the original weighted presentation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/PartSpan.lean#L5-L51
theorem UpperTheory.V.Alg.part_span :
    ∀ (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (X : Type) [AddCommGroup X] [Module.Free ℤ X] [Module.Finite ℤ X] (W : WPres O X R) (S : Set (W.σ →₀ ℕ)) (j : X), (∀ n ∈ S, Finsupp.weight W.wt n = j) →
      ∃ μ : Finset (W.σ →₀ ℕ), (μ : Set _) ⊆ S ∧ ∀ f : MvPowerSeries W.σ O, (∀ n, n ∉ S → coeff n f = 0) →
        ∃ b : (W.σ →₀ ℕ) → MvPowerSeries W.σ O, (∀ m, b m ∈ wtPart O W.wt 0) ∧ f = ∑ m ∈ μ, b m * monomial m 1 := by
  intro O R _ _ _ X _ _ _ W S j hS
  classical
  haveI := W.fin
  have hMfin : {m : W.σ →₀ ℕ | Minimal (· ∈ S) m}.Finite :=
    WellQuasiOrderedLE.finite_of_isAntichain (setOf_minimal_antichain _)
  have hex : ∀ n ∈ S, ∃ m ∈ {m : W.σ →₀ ℕ | Minimal (· ∈ S) m}, m ≤ n := by
    intro n hn
    obtain ⟨b, hb, hmin⟩ := (Set.isPWO_of_wellQuasiOrderedLE S).exists_le_minimal hn
    exact ⟨b, hmin, hb⟩
  choose! c hcM hcle using hex
  refine ⟨hMfin.toFinset, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [Set.Finite.coe_toFinset]
    exact fun m hm => hm.1
  · intro f hf
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ : ∃ b : (W.σ →₀ ℕ) → MvPowerSeries W.σ O, ∀ m k,
        coeff k (b m) = if k + m ∈ S ∧ c (k + m) = m then coeff (k + m) f else 0 :=
      ⟨fun m k => if k + m ∈ S ∧ c (k + m) = m then coeff (k + m) f else 0, fun _ _ => rfl⟩
    refine ⟨b, ?_, ?_⟩
    · intro m k hk
      rw [hb, if_neg]
      rintro ⟨h1, h2⟩
      apply hk
      have hm : Finsupp.weight W.wt m = j := hS m (by rw [← h2]; exact (hcM _ h1).1)
      have hkm := hS _ h1
      rw [map_add, hm] at hkm
      simpa using hkm
    · ext n
      rw [map_sum, Finset.sum_congr rfl
        (g := fun m => if c n = m then (if n ∈ S then coeff n f else 0) else 0), Finset.sum_ite_eq]
      · by_cases hn : n ∈ S
        · rw [if_pos ((Set.Finite.mem_toFinset hMfin).2 (hcM n hn)), if_pos hn]
        · rw [hf n hn]
          simp
      · intro m _
        rw [MvPowerSeries.coeff_mul_monomial, mul_one]
        by_cases hmn : m ≤ n
        · rw [if_pos hmn, hb, tsub_add_cancel_of_le hmn]
          by_cases h1 : n ∈ S <;> by_cases h2 : c n = m <;> simp [h1, h2]
        · rw [if_neg hmn]
          by_cases h1 : n ∈ S
          · have h3 : c n ≠ m := fun h => hmn (h ▸ hcle n h1)
            simp [h3]
          · simp [h1]


[UCE_SRC_WEIGHT_ZERO_COMPLETE] Complete Noetherian weight-zero coefficient algebra from positive-degree monomial generators
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/WPresGrZeroIsCOOfMemMaximalIdeal.lean#L8-L107
private theorem weight_zero_power_series_surjective (O : Type) [CommRing O] {σ τ X : Type} [Finite τ] [AddCommGroup X] (wt : σ → X)
    (g : τ → (σ →₀ ℕ)) (hg0 : ∀ s, g s ≠ 0) (hgw : ∀ s, Finsupp.weight wt (g s) = 0)
    (hgen : ∀ n, Finsupp.weight wt n = 0 → ∃ d : τ →₀ ℕ, Finsupp.linearCombination ℕ g d = n) :
    ∃ φ : MvPowerSeries τ O →ₐ[O] MvPowerSeries σ O,
      (∀ F, φ F ∈ wtPart O wt 0) ∧ ∀ f ∈ wtPart O wt 0, ∃ F, φ F = f := by
  classical
  let a : τ → MvPowerSeries σ O := fun s => monomial (g s) 1
  have ha : HasSubst a := hasSubst_of_constantCoeff_zero fun s => by
    show constantCoeff (monomial (g s) (1 : O)) = 0
    rw [← coeff_zero_eq_constantCoeff_apply, coeff_monomial, if_neg (hg0 s).symm]
  have hprod : ∀ d : τ →₀ ℕ, (d.prod fun s e => a s ^ e) = monomial (Finsupp.linearCombination ℕ g d) (1 : O) := by
    intro d
    induction d using Finsupp.induction_linear with
    | zero => simp
    | add d d' h h' =>
      rw [Finsupp.prod_add_index' (by simp) (fun s m n => pow_add _ _ _), h, h', map_add, monomial_mul_monomial,
        one_mul]
    | single s k =>
      rw [Finsupp.prod_single_index (by simp), Finsupp.linearCombination_single]
      show (monomial (g s) (1 : O)) ^ k = _
      induction k with
      | zero => simp
      | succ k ih => rw [pow_succ, ih, monomial_mul_monomial, one_mul, succ_nsmul]
  have hw : ∀ d : τ →₀ ℕ, Finsupp.weight wt (Finsupp.linearCombination ℕ g d) = 0 := by
    intro d
    induction d using Finsupp.induction_linear with
    | zero => simp
    | add d d' h h' => rw [map_add, map_add, h, h', add_zero]
    | single s k => rw [Finsupp.linearCombination_single, map_nsmul, hgw, nsmul_zero]
  have hco : ∀ (F : MvPowerSeries τ O) n, coeff n (substAlgHom ha F) =
      ∑ᶠ d, coeff d F * (if n = Finsupp.linearCombination ℕ g d then 1 else 0) := by
    intro F n
    rw [coe_substAlgHom, coeff_subst ha]
    refine finsum_congr fun d => ?_
    rw [hprod, coeff_monomial, smul_eq_mul]
  refine ⟨substAlgHom ha, fun F n hn => ?_, fun f hf => ?_⟩
  · rw [hco]
    refine finsum_eq_zero_of_forall_eq_zero fun d => ?_
    rw [if_neg, mul_zero]
    rintro rfl
    exact hn (hw d)
  · choose! e he using hgen
    let F : MvPowerSeries τ O := fun d =>
      if d = e (Finsupp.linearCombination ℕ g d) then coeff (Finsupp.linearCombination ℕ g d) f else 0
    have hF : ∀ d, coeff d F =
      if d = e (Finsupp.linearCombination ℕ g d) then coeff (Finsupp.linearCombination ℕ g d) f else 0 :=
      fun _ => rfl
    refine ⟨F, ?_⟩
    ext n
    rw [hco]
    by_cases hn : Finsupp.weight wt n = 0
    · rw [finsum_eq_single _ (e n)]
      · rw [hF, he n hn, if_pos rfl, if_pos rfl, mul_one]
      · intro d hd
        by_cases h : n = Finsupp.linearCombination ℕ g d
        · rw [hF, ← h, if_neg hd, zero_mul]
        · rw [if_neg h, mul_zero]
    · rw [hf n hn]
      refine finsum_eq_zero_of_forall_eq_zero fun d => ?_
      rw [if_neg, mul_zero]
      rintro rfl
      exact hn (hw d)

theorem UpperTheory.V.Alg.wPres_gr_zero_isCO_of_mem_maximalIdeal :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (X : Type) [AddCommGroup X] [Module.Free ℤ X] [Module.Finite ℤ X] [DecidableEq X] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (W : WPres O X R), (∀ s, W.z s ∈ maximalIdeal R) → IsCO O (W.gr 0) := by
  intro O _ _ _ _ X _ _ _ _ R _ _ _ W _
  obtain ⟨g, hg, hgen, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Alg.gordan W.σ X W.wt
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, hs⟩ := weight_zero_power_series_surjective O W.wt (fun s : g => (s : W.σ →₀ ℕ)) (fun s => (hg s s.2).1)
    (fun s => (hg s s.2).2) (fun n hn => by
      have h := hgen n hn
      induction h using AddSubmonoid.closure_induction with
      | mem x hx => exact ⟨Finsupp.single ⟨x, hx⟩ 1, by simp⟩
      | zero => exact ⟨0, map_zero _⟩
      | add x y hx hy ihx ihy =>
        have hw : ∀ z ∈ AddSubmonoid.closure (g : Set (W.σ →₀ ℕ)), Finsupp.weight W.wt z = 0 := by
          intro z hz
          induction hz using AddSubmonoid.closure_induction with
          | mem x hx => exact (hg x hx).2
          | zero => exact map_zero _
          | add x y _ _ hx hy => rw [map_add, hx, hy, add_zero]
        obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := ihx (hw x hx)
        obtain ⟨d', hd'⟩ := ihy (hw y hy)
        exact ⟨d + d', by rw [map_add, hd, hd']⟩)
  have hp : ∀ F, W.π (φ F) ∈ W.part 0 := fun F => ⟨φ F, hφ F, rfl⟩
  let p : MvPowerSeries g O →ₐ[O] W.gr 0 :=
    { toFun := fun F => ⟨⟨W.π (φ F), W.part_le_A (hp F)⟩, hp F⟩
      map_one' := Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (by simp))
      map_mul' := fun F G => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (by simp))
      map_zero' := Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (by simp))
      map_add' := fun F G => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (by simp))
      commutes' := fun c => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (by
        show W.π (φ (algebraMap O _ c)) = algebraMap O R c
        rw [AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes])) }
  haveI : IsCO O (MvPowerSeries g O) := UpperTheory.V.Alg.co_mvps O O g
  haveI : Nontrivial (W.gr 0) := ⟨⟨0, 1, fun h =>
    zero_ne_one (α := R) (congrArg (fun x : W.gr 0 => ((x : W.A) : R)) h)⟩⟩
  refine UpperTheory.V.Alg.co_quot O (MvPowerSeries g O) (W.gr 0) p ?_
  rintro ⟨⟨x, hxA⟩, f, hf, hx⟩
  obtain ⟨F, rfl⟩ := hs f hf
  exact ⟨F, Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext hx)⟩


[UCE_SRC_WEIGHT_ZERO_MAXIMAL] Exact maximal ideal generated by coefficient uniformizer and balanced monomials
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/WPresMaximalIdealGrZeroEqSupSpanMonomials.lean#L6-L81
private theorem UpperTheory.V.Alg.monomial_mem_weight_zero {O σ X : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] (wt : σ → X)
    (m : σ →₀ ℕ) (hm : Finsupp.weight wt m = 0) : (monomial m (1 : O) : MvPowerSeries σ O) ∈ wtPart O wt 0 := by
  classical
  intro n hn
  rw [coeff_monomial, if_neg]
  rintro rfl
  exact hn hm

theorem UpperTheory.V.Alg.wPres_maximalIdeal_gr_zero_eq_sup_span_monomials :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (X : Type) [AddCommGroup X] [Module.Free ℤ X] [Module.Finite ℤ X] [DecidableEq X] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (W : WPres O X R), (∀ s, W.z s ∈ maximalIdeal R) → ∀ [IsCO O (W.gr 0)], (∀ b : W.gr 0, b ∈ maximalIdeal (W.gr 0) ↔ ((b : W.A) : R) ∈ maximalIdeal R) ∧
      ∃ u : Finset (W.gr 0), (∀ b ∈ u, ∃ n : W.σ →₀ ℕ, n ≠ 0 ∧ Finsupp.weight W.wt n = 0 ∧ ((b : W.A) : R) = n.prod fun s k => W.z s ^ k) ∧
        maximalIdeal (W.gr 0) = (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O (W.gr 0)) ⊔ Ideal.span (u : Set (W.gr 0)) := by
  intro O _ _ _ _ X _ _ _ _ R _ _ _ W hz _
  classical
  let φ : W.gr 0 →ₐ[O] R :=
    { toFun := fun b => ((b : W.A) : R)
      map_one' := rfl
      map_mul' := fun _ _ => rfl
      map_zero' := rfl
      map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
      commutes' := fun _ => rfl }
  have hφ : ∀ b : W.gr 0, φ b = ((b : W.A) : R) := fun _ => rfl
  have hinj : Function.Injective φ := fun a b h => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext h)
  have h1 : ∀ b : W.gr 0, b ∈ maximalIdeal (W.gr 0) ↔ ((b : W.A) : R) ∈ maximalIdeal R := fun b => by
    rw [← hφ, ← res_eq_zero_iff O, ← res_eq_zero_iff O, res_map]
  refine ⟨h1, ?_⟩
  let mk : ∀ f : MvPowerSeries W.σ O, f ∈ wtPart O W.wt 0 → W.gr 0 := fun f hf =>
    ⟨⟨W.π f, W.part_le_A (j := 0) ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩⟩, ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩⟩
  have hmk : ∀ f hf, φ (mk f hf) = W.π f := fun _ _ => rfl
  obtain ⟨μ, hμ, hspan⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Alg.part_span O R X W {n | n ≠ 0 ∧ Finsupp.weight W.wt n = 0} 0
    (fun n hn => hn.2)
  let U : μ → W.gr 0 := fun m => mk (monomial m.1 1) (UpperTheory.V.Alg.monomial_mem_weight_zero W.wt m.1 (hμ m.2).2)
  have hU : ∀ m : μ, φ (U m) = m.1.prod fun s k => W.z s ^ k := fun m => by
    show W.π (monomial m.1 1) = _
    rw [monomial_one_eq, Finsupp.prod, Finsupp.prod, map_prod]
    simp only [map_pow, WPres.z]
  have hUm : ∀ m : μ, U m ∈ maximalIdeal (W.gr 0) := fun m => by
    rw [h1, ← hφ, hU]
    obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := Finsupp.support_nonempty_iff.2 (hμ m.2).1
    exact Ideal.prod_mem _ hs (Ideal.pow_mem_of_mem _ (hz s) _
      (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (Finsupp.mem_support_iff.1 hs)))
  have hle : Ideal.span ((Finset.univ.image U : Finset (W.gr 0)) : Set (W.gr 0)) ≤ maximalIdeal (W.gr 0) := by
    rw [Ideal.span_le]
    intro b hb
    obtain ⟨m, -, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 (Finset.mem_coe.1 hb)
    exact hUm m
  refine ⟨Finset.univ.image U, fun b hb => ?_, le_antisymm (fun b hb => ?_) (sup_le ?_ hle)⟩
  · obtain ⟨m, -, rfl⟩ := Finset.mem_image.1 hb
    exact ⟨m.1, (hμ m.2).1, (hμ m.2).2, hU m⟩
  · obtain ⟨f, hf, hfb⟩ : ((b : W.A) : R) ∈ W.part 0 := b.2
    obtain ⟨c, hc, hsum⟩ := hspan (f - C (constantCoeff f)) (fun n hn => by
      rw [map_sub, coeff_C]
      by_cases h0 : n = 0
      · subst h0; simp
      · rw [if_neg h0, sub_zero]
        exact hf n fun h => hn ⟨h0, h⟩)
    have hb' : b - algebraMap O (W.gr 0) (constantCoeff f) = ∑ m : μ, mk (c m.1) (hc m.1) * U m := by
      apply hinj
      have h2 := congrArg W.π hsum
      rw [map_sub, map_sum, ← Finset.sum_coe_sort] at h2
      rw [map_sub, map_sum, AlgHom.commutes, hφ, ← hfb, ← W.π.commutes]
      simp only [map_mul] at h2 ⊢
      exact h2
    have hs : b - algebraMap O (W.gr 0) (constantCoeff f) ∈
        Ideal.span ((Finset.univ.image U : Finset (W.gr 0)) : Set (W.gr 0)) := by
      rw [hb']
      exact Ideal.sum_mem _ fun m _ => Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _
        (Ideal.subset_span (Finset.mem_coe.2 (Finset.mem_image_of_mem U (Finset.mem_univ m))))
    have h3 : algebraMap O (W.gr 0) (constantCoeff f) ∈ maximalIdeal (W.gr 0) := by
      simpa using Ideal.sub_mem _ hb (hle hs)
    have h4 : constantCoeff f ∈ maximalIdeal O := fun h => h3 (h.map _)
    rw [← add_sub_cancel (algebraMap O (W.gr 0) (constantCoeff f)) b]
    exact Submodule.add_mem_sup (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ h4) hs
  · rw [Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap]
    intro a ha h
    exact ha (IsLocalHom.map_nonunit a h)


[UCE_SRC_DVR_CONTRACT] Full ConstOfChar, IsCD and FinCrit contracts, without a characteristic-three exclusion
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/IsUnitOrIsUnitOfAddOne.lean#L95-L122
def ConstOfAlg : Prop :=
  ∀ (k A : Type) [Field k] [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra k A] (x : A) (c : k),
    IsAlgebraic k x → x - algebraMap k A c ∈ maximalIdeal A → x = algebraMap k A c

def ConstOfChar : Prop :=
  ∀ (G k A : Type) [Group G] [Field k] [Finite k] [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra k A],
    (2 : k) ≠ 0 → ∀ (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* kˣ) (c g₁ : G), Marks η Ψ c g₁ → ∀ r : G →* GG 3 A, HasMult η r →
    (∀ g, EntIn (maximalIdeal A) ((r g).mat - ((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap k A))) → Normalised c g₁ r →
    ∀ U : Subgroup G, U.FiniteIndex →
    (∀ g ∈ U, η g = 1 → (r g).mat.charpoly = (((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap k A)).charpoly) →
    (Upper η r ∨ Lower η r) ∧ ∀ g a, (r g).mat a a = algebraMap k A ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a a)

class IsCD (O A : Type) [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] : Prop extends IsDomain A, IsDiscreteValuationRing A where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal A) A
  isLocalHom : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O A)
  fin : Finite (ResidueField A)

attribute [instance] IsCD.complete IsCD.isLocalHom IsCD.fin

def FinCrit : Prop :=
  ∀ (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O]
    [Finite (ResidueField O)] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (W : WPres O ℤ R),
    (∀ i, W.z i ∈ maximalIdeal R) →
    (∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsCD O A] (f : R →ₐ[O] A), IsLocalHom f →
      (∀ a ∈ maximalIdeal O, algebraMap O A a = 0) →
      (∀ i, W.wt i = 0 → f (W.z i) = 0) ∧
        ((∀ i, W.wt i < 0 → f (W.z i) = 0) ∨ ∀ i, 0 < W.wt i → f (W.z i) = 0)) →
    Module.Finite O (W.part 0)


[UCE_SRC_CURVE_DIMENSION] Original dimension-one curve detection and maximal-prime argument
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/NicePrimeEx.lean#L5-L76
private theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_height_le_one {B : Type*} [CommRing B] [IsDomain B] [IsNoetherianRing B]
    (m : Ideal B) [m.IsPrime] {f : B} (hf : f ≠ 0)
    (h : ∀ p : Ideal B, p.IsPrime → p ≠ ⊥ → p ≤ m → f ∈ p) : m.height ≤ 1 := by
  by_cases hm : m = ⊥
  · subst hm
    simp
  have hfin := (Ideal.span {f}).finite_minimalPrimes_of_isNoetherianRing
  have hsub : (m : Set B) ⊆ ⋃ P ∈ (Ideal.span {f}).minimalPrimes, ((id P : Ideal B) : Set B) := by
    intro x hx
    simp only [Set.mem_iUnion, id, SetLike.mem_coe]
    by_cases hx0 : x = 0
    · obtain ⟨P, hP, -⟩ := Ideal.exists_minimalPrimes_le (I := Ideal.span {f}) (J := m)
        ((Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mpr (h m inferInstance hm le_rfl))
      exact ⟨P, hP, hx0 ▸ P.zero_mem⟩
    obtain ⟨P, hP, hPm⟩ := Ideal.exists_minimalPrimes_le (I := Ideal.span {x}) (J := m)
        ((Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mpr hx)
    have hPp : P.IsPrime := hP.1.1
    have hxP : x ∈ P := (Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mp hP.1.2
    have hP1 := Ideal.height_le_one_of_isPrincipal_of_mem_minimalPrimes _ P hP
    have hfP : f ∈ P := h P hPp (fun e => hx0 (by simpa [e] using hxP)) hPm
    obtain ⟨Q, hQ, hQP⟩ := Ideal.exists_minimalPrimes_le (I := Ideal.span {f}) (J := P)
        ((Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mpr hfP)
    have hQp : Q.IsPrime := hQ.1.1
    have hfQ : f ∈ Q := (Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mp hQ.1.2
    have hQ0 : ⊥ < Q := bot_lt_iff_ne_bot.mpr fun e => hf (by simpa [e] using hfQ)
    refine ⟨Q, hQ, ?_⟩
    rcases hQP.eq_or_lt with e | hlt
    · exact e ▸ hxP
    · exfalso
      have h1 := Ideal.height_add_one_le_of_lt_of_isPrime hQ0
      have h2 := Ideal.height_add_one_le_of_lt_of_isPrime hlt
      have h3 : (⊥ : Ideal B).height + 1 + 1 ≤ 1 := (add_le_add h1 le_rfl).trans (h2.trans hP1)
      simp at h3
      exact absurd h3 (by decide)
  obtain ⟨P, hP, hmP⟩ := (Ideal.subset_union_prime_finite hfin (f := id) ⊥ ⊥
    (fun i hi _ _ => hi.1.1)).mp hsub
  have hPp : P.IsPrime := hP.1.1
  exact (Ideal.height_mono hmP).trans
    (Ideal.height_le_one_of_isPrincipal_of_mem_minimalPrimes _ P hP)

private theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_dim_one {R : Type} [CommRing R] [IsNoetherianRing R] [IsLocalRing R]
    (Q : Ideal R) [Q.IsPrime] {f : R} (hfm : f ∈ maximalIdeal R) (hf : f ∉ Q) :
    ∃ p : Ideal R, p.IsPrime ∧ Q ≤ p ∧ f ∉ p ∧ ringKrullDim (R ⧸ p) = 1 := by
  obtain ⟨p, ⟨hp, hQp, hfp⟩, hmax⟩ := set_has_maximal_iff_noetherian.mpr
    (inferInstance : IsNoetherian R R) {p : Ideal R | p.IsPrime ∧ Q ≤ p ∧ f ∉ p}
    ⟨Q, inferInstance, le_rfl, hf⟩
  refine ⟨p, hp, hQp, hfp, ?_⟩
  haveI : Nontrivial (R ⧸ p) := Ideal.Quotient.nontrivial_iff.mpr hp.ne_top
  haveI : IsLocalRing (R ⧸ p) :=
    IsLocalRing.of_surjective' (Ideal.Quotient.mk p) Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
  have hf0 : Ideal.Quotient.mk p f ≠ 0 := fun h => hfp (Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.mp h)
  have hh : (maximalIdeal (R ⧸ p)).height ≤ 1 := by
    refine UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_height_le_one _ hf0 fun P hP hP0 _ => ?_
    by_contra hfP
    have hle : p ≤ P.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk p) := fun x hx => by
      rw [Ideal.mem_comap, Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.mpr hx]; exact P.zero_mem
    refine hmax (P.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk p))
      ⟨Ideal.comap_isPrime _ _, hQp.trans hle, hfP⟩ (lt_of_le_of_ne hle fun e => hP0 ?_)
    rw [eq_bot_iff]
    intro y hy
    obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective y
    have : x ∈ p := by rw [e]; exact hy
    exact Ideal.mem_bot.mpr (Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem.mpr this)
  have hfm' : Ideal.Quotient.mk p f ∈ maximalIdeal (R ⧸ p) := by
    haveI := IsLocalHom.of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.mk p) Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
    exact map_nonunit _ f hfm
  have hlt : (⊥ : Ideal (R ⧸ p)) < maximalIdeal (R ⧸ p) :=
    bot_lt_iff_ne_bot.mpr fun e => hf0 (by simpa [e] using hfm')
  have h1 := Ideal.height_add_one_le_of_lt_of_isPrime hlt
  have h2 : (maximalIdeal (R ⧸ p)).height = 1 := le_antisymm hh (le_trans le_add_self h1)
  rw [← IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal_height_eq_ringKrullDim, h2]
  rfl


[UCE_SRC_CURVE_REALIZATION] Characteristic-prime DVR realization with finite residue field and a local coefficient map
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FinCrit.lean#L14-L71
private theorem fc_abs (D V : Type) [CommRing D] [IsDomain D] [IsNoetherianRing D] [IsLocalRing D]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal D) D] [Finite (ResidueField D)] [Ring.DimensionLEOne D]
    [CommRing V] [IsDomain V] [ValuationRing V] [Algebra D V] [FaithfulSMul D V] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap D V)]
    (hm : maximalIdeal D ≠ ⊥)
    (hB : ∀ x : V, ∃ c : D, c ≠ 0 ∧ ∃ b : D, algebraMap D V c * x = algebraMap D V b) :
    IsDiscreteValuationRing V ∧ IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal V) V ∧ Finite (ResidueField V) := by
  have hVn : IsNoetherianRing V := (GlobalTheory.krullAkizuki_of_birational (A := D) (B := V) hB).1
  obtain ⟨t, ht, ht0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hm
  have hle : (maximalIdeal D).map (algebraMap D V) ≤ maximalIdeal V :=
    Ideal.map_le_iff_le_comap.2 fun a ha => map_nonunit (algebraMap D V) a ha
  have h1 := GlobalTheory.finite_quotient_span_of_birational hB ht0
  have hs : Ideal.span {algebraMap D V t} ≤ (maximalIdeal D).map (algebraMap D V) :=
    (Ideal.span_singleton_le_iff_mem _).mpr (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ ht)
  have h2 : Module.Finite D (V ⧸ (maximalIdeal D).map (algebraMap D V)) :=
    Module.Finite.of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.factorₐ D hs).toLinearMap
      (Ideal.Quotient.factor_surjective hs)
  have h3 : IsHausdorff ((maximalIdeal D).map (algebraMap D V)) V :=
    IsHausdorff.of_isLocalRing _ _ (ne_top_of_le_ne_top (maximalIdeal.isMaximal V).ne_top hle)
  have h4 : Module.Finite D V := GlobalTheory.FormalIsogeny.finite_of_finite_quotient_map (maximalIdeal D)
  have hnf : ¬ IsField V := by
    rw [isField_iff_maximalIdeal_eq]
    intro h
    have := hle (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ ht)
    rw [h, Ideal.mem_bot] at this
    exact ht0 (FaithfulSMul.algebraMap_injective D V (this.trans (map_zero _).symm))
  have hval : ValuationRing V := inferInstance
  refine ⟨((IsDiscreteValuationRing.TFAE V hnf).out 1 0).mp hval,
    GlobalTheory.FormalIsogeny.isAdicComplete_maximalIdeal_of_finite D V, ?_⟩
  have : Module.Finite (ResidueField D) (ResidueField V) := inferInstance
  exact Module.finite_of_finite (ResidueField D)

private theorem fc_point (D : Type) [CommRing D] [IsDomain D] [IsNoetherianRing D] [IsLocalRing D]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal D) D] [Finite (ResidueField D)] (hd : ringKrullDim D = 1) :
    ∃ (A : Type) (_ : CommRing A) (_ : IsDomain A) (_ : IsDiscreteValuationRing A),
      IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal A) A ∧ Finite (ResidueField A) ∧
        ∃ φ : D →+* A, Function.Injective φ ∧ IsLocalHom φ := by
  have hk : Ring.KrullDimLE 1 D := by
    rw [Ring.krullDimLE_iff, hd]; exact le_rfl
  have : Ring.DimensionLEOne D :=
    ⟨fun {q} hq hq' => Ring.krullDimLE_one_iff_of_noZeroDivisors.mp hk q hq hq'⟩
  have hm : maximalIdeal D ≠ ⊥ := fun h => by
    rw [ringKrullDim_eq_zero_of_isField (isField_iff_maximalIdeal_eq.2 h)] at hd
    exact absurd hd (by decide)
  obtain ⟨V, hV, hloc⟩ := IsLocalRing.exists_factor_valuationRing (algebraMap D (FractionRing D))
  let φ : D →+* V := (algebraMap D (FractionRing D)).codRestrict V.toSubring hV
  have hφ : Function.Injective φ := fun a b h =>
    IsFractionRing.injective D (FractionRing D) (congrArg Subtype.val h)
  letI : Algebra D V := φ.toAlgebra
  have : FaithfulSMul D V := (faithfulSMul_iff_algebraMap_injective _ _).mpr hφ
  have hl : IsLocalHom (algebraMap D V) := hloc
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩ := fc_abs D V hm fun x => by
    obtain ⟨⟨b, c⟩, h⟩ := IsLocalization.surj (nonZeroDivisors D) (x : FractionRing D)
    exact ⟨c, nonZeroDivisors.coe_ne_zero c, b, Subtype.ext ((mul_comm _ _).trans h)⟩
  exact ⟨V, inferInstance, inferInstance, h1, h2, h3, φ, hφ, hl⟩

private theorem fc_finres (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Finite (ResidueField O)] [CommRing R]
    [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] : Finite (ResidueField R) :=
  Finite.of_surjective _ (resMap_bijective O R).2


[UCE_SRC_FINITE_CRITERION] Balanced-monomial contradiction, homogeneous contraction and literal finCrit conclusion
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FinCrit.lean#L73-L186
private theorem fc_sign {σ : Type} (wt : σ → ℤ) (n : σ →₀ ℕ) (hn : n ≠ 0) (hw : Finsupp.weight wt n = 0)
    (P : σ → Prop) (h0 : ∀ i, wt i = 0 → P i) (h : (∀ i, wt i < 0 → P i) ∨ ∀ i, 0 < wt i → P i) :
    ∃ i ∈ n.support, P i := by
  by_contra hc
  push Not at hc
  have hne := Finsupp.support_nonempty_iff.2 hn
  rw [Finsupp.weight_apply, Finsupp.sum] at hw
  rcases h with h | h
  · refine absurd hw (ne_of_gt (Finset.sum_pos (fun i hi => ?_) hne))
    have h1 : 0 < wt i := by
      rcases lt_trichotomy (wt i) 0 with e | e | e
      · exact absurd (h i e) (hc i hi)
      · exact absurd (h0 i e) (hc i hi)
      · exact e
    have := Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (Finsupp.mem_support_iff.1 hi)
    rw [nsmul_eq_mul]
    positivity
  · refine absurd hw (ne_of_lt (Finset.sum_neg (fun i hi => ?_) hne))
    have h1 : wt i < 0 := by
      rcases lt_trichotomy (wt i) 0 with e | e | e
      · exact e
      · exact absurd (h0 i e) (hc i hi)
      · exact absurd (h i e) (hc i hi)
    have := Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (Finsupp.mem_support_iff.1 hi)
    rw [nsmul_eq_mul]
    exact mul_neg_of_pos_of_neg (by exact_mod_cast this) h1

private theorem fc_contract {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (W : WPres O ℤ R) (ϖ : O)
    (b : W.gr 0) (hb : ((b : W.A) : R) ∈ Ideal.span {algebraMap O R ϖ}) :
    b ∈ Ideal.span {algebraMap O (W.gr 0) ϖ} := by
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := Ideal.mem_span_singleton'.1 hb
  obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := W.surj r
  obtain ⟨f, hf, hfb⟩ : ((b : W.A) : R) ∈ W.part 0 := b.2
  have hfb' : W.π f = ((b : W.A) : R) := hfb
  have h1 : ϖ • g - f ∈ RingHom.ker W.π := by
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_smul, hfb', ← hr, Algebra.smul_def, mul_comm, sub_self]
  have h2 := W.homog _ h1 0
  have h3 : wcomp O W.wt 0 f = f := by
    ext n
    rw [coeff_wcomp]
    split_ifs with h
    · rfl
    · exact (hf n h).symm
  rw [map_sub, map_smul, h3, RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_smul, hfb', sub_eq_zero] at h2
  have hp : W.π (wcomp O W.wt 0 g) ∈ W.part 0 := ⟨_, wcomp_mem O W.wt 0 g, rfl⟩
  refine Ideal.mem_span_singleton'.2 ⟨⟨⟨_, W.part_le_A hp⟩, hp⟩, Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext ?_)⟩
  rw [← h2, Algebra.smul_def, mul_comm]
  rfl

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.finCrit :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.FinCrit := by
  intro O R _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ W hz H
  classical
  haveI hB : IsCO O (W.gr 0) := UpperTheory.V.Alg.wPres_gr_zero_isCO_of_mem_maximalIdeal O ℤ R W hz
  obtain ⟨h1, u, hu, hmax⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Alg.wPres_maximalIdeal_gr_zero_eq_sup_span_monomials O ℤ R W hz
  obtain ⟨ϖ, hϖ⟩ := IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible O
  have hmO := hϖ.maximalIdeal_eq
  have hJ : (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O (W.gr 0)) = Ideal.span {algebraMap O (W.gr 0) ϖ} := by
    rw [hmO, Ideal.map_span, Set.image_singleton]
  have hrad : ∀ b ∈ u, ((b : W.A) : R) ∈ (Ideal.span {algebraMap O R ϖ}).radical := by
    intro b hb
    rw [Ideal.radical_eq_sInf, Submodule.mem_sInf]
    rintro Q ⟨hQ, hQp⟩
    by_contra hbQ
    have hbm : ((b : W.A) : R) ∈ maximalIdeal R :=
      (h1 b).1 (hmax ▸ Ideal.mem_sup_right (Ideal.subset_span hb))
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hQle, hbp, hd⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.npe_dim_one Q hbm hbQ
    haveI : IsCO O (R ⧸ p) := UpperTheory.V.Alg.co_quot O R (R ⧸ p) (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ O p)
      Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
    haveI := fc_finres O (R ⧸ p)
    obtain ⟨A, _, _, _, hc, hf, φ, hφ, hl⟩ := fc_point (R ⧸ p) hd
    letI : Algebra O A := (φ.comp (algebraMap O (R ⧸ p))).toAlgebra
    haveI : UpperTheory.V.Pr.IsCD O A :=
      { complete := hc
        isLocalHom := inferInstanceAs (IsLocalHom (φ.comp (algebraMap O (R ⧸ p))))
        fin := hf }
    let f : R →ₐ[O] A := { φ.comp (Ideal.Quotient.mk p) with commutes' := fun _ => rfl }
    have hker : ∀ x, f x = 0 ↔ x ∈ p := fun x => by
      rw [← Ideal.Quotient.eq_zero_iff_mem, ← map_eq_zero_iff φ hφ]
      rfl
    haveI := IsLocalHom.of_surjective (Ideal.Quotient.mk p) Ideal.Quotient.mk_surjective
    have hfl : IsLocalHom f := ⟨fun a ha =>
      IsLocalHom.map_nonunit (f := φ.comp (Ideal.Quotient.mk p)) a ha⟩
    obtain ⟨h0, hs⟩ := H A f hfl fun a ha => by
      rw [← f.commutes, hker]
      rw [hmO] at ha
      obtain ⟨c, rfl⟩ := Ideal.mem_span_singleton'.1 ha
      rw [map_mul]
      exact p.mul_mem_left _ (hQle (hQ (Ideal.mem_span_singleton_self _)))
    obtain ⟨n, hn, hw, hbn⟩ := hu b hb
    obtain ⟨i, hi, hi0⟩ := fc_sign W.wt n hn hw _ h0 hs
    apply hbp
    rw [hbn]
    exact Ideal.prod_mem _ hi (Ideal.pow_mem_of_mem _ ((hker _).1 hi0) _
      (Nat.pos_of_ne_zero (Finsupp.mem_support_iff.1 hi)))
  have hrad' : maximalIdeal (W.gr 0) ≤ ((maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O (W.gr 0))).radical := by
    rw [hmax]
    refine sup_le Ideal.le_radical (Ideal.span_le.2 fun b hb => ?_)
    obtain ⟨N, hN⟩ := hrad b hb
    exact ⟨N, hJ ▸ fc_contract W ϖ (b ^ N) hN⟩
  obtain ⟨N, hN⟩ := Ideal.exists_pow_le_of_le_radical_of_fg hrad' (IsNoetherian.noetherian _)
  haveI : Finite (W.gr 0 ⧸ maximalIdeal (W.gr 0)) := fc_finres O (W.gr 0)
  haveI := Ideal.finite_quotient_pow (IsNoetherian.noetherian (maximalIdeal (W.gr 0))) N
  haveI : Finite (W.gr 0 ⧸ (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O (W.gr 0))) :=
    Finite.of_surjective _ (Ideal.Quotient.factor_surjective hN)
  haveI : IsHausdorff ((maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O (W.gr 0))) (W.gr 0) :=
    IsHausdorff.of_isLocalRing _ _ (ne_top_of_le_ne_top (maximalIdeal.isMaximal (W.gr 0)).ne_top
      (le_sup_left.trans hmax.ge))
  haveI : Module.Finite O (W.gr 0) := GlobalTheory.FormalIsogeny.finite_of_finite_quotient_map (maximalIdeal O)
  let e : W.gr 0 →ₗ[O] W.part 0 :=
    { toFun := fun b => ⟨((b : W.A) : R), b.2⟩
      map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
      map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }
  exact Module.Finite.of_surjective e fun x => ⟨⟨⟨x.1, W.part_le_A x.2⟩, x.2⟩, rfl⟩


[UCE_SRC_CONSTANT_POLYNOMIALS] Finite-index characteristic polynomials, algebraic local constants and fixed diagonal values
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/ConstOfChar.lean#L9-L64
private theorem charpoly_dvd_expand {A : Type} [CommRing A] {m : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) A) (n : ℕ) :
    M.charpoly ∣ expand A n (M ^ n).charpoly := by
  have hc : Commute (scalar (Fin m) (X : A[X])) ((C : A →+* A[X]).mapMatrix M) :=
    scalar_commute _ (fun _ => Commute.all _ _) _
  have key : (expand A n : A[X] →+* A[X]).mapMatrix (charmatrix (M ^ n)) =
      charmatrix M * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n,
        (scalar (Fin m) (X : A[X])) ^ i * ((C : A →+* A[X]).mapMatrix M) ^ (n - 1 - i) := by
    rw [show charmatrix M = scalar (Fin m) (X : A[X]) - (C : A →+* A[X]).mapMatrix M from rfl,
      hc.mul_geom_sum₂, ← map_pow, ← map_pow]
    ext i j
    by_cases h : i = j <;> simp [charmatrix_apply, h]
  unfold Matrix.charpoly
  have := RingHom.map_det (expand A n : A[X] →+* A[X]) (charmatrix (M ^ n))
  rw [RingHom.coe_coe] at this
  rw [this, key, det_mul]
  exact dvd_mul_right _ _

set_option linter.overlappingInstances false in
private theorem charpoly_const {k A : Type} [Field k] [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra k A]
    {m : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) A) (M₀ N₀ : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) k) (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n)
    (h : EntIn (maximalIdeal A) (M - M₀.map (algebraMap k A)))
    (hp : (M ^ n).charpoly = (N₀.map (algebraMap k A)).charpoly) :
    M.charpoly = M₀.charpoly.map (algebraMap k A) := by
  classical
  have hmon : (expand k n N₀.charpoly).Monic := (charpoly_monic N₀).expand hn
  have hdvd : M.charpoly ∣ (expand k n N₀.charpoly).map (algebraMap k A) := by
    rw [map_expand, ← charpoly_map, ← hp]
    exact charpoly_dvd_expand M n
  have hint := isIntegral_coeff_of_dvd _ _ hmon (charpoly_monic M) hdvd
  have hres : M.map (IsLocalRing.residue A) = (M₀.map (algebraMap k A)).map (IsLocalRing.residue A) := by
    ext a b
    simp only [Matrix.map_apply]
    apply Ideal.Quotient.eq.mpr
    simpa using h a b
  have hcp := congrArg Matrix.charpoly hres
  simp only [charpoly_map] at hcp
  ext i
  rw [Polynomial.coeff_map]
  apply UpperTheory.V.Pr.constOfAlg k A _ _ (hint i).isAlgebraic
  have hi := congrArg (fun p => p.coeff i) hcp
  simp only [Polynomial.coeff_map] at hi
  exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.mp hi

set_option linter.overlappingInstances false in
private theorem diag_const {k A : Type} [Field k] [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra k A]
    {m : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) A) (M₀ : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) k)
    (h : EntIn (maximalIdeal A) (M - M₀.map (algebraMap k A)))
    (hp : M.charpoly = M₀.charpoly.map (algebraMap k A)) (a : Fin m)
    (ha : (Matrix.scalar (Fin m) (M a a) - M).det = 0) : M a a = algebraMap k A (M₀ a a) := by
  classical
  apply UpperTheory.V.Pr.constOfAlg k A
  · refine ⟨M₀.charpoly, (charpoly_monic M₀).ne_zero, ?_⟩
    rw [Polynomial.aeval_def, ← Polynomial.eval_map, ← hp, Matrix.eval_charpoly]
    exact ha
  · simpa using h a a



[UCE_SRC_CONSTANT_TRIANGULAR] Original normalized rank-three triangular alternative and complete constOfChar endpoint
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/ConstOfChar.lean#L65-L294
private theorem zmat_map {G R S : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S) (c g₁ : G)
    (r : G →* GG 3 R) (i j : Fin 3) : zmat c g₁ ((GG.map f).comp r) i j = f (zmat c g₁ r i j) := by
  simp only [zmat, Matrix.sub_apply, MonoidHom.comp_apply, GG.map_mat, Matrix.map_apply, map_sub]

private theorem normalised_map {G R S : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] [CommRing S] (f : R →+* S) {c g₁ : G}
    {r : G →* GG 3 R} (h : Normalised c g₁ r) : Normalised c g₁ ((GG.map f).comp r) := by
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · simp [GG.map_mat, h.atc]
  · rw [zmat_map, h.z12, map_zero]
  · rw [zmat_map, h.z21, map_zero]

private theorem charpoly_diagonal_character_rep {G k : Type} [Group G] [Field k] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* kˣ) (g : G) :
    (r0 η Ψ g).mat.charpoly = (X - C (Ψ g : k)) * (X - C 1) * (X - C ((Ψ g)⁻¹ : kˣ).1) := by
  simp [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, charpoly_diagonal, Fin.prod_univ_three, wt1]

private theorem corner_eq {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) :
    corner M = !![M 0 0, M 0 2; M 2 0, M 2 2] := by
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> rfl

private theorem charpoly_block {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) (h1 : M 0 1 = 0)
    (h2 : M 1 0 = 0) (h3 : M 1 2 = 0) (h4 : M 2 1 = 0) :
    M.charpoly = (corner M).charpoly * (X - C (M 1 1)) := by
  simp only [Matrix.charpoly, Matrix.det_fin_three, Matrix.det_fin_two]
  simp [corner_eq, Matrix.charmatrix_apply, h1, h2, h3, h4]
  ring

private theorem corner_mul {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (M N : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) (h1 : M 0 1 = 0)
    (h4 : M 2 1 = 0) : corner (M * N) = corner M * corner N := by
  rw [corner_eq, corner_eq, corner_eq, Matrix.mul_fin_two]
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, h1, h4]

private theorem not_typeI {G κ : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Φ : G →* κˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 κ)
    (hm : HasMult η r)
    (hp : ∀ g ∈ η.ker, (r g).mat.charpoly = (X - C (Φ g : κ)) * (X - C 1) * (X - C ((Φ g)⁻¹ : κˣ).1)) :
    ¬ TypeI η r := by
  intro h
  refine notIrrOfChar η.ker κ 3 (by norm_num) (glPart η r hm)
    ![Φ.comp η.ker.subtype, 1, (Φ.comp η.ker.subtype)⁻¹] (fun g => ?_) fun W hW => h W fun g hg => hW ⟨g, hg⟩ trivial
  rw [chPoly, Fin.prod_univ_three]
  exact hp g g.2

private theorem exists_corner_rep_of_block {G κ : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 κ)
    (hm : HasMult η r) (hB : Block η r) :
    ∃ ρ : η.ker →* GL (Fin 2) κ, ∀ g, (ρ g : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) κ) = corner (r g).mat := by
  have hmul : ∀ g h : η.ker, (r (g * h : η.ker)).mat = (r g).mat * (r h).mat := by
    intro g h
    have hr : (r g).right = 1 := by rw [hm]; exact g.2
    rw [Subgroup.coe_mul, map_mul]
    show (((r g * r h).left : GL (Fin 3) κ) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) =
      ((r g).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) κ) * (r h).left
    rw [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, hr, map_one, MulAut.one_apply, Units.val_mul]
  let μ : η.ker →* Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) κ :=
    { toFun := fun g => corner (r g).mat
      map_one' := by
        rw [OneMemClass.coe_one, map_one, corner_eq]
        ext i j
        fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [GG.mat]
      map_mul' := fun g h => by
        show corner (r (g * h : η.ker)).mat = corner (r g).mat * corner (r h).mat
        rw [hmul g h]
        exact corner_mul _ _ (hB g g.2 0 1 ⟨0, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩) (hB g g.2 2 1 ⟨-1, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩) }
  exact ⟨μ.toHomUnits, fun g => rfl⟩

private theorem not_typeII {G κ : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Φ : G →* κˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 κ)
    (hm : HasMult η r)
    (hp : ∀ g ∈ η.ker, (r g).mat.charpoly = (X - C (Φ g : κ)) * (X - C 1) * (X - C ((Φ g)⁻¹ : κˣ).1))
    (hB : Block η r) (h11 : ∀ g ∈ η.ker, (r g).mat 1 1 = 1) : ¬ TypeII η r := by
  rintro ⟨-, hirr⟩
  obtain ⟨ρ, hρ⟩ := exists_corner_rep_of_block η r hm hB
  let χ : Fin 2 → η.ker →* κˣ := ![Φ.comp η.ker.subtype, (Φ.comp η.ker.subtype)⁻¹]
  apply UpperTheory.V.Pr.notIrrOfChar η.ker κ 2 le_rfl ρ χ
  · intro g
    have hcp := charpoly_block (r g).mat (hB g g.2 0 1 ⟨0, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩)
      (hB g g.2 1 0 ⟨-1, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩) (hB g g.2 1 2 ⟨0, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩)
      (hB g g.2 2 1 ⟨-1, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩)
    rw [hp g g.2, h11 g g.2] at hcp
    have hc2 : (corner (r g).mat).charpoly = (X - C (Φ g : κ)) * (X - C ((Φ g)⁻¹ : κˣ).1) := by
      apply mul_right_cancel₀ (Polynomial.X_sub_C_ne_zero (1 : κ))
      rw [← hcp]
      ring
    rw [show UpperTheory.V.Pr.gm ρ g = corner (r g).mat from hρ g, hc2]
    simp [chPoly, Fin.prod_univ_two, χ]
  · intro W hW
    exact hirr W (fun g hg v hv => by simpa [hρ] using hW ⟨g, hg⟩ trivial v hv)

private theorem mat_mul_eq_of_mat_eq_one {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (r : G →* GG 3 R) (c g : G)
    (h : (r c).mat = 1) : (r (g * c)).mat = (r g).mat := by
  have hc : (r c).left = 1 := Units.ext h
  rw [map_mul]
  simp [GG.mat, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, hc]

private theorem mat_pow_of_mult_one {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R)
    (hm : HasMult η r) (g : G) (hg : η g = 1) (n : ℕ) : (r (g ^ n)).mat = (r g).mat ^ n := by
  induction n with
  | zero => simp [GG.mat]
  | succ n ih =>
    rw [pow_succ, map_mul, pow_succ, ← ih]
    have hr : (r (g ^ n)).right = 1 := by rw [hm, map_pow, hg, one_pow]
    show (((r (g ^ n) * r g).left : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) =
      ((r (g ^ n)).left : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) * (r g).left
    rw [SemidirectProduct.mul_left, hr, map_one, MulAut.one_apply, Units.val_mul]

private theorem det_up_lemma_3 {A : Type} [CommRing A] (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A)
    (hM : ∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → M a b = 0) (a : Fin 3) : (scalar (Fin 3) (M a a) - M).det = 0 := by
  have h10 := hM 1 0 (by simp [wt, wt1])
  have h20 := hM 2 0 (by simp [wt, wt1])
  have h21 := hM 2 1 (by simp [wt, wt1])
  rw [det_fin_three]
  fin_cases a <;> simp [h10, h20, h21]

private theorem det_scalar_diag_sub_triangular_eq_zero {A : Type} [CommRing A] (M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A)
    (hM : ∀ a b, 0 < wt a b → M a b = 0) (a : Fin 3) : (scalar (Fin 3) (M a a) - M).det = 0 := by
  have h01 := hM 0 1 (by simp [wt, wt1])
  have h02 := hM 0 2 (by simp [wt, wt1])
  have h12 := hM 1 2 (by simp [wt, wt1])
  rw [det_fin_three]
  fin_cases a <;> simp [h01, h02, h12]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.constOfChar :
    UpperTheory.V.Pr.ConstOfChar := by
  intro G k A _ _ _ _ _ _ _ h2 η Ψ c g₁ hMk r hm hent hN U hU hchar
  have hcp : ∀ g, η g = 1 → (r g).mat.charpoly = ((r0 η Ψ g).mat).charpoly.map (algebraMap k A) := by
    intro g hg
    obtain ⟨n, hn, -, hgn⟩ := Subgroup.exists_pow_mem_of_index_ne_zero (Subgroup.FiniteIndex.index_ne_zero (H := U)) g
    apply charpoly_const (r g).mat (r0 η Ψ g).mat (r0 η Ψ (g ^ n)).mat n hn (hent g)
    rw [← mat_pow_of_mult_one η r hm g hg n]
    exact hchar _ hgn (by rw [map_pow, hg, one_pow])
  have hdiagK : ∀ g, η g = 1 → ∀ a, ((scalar (Fin 3) ((r g).mat a a)) - (r g).mat).det = 0 →
      (r g).mat a a = algebraMap k A ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a a) :=
    fun g hg a ha => diag_const _ _ (hent g) (hcp g hg) a ha
  have hzu : IsUnit (zeta c g₁ r) := by
    have hsub : zeta c g₁ r - algebraMap k A (zeta c g₁ (r0 η Ψ)) ∈ maximalIdeal A := by
      have e1 := hent g₁ 0 0
      have e2 := hent (gstar c g₁) 0 0
      have he : zeta c g₁ r - algebraMap k A (zeta c g₁ (r0 η Ψ)) =
          ((r g₁).mat - (r0 η Ψ g₁).mat.map (algebraMap k A)) 0 0 -
            ((r (gstar c g₁)).mat - (r0 η Ψ (gstar c g₁)).mat.map (algebraMap k A)) 0 0 := by
        simp [zeta, zmat]
        ring
      rw [he]
      exact Ideal.sub_mem _ e1 e2
    have hu : IsUnit (algebraMap k A (zeta c g₁ (r0 η Ψ))) := (zeta_reducible_rep_eq_sub_inv_and_isUnit η Ψ c g₁ hMk).2.map _
    by_contra hnu
    have hm1 := Ideal.sub_mem _ ((IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).2 hnu) hsub
    rw [sub_sub_cancel] at hm1
    exact (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).1 hm1 hu
  let f : A →+* FractionRing A := algebraMap A (FractionRing A)
  have hf : Function.Injective f := IsFractionRing.injective A (FractionRing A)
  let r' : G →* GG 3 (FractionRing A) := (GG.map f).comp r
  have hm' : HasMult η r' := fun g => hm g
  have hN' : Normalised c g₁ r' := normalised_map f hN
  have hmat' : ∀ g, (r' g).mat = (r g).mat.map f := fun g => GG.map_mat f (r g)
  have hz' : zeta c g₁ r' ≠ 0 := by
    show zmat c g₁ r' 0 0 ≠ 0
    rw [zmat_map]
    intro h0
    exact hzu.ne_zero (hf (h0.trans (map_zero f).symm))
  have h2A : (2 : A) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h0
    apply h2
    apply (algebraMap k A).injective
    rw [map_ofNat, map_zero]
    exact h0
  have h2' : (2 : FractionRing A) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h0
    apply h2A
    apply hf
    rw [map_ofNat, map_zero]
    exact h0
  let Φ : G →* (FractionRing A)ˣ := (Units.map (f.comp (algebraMap k A)).toMonoidHom).comp Ψ
  have hp' : ∀ g ∈ η.ker, (r' g).mat.charpoly =
      (X - C (Φ g : FractionRing A)) * (X - C 1) * (X - C ((Φ g)⁻¹ : (FractionRing A)ˣ).1) := by
    intro g hg
    rw [hmat', Matrix.charpoly_map, hcp g hg, charpoly_diagonal_character_rep, Polynomial.map_map]
    simp [Φ, Polynomial.map_mul, Polynomial.map_sub]
  have hUpT : Upper η r' → Upper η r := by
    intro h g hg a b hab
    apply hf
    rw [map_zero]
    have := h g hg a b hab
    rw [hmat'] at this
    exact this
  have hLoT : Lower η r' → Lower η r := by
    intro h g hg a b hab
    apply hf
    rw [map_zero]
    have := h g hg a b hab
    rw [hmat'] at this
    exact this
  have hUL : Upper η r ∨ Lower η r := by
    rcases shape_cases η r' c g₁ h2' hm' hMk.ηc hMk.ηg hN' hz' with hI | hUp' | hLo' | hII
    · exact absurd hI (not_typeI η Φ r' hm' hp')
    · exact Or.inl (hUpT hUp')
    · exact Or.inr (hLoT hLo')
    · exfalso
      apply not_typeII η Φ r' hm' hp' hII.1 _ hII
      intro g hg
      have hB : ∀ a b, Odd (wt a b) → (r g).mat a b = 0 := by
        intro a b hab
        apply hf
        rw [map_zero]
        have := hII.1 g hg a b hab
        rw [hmat'] at this
        exact this
      have hcpb := charpoly_block (r g).mat (hB 0 1 ⟨0, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩) (hB 1 0 ⟨-1, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩)
        (hB 1 2 ⟨0, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩) (hB 2 1 ⟨-1, by simp [wt, wt1]⟩)
      have hdet : (scalar (Fin 3) ((r g).mat 1 1) - (r g).mat).det = 0 := by
        rw [← Matrix.eval_charpoly, hcpb]
        simp
      rw [hmat', Matrix.map_apply, hdiagK g hg 1 hdet]
      simp [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, wt1]
  have hdk : ∀ g, η g = 1 → ∀ a, (r g).mat a a = algebraMap k A ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a a) := by
    intro g hg a
    apply hdiagK g hg a
    rcases hUL with hu | hl
    · exact det_up_lemma_3 _ (fun a b hab => hu g hg a b hab) a
    · exact det_scalar_diag_sub_triangular_eq_zero _ (fun a b hab => hl g hg a b hab) a
  refine ⟨hUL, fun g a => ?_⟩
  by_cases hg : η g = 1
  · exact hdk g hg a
  · have hneg : η g = -1 := (Int.units_eq_one_or (η g)).resolve_left hg
    have hgc : η (g * c) = 1 := by
      rw [map_mul, hneg, hMk.ηc]
      rfl
    have hrc : (r0 η Ψ c).mat = 1 := by
      simp [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, hMk.Ψc]
    rw [← mat_mul_eq_of_mat_eq_one r c g hN.atc, ← mat_mul_eq_of_mat_eq_one (r0 η Ψ) c g hrc]
    exact hdk _ hgc a


[UCE_SRC_FINITE_END] Actual weighted entry presentation and full finA_of_end consumer
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/GfResZpow.lean#L43-L205
private theorem gf_congOne {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]
    (t : Rˣ) (ht : res O R t = 1) : CongOne (maximalIdeal R) (tor R t) := by
  intro a b
  rw [Matrix.sub_apply, tor_val]
  by_cases h : a = b
  · subst h
    rw [Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq, Matrix.one_apply_eq, ← res_eq_zero_iff O, map_sub, gf_res_zpow t ht, map_one,
      sub_self]
  · rw [Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ h, Matrix.one_apply_ne h, sub_self]
    exact zero_mem _

private theorem gf_bar (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ) (g : Γ) (a b : Fin 3) :
    (r0 η (barChar O Ψ) g).mat a b = residue O ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) := by
  simp only [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_apply]
  split_ifs
  · exact (congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map (residue O).toMonoidHom) (Ψ g) (wt1 a))).symm
  · simp

omit [IsLocalRing O] in
private theorem gf_off (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ) (g : Γ) (a b : Fin 3) (h : wt a b ≠ 0) :
    (r0 η Ψ g).mat a b = 0 := by
  simp only [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val]
  exact Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ (by rintro rfl; exact h (sub_self _))

private theorem gf_pres [IsDomain O] [IsNoetherianRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
    {H : Type} [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ}
    {hι : Continuous ι} {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {c g₁ : Γ}
    (hI : ConjStable U I) (S : SRing N hι U I c g₁) :
    ∃ W : WPres O ℤ S.R, (∀ i, W.z i ∈ maximalIdeal S.R) ∧
      (∀ i, ∃ g a b, W.z i = (S.r.hom g).mat a b - algebraMap O _ ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) ∧ W.wt i = wt a b) ∧
      ∀ g a b, (S.r.hom g).mat a b ∈ W.part (wt a b) := by
  have hm : ∀ g a b, (S.r.hom g).mat a b - algebraMap O _ ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) ∈ maximalIdeal S.R := by
    intro g a b
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero]
    exact (congrArg (fun x => GG.mat x a b) (S.r.red g)).trans (gf_bar η Ψ g a b)
  obtain ⟨W, u, hu, he⟩ := coord_grading O ℤ S.R (Γ × Fin 3 × Fin 3)
    (fun s => (S.r.hom s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2 - algebraMap O _ ((r0 η Ψ s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2))
    (fun s => wt s.2.1 s.2.2) (fun s => hm _ _ _)
    (fun R' _ _ _ f f' h => by
      have h1 : S.r.map f = S.r.map f' := by
        refine GLift.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl)
        ext a b
        have := h (g, a, b)
        simp only [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_left_inj] at this
        exact this
      exact ((S.rep R').2 _ ((S.rep R').1 f')).unique h1 rfl)
    (fun R' _ _ _ f t ht => by
      obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩ := (S.rep R').1 f
      obtain ⟨f', hf', -⟩ := (S.rep R').2 (gf_torLift (S.r.map f) (t (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1)) (ht _))
        ⟨(conjBy_tor _ _).2.2.1 c g₁ h1, fun g hg => by
          show MulAut.conj _ ((S.r.map f).hom g) = 1
          rw [h2 g hg, map_one],
          hI R' ((S.r.map f).comap ι hι) _ _ (gf_congOne _ (ht _)) rfl h3⟩
      have htj : ∀ j : ℤ, t (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1) ^ j = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd j) := fun j => by
        rw [← map_zpow, ← ofAdd_zsmul, smul_eq_mul, mul_one]
      refine ⟨f', fun ⟨g, a, b⟩ => ?_⟩
      dsimp only
      have e := (conjBy_tor (S.r.map f).hom (t (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1))).1 g a b
      have e' : f' ((S.r.hom g).mat a b) = _ := (congrArg (fun x => (x.hom g).mat a b) hf').trans e
      rw [map_sub, map_sub f, AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes, e', htj, mul_sub]
      congr 1
      by_cases hab : wt a b = 0
      · simp [hab]
      · simp [gf_off η Ψ g a b hab])
  refine ⟨W, fun i => (hu i).1 ▸ hm _ _ _, fun i => ⟨(u i).1, (u i).2.1, (u i).2.2, hu i⟩, fun g a b => ?_⟩
  have h1 := he (g, a, b)
  have h2 : algebraMap O S.R ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) ∈ W.part (wt a b) := by
    by_cases hab : wt a b = 0
    · rw [hab, Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one]
      exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem : (1 : S.R) ∈ W.part 0)
    · rw [gf_off η Ψ g a b hab, map_zero]
      exact zero_mem _
  simpa only [sub_add_cancel] using add_mem h1 h2

omit [IsLocalRing O] in
private theorem gf_fin [IsNoetherianRing O] {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (W : WPres O ℤ R)
    (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (h : ∀ g a b, (r g).mat a b ∈ W.part (wt a b)) (hf : Module.Finite O (W.part 0)) :
    Module.Finite O (weightZeroSubalgebra O r) := by
  have h1 : ∀ L : List (Γ × Fin 3 × Fin 3),
      (L.map fun s => (r s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2).prod ∈ W.part (L.map fun s => wt s.2.1 s.2.2).sum := by
    intro L
    induction L with
    | nil => exact (SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem : (1 : R) ∈ W.part 0)
    | cons s L ih =>
      simp only [List.map_cons, List.prod_cons, List.sum_cons]
      exact SetLike.GradedMul.mul_mem (h _ _ _) ih
  let P : Subalgebra O R := (W.part 0).toSubalgebra (SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem : (1 : R) ∈ W.part 0)
    fun x y hx hy => by simpa using (SetLike.GradedMul.mul_mem hx hy : x * y ∈ W.part (0 + 0))
  have h2 : weightZeroSubalgebra O r ≤ P := Algebra.adjoin_le (by
    rintro x ⟨L, hL, rfl⟩
    have := h1 L
    rw [hL] at this
    exact this)
  have h3 : (weightZeroSubalgebra O r).toSubmodule ≤ W.part 0 := h2
  exact Module.Finite.of_injective (Submodule.inclusion h3) (Submodule.inclusion_injective h3)

private theorem diag_eq_and_triangular_of_charpoly_eq [Finite (ResidueField O)] (h2 : IsUnit (2 : O)) {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsDomain A]
    [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra O A] (h0 : ∀ a ∈ maximalIdeal O, algebraMap O A a = 0) {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ}
    {c g₁ : Γ} (hM : Marks η Ψ c g₁) (r : Γ →* GG 3 A) (hr : UnitaryThreeInertia.Red η Ψ r) (hN : Normalised c g₁ r)
    (U : Subgroup Γ) (hU : U.FiniteIndex)
    (hc : ∀ g ∈ U, η g = 1 → (r g).mat.charpoly = (((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap O A)).charpoly) :
    (∀ g a, (r g).mat a a = algebraMap O A ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a a)) ∧
      ((∀ g a b, wt a b < 0 → (r g).mat a b = 0) ∨ ∀ g a b, 0 < wt a b → (r g).mat a b = 0) := by
  letI : Algebra (ResidueField O) A := (Ideal.Quotient.lift (maximalIdeal O) (algebraMap O A) h0).toAlgebra
  have e : ∀ g a b, algebraMap (ResidueField O) A ((r0 η (barChar O Ψ) g).mat a b) =
      algebraMap O A ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) := fun g a b => by
    rw [gf_bar]
    rfl
  have e' : ∀ g, ((r0 η (barChar O Ψ) g).mat).map (algebraMap (ResidueField O) A) =
      ((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap O A) := fun g => Matrix.ext (e g)
  have h2' : (2 : ResidueField O) ≠ 0 := by
    have := (h2.map (residue O)).ne_zero
    rwa [map_ofNat] at this
  have hM' : Marks η (barChar O Ψ) c g₁ := by
    refine ⟨hM.csq, hM.ηc, ?_, hM.ηg, ?_⟩
    · show Units.map _ (Ψ c) = 1
      rw [hM.Ψc, map_one]
    · have := hM.Ψg.map (residue O)
      rw [map_sub, map_pow, map_one] at this
      exact this
  obtain ⟨hT, hd⟩ := constOfChar Γ (ResidueField O) A h2' η (barChar O Ψ) c g₁ hM' r hr.mult
    (fun g => e' g ▸ hr.red g) hN U hU fun g hg hη => e' g ▸ hc g hg hη
  have key : ∀ g, ∃ g' ∈ η.ker, (r g).mat = (r g').mat := by
    intro g
    rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η g) with hg | hg
    · exact ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩
    · have hl : (r c).left = 1 := Units.ext hN.atc
      refine ⟨g * c, by rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, map_mul, hg, hM.ηc]; rfl, ?_⟩
      rw [map_mul, GG.mat, GG.mat, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, hl, map_one, mul_one]
  refine ⟨fun g a => (hd g a).trans (e g a a), hT.imp (fun h g a b hab => ?_) fun h g a b hab => ?_⟩ <;>
  · obtain ⟨g', hg', e⟩ := key g
    rw [e]
    exact h g' hg' a b hab

end Gen

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.finA_of_end :
    Loc.LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω), D.Marked g₁ →
    ∀ (B : ValuationSubring Ω), (∀ x, x ∈ B ↔ ‖D.j x‖ ≤ 1) → FinEnd l D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B → FinA D g₁ := by
  intro hP Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D g₁ hM B hB ⟨V, hV, hE⟩ U S
  obtain ⟨W, hz, hw, hp⟩ := gf_pres (Loc.locGood hP D.loc U).conj S
  refine gf_fin W _ hp (finCrit c.O S.R W hz fun A _ _ hA f hf h0 => ?_)
  haveI := hA.toIsDomain
  haveI := hA.toIsDiscreteValuationRing
  haveI := hA.complete
  have hO := ordPt_of_gd hP D g₁ hM U S B hB A f hf
  obtain ⟨hd, hT⟩ := diag_eq_and_triangular_of_charpoly_eq (dat_facts D).2.2.2.2.2.2.1 h0 hM _ hO.red hO.norm V hV (hE A h0 _ hO)
  refine ⟨fun i hi => ?_, hT.imp (fun h i hi => ?_) fun h i hi => ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨g, a, b, e1, e2⟩ := hw i
    obtain rfl : a = b := by
      rw [e2] at hi
      revert hi
      fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> decide
    rw [e1, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero]
    exact hd g a
  · obtain ⟨g, a, b, e1, e2⟩ := hw i
    rw [e2] at hi
    rw [e1, gf_off _ _ g a b hi.ne, map_zero, sub_zero]
    exact h g a b hi
  · obtain ⟨g, a, b, e1, e2⟩ := hw i
    rw [e2] at hi
    rw [e1, gf_off _ _ g a b hi.ne', map_zero, sub_zero]
    exact h g a b hi


[UCE_SRC_ENTRY_ZERO] Actual entry presentation, weight-zero closedness and graded-entry algebra equality
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/WeightZeroAlgebraLeEntryAlgebra.lean#L152-L275
private theorem exists_pres {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] {Γ H : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H]
    {η : Γ →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ} {N : Set Γ} {ι : H →* Γ} {hι : Continuous ι}
    {U : Univ (GCond.noCondition O (η.comp ι) (Ψ.comp ι))} {I : Ideal U.R} {cc g₁ : Γ} (S : SRing N hι U I cc g₁)
    (hL : ConjStable U I) :
    ∃ W : WPres O ℤ S.R, (∀ i, W.z i ∈ maximalIdeal S.R) ∧
      (∀ i, W.z i ∈ Submodule.span O (entMon S.r.hom (W.wt i))) ∧
      ∀ g a b, (S.r.hom g).mat a b ∈ W.part (wt a b) := by
  have hm : ∀ s : Γ × Fin 3 × Fin 3, (S.r.hom s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2 -
      algebraMap O S.R ((r0 η Ψ s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2) ∈ maximalIdeal S.R := fun ⟨g, a, b⟩ => by
    have h1 := congrArg (fun x : GG 3 (ResidueField O) => x.mat a b) ((S.r.red g).trans (map_reducible_three_dim η Ψ g).symm)
    rw [← res_eq_zero_iff O, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_eq_zero]
    exact h1
  obtain ⟨W, u, hu, he⟩ := coord_grading O ℤ S.R (Γ × Fin 3 × Fin 3)
    (fun s => (S.r.hom s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2 - algebraMap O S.R ((r0 η Ψ s.1).mat s.2.1 s.2.2))
    (fun s => wt s.2.1 s.2.2) hm
    (fun R' _ _ _ f f' h => by
      have h1 : S.r.map f = S.r.map f' := by
        refine GLift.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext ?_) rfl)
        ext a b
        have := h (g, a, b)
        simp only [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_left_inj] at this
        exact this
      exact ((S.rep R').2 _ ((S.rep R').1 f')).unique h1 rfl)
    (fun R' _ _ _ f t ht => by
      obtain ⟨hn, hk, hI⟩ := (S.rep R').1 f
      obtain ⟨f', hf', -⟩ := (S.rep R').2 (torLift (S.r.map f) (t (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1)) (ht _))
        ⟨(conjBy_tor _ _).2.2.1 _ _ hn, fun g hg => by
          show MulAut.conj _ ((S.r.map f).hom g) = 1
          rw [hk g hg, map_one], hL R' ((S.r.map f).comap ι hι)
            ((torLift (S.r.map f) (t (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1)) (ht _)).comap ι hι) _ (congOne_tor _ (ht _))
            (MonoidHom.ext fun _ => rfl) hI⟩
      have htj : ∀ j : ℤ, t (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1) ^ j = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd j) := fun j => by
        rw [← map_zpow, ← ofAdd_zsmul, smul_eq_mul, mul_one]
      refine ⟨f', fun ⟨g, a, b⟩ => ?_⟩
      dsimp only
      rw [map_sub, map_sub f, AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes, mul_sub, ← htj (wt a b)]
      congr 1
      · exact (congrArg (fun x : GLift O η Ψ R' => (x.hom g).mat a b) hf').trans
          ((conjBy_tor (S.r.map f).hom _).1 g a b)
      · by_cases hab : a = b
        · simp [hab, wt]
        · simp [diagonal_rep_off_diagonal_zero η Ψ g hab])
  refine ⟨W, fun i => (hu i).1 ▸ hm _, fun i => ?_, fun g a b => ?_⟩
  · rw [(hu i).1, (hu i).2]
    obtain ⟨g, a, b⟩ := u i
    refine sub_mem (Submodule.subset_span ⟨[(g, a, b)], by simp, by simp⟩) ?_
    by_cases hab : a = b
    · subst hab
      rw [Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one]
      exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨[], by simp [wt], by simp⟩)
    · rw [diagonal_rep_off_diagonal_zero η Ψ g hab, map_zero]
      exact zero_mem _
  · have h1 := he (g, a, b)
    have h2 : algebraMap O S.R ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a b) ∈ W.part (wt a b) := by
      by_cases hab : a = b
      · rw [hab, wt, sub_self, Algebra.algebraMap_eq_smul_one]
        exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (SetLike.GradedOne.one_mem : (1 : S.R) ∈ W.part 0)
      · rw [diagonal_rep_off_diagonal_zero η Ψ g hab, map_zero]
        exact zero_mem _
    simpa only [sub_add_cancel] using add_mem h1 h2

private theorem span_le_ent {O Γ R : Type} [CommRing O] [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (j : ℤ) :
    Submodule.span O (entMon r j) ≤ Subalgebra.toSubmodule (entAlg O r) := by
  refine Submodule.span_le.2 ?_
  rintro _ ⟨L, -, rfl⟩
  refine Subalgebra.list_prod_mem (entAlg O r) fun y hy => ?_
  obtain ⟨s, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hy
  exact Algebra.subset_adjoin ⟨_, _, _, rfl⟩

private theorem weight_zero_algebra_le_entry_algebra {O Γ R : Type} [CommRing O] [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) :
    weightZeroSubalgebra O r ≤ entAlg O r :=
  Algebra.adjoin_le fun _ hx => span_le_ent r 0 (Submodule.subset_span hx)

private theorem part_zero_le {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O Γ R : Type} [CommRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O]
    [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O] [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (J : Ideal R) [IsHausdorff J R] (hl : (l : R) ∈ J)
    (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) (W : WPres O ℤ R) (hz : ∀ i, W.z i ∈ J)
    (hs : ∀ i, W.z i ∈ Submodule.span O (entMon r (W.wt i))) (hfin : Module.Finite O (weightZeroSubalgebra O r)) :
    ∀ x ∈ W.part 0, x ∈ weightZeroSubalgebra O r := by
  rintro _ ⟨F, hF, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨n, f, hf⟩ := Module.Finite.exists_fin' O (weightZeroSubalgebra O r)
  obtain ⟨k, g, hg⟩ := Module.Finite.exists_fin' ℤ_[l] (Fin n → O)
  let φ : (Fin k → ℤ_[l]) →+ R :=
    ((weightZeroSubalgebra O r).val.toLinearMap.toAddMonoidHom.comp f.toAddMonoidHom).comp g.toAddMonoidHom
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := mem_range_of_approx J hl φ (W.π F) fun m => by
    obtain ⟨y, hy, hy'⟩ := Mop.approx W.wt W.π J (fun j => Submodule.span O (entMon r j))
      (Submodule.subset_span ⟨[], by simp, by simp⟩) (fun i j a ha b hb => by
        have := Submodule.mul_mem_mul ha hb
        rw [Submodule.span_mul_span] at this
        refine Submodule.span_mono ?_ this
        rintro _ ⟨_, ⟨L, hL, rfl⟩, _, ⟨L', hL', rfl⟩, rfl⟩
        exact ⟨L ++ L', by simp [hL, hL'], by simp⟩) hz hs 0 F hF m
    obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := (hf.comp hg) ⟨y, Algebra.span_le_adjoin _ _ hy⟩
    exact ⟨v, by rwa [show φ v = y from congrArg Subtype.val hv]⟩
  show W.π F ∈ weightZeroSubalgebra O r
  rw [← hv]
  exact (f (g v)).2

private theorem A_le {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (W : WPres O ℤ R) (T : Subalgebra O R)
    (hz : ∀ i, W.z i ∈ T) (h0 : ∀ x ∈ W.part 0, x ∈ T) : W.A ≤ T := by
  have hm : ∀ b : W.σ →₀ ℕ, W.π (MvPowerSeries.monomial b 1) ∈ T := fun b => by
    induction b using Finsupp.induction_linear with
    | zero => simpa using one_mem _
    | add a b ha hb =>
      have : (MvPowerSeries.monomial (a + b) 1 : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) =
          MvPowerSeries.monomial a 1 * MvPowerSeries.monomial b 1 := by
        rw [MvPowerSeries.monomial_mul_monomial, one_mul]
      rw [this, map_mul]
      exact mul_mem ha hb
    | single i k =>
      rw [← MvPowerSeries.X_pow_eq, map_pow]
      exact pow_mem (hz i) k
  have hle : ⨆ j, W.part j ≤ Subalgebra.toSubmodule T := by
    refine iSup_le fun j => ?_
    rintro _ ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨μ, -, hd⟩ := part_span O R ℤ W {n | Finsupp.weight W.wt n = j} j fun _ h => h
    obtain ⟨b, hb, rfl⟩ := hd f hf
    show W.π _ ∈ T
    rw [map_sum]
    exact sum_mem fun m _ => by
      rw [map_mul]
      exact mul_mem (h0 _ ⟨_, hb m, rfl⟩) (hm m)
  exact fun x hx => hle hx



[UCE_SRC_FINITE_COEFFICIENTS] Finite coefficient DVR inside the supplied algebraic closure
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/WeightZeroAlgebraLeEntryAlgebra.lean#L17-L66
private theorem int_iff_norm {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (x : PadicAlgCl l) : IsIntegral ℤ_[l] x ↔ ‖x‖ ≤ 1 := by
  have hi : IsIntegral ℚ_[l] x := Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral x
  have h0 : ‖x‖ = spectralValue (minpoly ℚ_[l] x) := rfl
  rw [h0, spectralValue_le_one_iff (minpoly.monic hi)]
  constructor
  · intro h n
    rw [minpoly.isIntegrallyClosed_eq_field_fractions' ℚ_[l] h, Polynomial.coeff_map]
    exact PadicInt.norm_le_one _
  · intro h
    have hl' : minpoly ℚ_[l] x ∈ Polynomial.lifts (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l]) :=
      (Polynomial.lifts_iff_coeff_lifts _).mpr fun n => ⟨⟨_, h n⟩, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨q, hq, -, hqm⟩ := Polynomial.lifts_and_degree_eq_and_monic hl' (minpoly.monic hi)
    exact ⟨q, hqm, by
      rw [← Polynomial.aeval_def, ← Polynomial.aeval_map_algebraMap ℚ_[l], hq, minpoly.aeval]⟩

private theorem exists_dvr {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O]
    [Algebra O (PadicAlgCl l)] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O (PadicAlgCl l)] {σ : Type} [Finite σ] (b : σ → PadicAlgCl l)
    (hb : ∀ i, ‖b i‖ ≤ 1) :
    ∃ (A : Subalgebra O (PadicAlgCl l)), IsDiscreteValuationRing A ∧ Module.Finite O A ∧ ∀ i, b i ∈ A := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := Module.Finite.fg_top (R := ℤ_[l]) (M := O)
  let S : Set (PadicAlgCl l) := Set.range b ∪ algebraMap O _ '' s
  haveI : Finite S := ((Set.finite_range b).union (s.finite_toSet.image _)).to_subtype
  let K : IntermediateField ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l) := IntermediateField.adjoin ℚ_[l] S
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ℚ_[l] K :=
    IntermediateField.finiteDimensional_adjoin fun x _ => Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral x
  obtain ⟨h1, h2, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_intClosure l K
  let f : K →ₐ[ℤ_[l]] PadicAlgCl l := K.val.restrictScalars ℤ_[l]
  have hf : Function.Injective f := Subtype.val_injective
  let e := (integralClosure ℤ_[l] K).equivMapOfInjective f hf
  let B := (integralClosure ℤ_[l] K).map f
  have hB : ∀ x ∈ S, IsIntegral ℤ_[l] x → x ∈ B := fun x hx hi =>
    ⟨⟨x, IntermediateField.subset_adjoin _ _ hx⟩, (isIntegral_algHom_iff f hf).1 hi, rfl⟩
  have hO : ∀ a : O, algebraMap O (PadicAlgCl l) a ∈ B := by
    intro a
    have ha : a ∈ Submodule.span ℤ_[l] (s : Set O) := hs ▸ Submodule.mem_top
    induction ha using Submodule.span_induction with
    | mem x hx =>
      exact hB _ (Or.inr ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩)
        ((Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral x).map (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℤ_[l] O (PadicAlgCl l)))
    | zero => simpa using B.zero_mem
    | add x y _ _ hx hy => rw [map_add]; exact B.add_mem hx hy
    | smul c x _ hx => rw [Algebra.smul_def, map_mul, ← IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply]; exact B.mul_mem (B.algebraMap_mem c) hx
  let A : Subalgebra O (PadicAlgCl l) := { B.toSubsemiring with algebraMap_mem' := hO }
  have d1 : IsDiscreteValuationRing B := IsDiscreteValuationRing.RingEquivClass.isDiscreteValuationRing e
  have d2 : Module.Finite ℤ_[l] B := Module.Finite.equiv e.toLinearEquiv
  refine ⟨A, d1, ?_, fun i => hB _ (Or.inl ⟨i, rfl⟩) ((int_iff_norm _).2 (hb i))⟩
  haveI : Module.Finite ℤ_[l] A := d2
  exact Module.Finite.of_restrictScalars_finite ℤ_[l] O A



[UCE_SRC_POINTS_FINITE] Strict torus rescaling and exact extension of arbitrary graded algebra maps to the complete ring
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/PointsFinite.lean#L91-L383
private theorem pf_unit {O X σ : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] (wt : σ → X) {U : MvPowerSeries σ O}
    (hU : U ∈ wtPart O wt 0) (hu : IsUnit U) : ∃ G ∈ wtPart O wt 0, U * G = 1 := by
  classical
  obtain ⟨G, hG⟩ := hu.exists_right_inv
  refine ⟨wcomp O wt 0 G, wcomp_mem O wt 0 G, ?_⟩
  ext n
  by_cases hn : Finsupp.weight wt n = 0
  · rw [← hG, coeff_mul, coeff_mul]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun q hq => ?_
    by_cases h1 : Finsupp.weight wt q.1 = 0
    · rw [coeff_wcomp, if_pos]
      rwa [← Finset.HasAntidiagonal.mem_antidiagonal.1 hq, map_add, h1, zero_add] at hn
    · rw [hU _ h1, zero_mul, zero_mul]
  · rw [one_mem_wtPart O wt n hn]
    exact mul_mem_wtPart O wt hU (wcomp_mem O wt 0 G) n (by rwa [add_zero])

private theorem pf_monomial {O σ S : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing S] [Algebra O S]
    (f : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] S) (m : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    f (monomial m 1) = m.prod fun s k => f (MvPowerSeries.X s) ^ k := by
  rw [← MvPolynomial.coe_monomial, GlobalTheory.algHom_coe_mvPolynomial, MvPolynomial.aeval_monomial, map_one, one_mul]

private theorem pf_prod {X σ S : Type} [AddCommGroup X] [CommRing S] (wt : σ → X)
    (t : Multiplicative X →* Sˣ) (x : σ → S) (d : σ →₀ ℕ) :
    (d.prod fun i k => ((t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (wt i)) : S) * x i) ^ k) =
      (t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (Finsupp.weight wt d)) : S) * d.prod fun i k => x i ^ k := by
  induction d using Finsupp.induction_linear with
  | zero => simp
  | add f g hf hg =>
    rw [Finsupp.prod_add_index' (by simp) (fun _ _ _ => pow_add _ _ _),
      Finsupp.prod_add_index' (by simp) (fun _ _ _ => pow_add _ _ _), hf, hg, map_add, ofAdd_add,
      map_mul, Units.val_mul]
    ring
  | single i k =>
    rw [Finsupp.prod_single_index (by simp), Finsupp.prod_single_index (by simp),
      Finsupp.weight_single, ofAdd_nsmul, map_pow, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, mul_pow]

private theorem pf_approx {O X σ : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [Finite σ] (wt : σ → X)
    (F : MvPowerSeries σ O) (n : ℕ) :
    ∃ s : Finset X, (n : ℕ∞) ≤ (F - ∑ j ∈ s, wcomp O wt j F).order := by
  classical
  refine ⟨(truncTotal n F).support.image (Finsupp.weight wt), nat_le_order fun d hd => ?_⟩
  rw [map_sub, map_sum]
  simp only [coeff_wcomp]
  rw [Finset.sum_ite_eq]
  split_ifs with h
  · exact sub_self _
  · rw [sub_zero]
    by_contra h0
    refine h (Finset.mem_image_of_mem _ (MvPolynomial.mem_support_iff.2 ?_))
    rwa [coeff_truncTotal _ hd]

private theorem pf_vanish {O X σ S : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [Finite σ] [CommRing S]
    [Algebra O S] (wt : σ → X) (J : Ideal S) [IsHausdorff J S] (φ : MvPowerSeries σ O →ₐ[O] S)
    (hφ : ∀ i, φ (MvPowerSeries.X i) ∈ J) (F : MvPowerSeries σ O) (h : ∀ j, φ (wcomp O wt j F) = 0) :
    φ F = 0 := by
  refine GlobalTheory.eq_of_forall_sub_mem_ideal_pow J fun n => ?_
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := pf_approx wt F n
  have := GlobalTheory.algHom_mem_pow_of_le_order φ hφ hs
  simpa [h] using this

section Core

variable {O X R K : Type} [CommRing O] [AddCommGroup X] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] (W : WPres O X R)
  [Field K] [Algebra O K] (ξ : W.A →ₐ[O] K)

private theorem pf_zprod (m : W.σ →₀ ℕ) :
    ((m.prod fun s k => W.zA s ^ k : W.A) : R) = W.π (monomial m 1) := by
  rw [pf_monomial]
  exact map_finsuppProd W.A.val _ _

private theorem pf_nonint (n : W.σ →₀ ℕ) (hn : n ≠ 0) (h0 : Finsupp.weight W.wt n = 0)
    (ha : (n.prod fun s k => ξ (W.zA s) ^ k) ≠ 0) :
    ¬ IsIntegral O (n.prod fun s k => ξ (W.zA s) ^ k)⁻¹ := by
  classical
  rintro ⟨p, hp, hpa⟩
  have hu : (n.prod fun s k => ξ (W.zA s) ^ k) = ξ (n.prod fun s k => W.zA s ^ k) := by
    rw [map_finsuppProd]; simp only [map_pow]
  rw [hu] at ha hpa
  set u : W.A := n.prod fun s k => W.zA s ^ k with hudef
  letI : Invertible (ξ u)⁻¹ := invertibleOfNonzero (inv_ne_zero ha)
  have h1 : Polynomial.aeval (ξ u) p.reverse = 0 := by
    have := (Polynomial.eval₂_reverse_eq_zero_iff (algebraMap O K) (ξ u)⁻¹ p).2 hpa
    rwa [invOf_eq_inv, inv_inv] at this
  have hU : (monomial n 1 : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) ∈ wtPart O W.wt 0 := fun d hd => by
    rw [coeff_monomial, if_neg]
    rintro rfl
    exact hd h0
  have hU' : Polynomial.aeval (monomial n 1 : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) p.reverse ∈ wtPart O W.wt 0 := by
    have : Algebra.adjoin O {(monomial n 1 : MvPowerSeries W.σ O)} ≤
        SetLike.GradeZero.subalgebra (wtPart O W.wt) := Algebra.adjoin_le (Set.singleton_subset_iff.2 hU)
    exact this (Polynomial.aeval_mem_adjoin_singleton O _)
  have hunit : IsUnit (Polynomial.aeval (monomial n 1 : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) p.reverse) := by
    rw [isUnit_iff_constantCoeff, Polynomial.map_aeval_eq_aeval_map (φ := RingHom.id O) (ψ := constantCoeff)
      (by ext a; simp [MvPowerSeries.algebraMap_apply])]
    have : constantCoeff (monomial n 1 : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) = 0 := by
      rw [← coeff_zero_eq_constantCoeff_apply, coeff_monomial, if_neg hn.symm]
    rw [this, Polynomial.map_id, ← Polynomial.coeff_zero_eq_aeval_zero, Polynomial.coeff_zero_reverse, hp.leadingCoeff]
    exact isUnit_one
  obtain ⟨G, hG, hG1⟩ := pf_unit W.wt hU' hunit
