  have h2 : Polynomial.aeval u p.reverse * (⟨W.π G, W.part_le_A ⟨G, hG, rfl⟩⟩ : W.A) = 1 := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    have h3 : ((Polynomial.aeval u p.reverse : W.A) : R) =
        W.π (Polynomial.aeval (monomial n 1 : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) p.reverse) := by
      rw [← Polynomial.aeval_algHom_apply, ← pf_zprod]
      exact (Polynomial.aeval_algHom_apply W.A.val u _).symm
    rw [Subalgebra.coe_mul, h3, ← map_mul, hG1, map_one, Subalgebra.coe_one]
  have h4 := congrArg ξ h2
  rw [map_mul, ← Polynomial.aeval_algHom_apply, h1, zero_mul, map_one] at h4
  exact zero_ne_one h4

variable [Module.Free ℤ X] [Module.Finite ℤ X] {B : Type} [CommRing B] [Algebra O B] (ι : B →ₐ[O] K)
  (t : Multiplicative X →* Kˣ) (g : MvPowerSeries W.σ O →ₐ[O] B)

private theorem pf_step (hg : ∀ s, ι (g (MvPowerSeries.X s)) = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (W.wt s)) * ξ (W.zA s))
    (I J : Ideal B) (S : Set (W.σ →₀ ℕ)) (j : X) (hS : ∀ n ∈ S, Finsupp.weight W.wt n = j)
    (hJ : ∀ n ∈ S, g (monomial n 1) ∈ J)
    (hI : ∀ b, b ∈ wtPart O W.wt 0 → ∀ x : W.A, (x : R) = W.π b → ∃ c, ι c = ξ x ∧ g b - c ∈ I)
    (F : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) (hF : ∀ n, n ∉ S → coeff n F = 0) (x : W.A) (hx : (x : R) = W.π F) :
    ∃ c, ι c = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd j) * ξ x ∧ g F - c ∈ I * J := by
  obtain ⟨μ, hμ, h⟩ := part_span O R X W S j hS
  obtain ⟨b, hb, rfl⟩ := h F hF
  let β : (W.σ →₀ ℕ) → W.A := fun m => ⟨W.π (b m), W.part_le_A ⟨b m, hb m, rfl⟩⟩
  choose c hc hc' using fun m => hI (b m) (hb m) (β m) rfl
  have hx' : x = ∑ m ∈ μ, β m * m.prod fun s k => W.zA s ^ k := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    rw [hx, map_sum, AddSubmonoidClass.coe_finset_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun m _ => ?_
    rw [Subalgebra.coe_mul, pf_zprod, map_mul]
  refine ⟨∑ m ∈ μ, c m * g (monomial m 1), ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [hx', map_sum, map_sum, Finset.mul_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun m hm => ?_
    rw [map_mul, map_mul, hc, pf_monomial, map_finsuppProd, map_finsuppProd]
    simp only [map_pow, hg]
    rw [pf_prod W.wt t (fun s => ξ (W.zA s)), hS m (hμ hm)]
    ring
  · rw [map_sum, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
    refine Ideal.sum_mem _ fun m hm => ?_
    rw [map_mul, ← sub_mul]
    exact Ideal.mul_mem_mul (hc' m) (hJ m (hμ hm))

private theorem pf_agree (hι : Function.Injective ι)
    (hg : ∀ s, ι (g (MvPowerSeries.X s)) = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (W.wt s)) * ξ (W.zA s))
    (J : Ideal B) [IsHausdorff J B] (hJ : ∀ s, g (MvPowerSeries.X s) ∈ J)
    (h0 : ∀ x ∈ W.gr 0, ∃ c, ι c = ξ x) (j : X) (F : MvPowerSeries W.σ O) (hF : F ∈ wtPart O W.wt j)
    (x : W.A) (hx : (x : R) = W.π F) : ι (g F) = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd j) * ξ x := by
  classical
  have hQ : ∀ N : ℕ, ∀ b, b ∈ wtPart O W.wt 0 → ∀ x : W.A, (x : R) = W.π b →
      ∃ c, ι c = ξ x ∧ g b - c ∈ J ^ N := by
    intro N
    induction N with
    | zero =>
      intro b hb x hx
      obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := h0 x ⟨b, hb, hx.symm⟩
      exact ⟨c, hc, by simp⟩
    | succ N ih =>
      intro b hb x hx
      obtain ⟨c, hc, hc'⟩ := pf_step W ξ ι t g hg (J ^ N) J {n | Finsupp.weight W.wt n = 0 ∧ n ≠ 0} 0
        (fun n hn => hn.1) (fun n hn => by
          have := GlobalTheory.algHom_sub_constantCoeff_mem g hJ (monomial n 1)
          rwa [← coeff_zero_eq_constantCoeff_apply, coeff_monomial, if_neg hn.2.symm, map_zero,
            sub_zero] at this) ih
        (b - algebraMap O _ (constantCoeff b)) (fun n hn => by
          rw [map_sub, MvPowerSeries.algebraMap_apply, coeff_C]
          split_ifs with h
          · subst h; simp
          · rw [sub_zero]
            exact hb n fun h' => hn ⟨h', h⟩)
        (x - algebraMap O _ (constantCoeff b)) (by
          rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, ← hx]; rfl)
      refine ⟨c + algebraMap O B (constantCoeff b), ?_, ?_⟩
      · rw [map_add, hc, AlgHom.commutes, map_sub, AlgHom.commutes]
        simp
      · rw [map_sub, AlgHom.commutes, sub_sub, add_comm] at hc'
        rwa [pow_succ]
  obtain ⟨c, hc, hc'⟩ := pf_step W ξ ι t g hg ⊥ ⊤ {n | Finsupp.weight W.wt n = j} j (fun n hn => hn)
    (fun _ _ => trivial) (fun b hb x hx => by
      obtain ⟨c, hc, -⟩ := hQ 0 b hb x hx
      refine ⟨c, hc, ?_⟩
      rw [Ideal.mem_bot, sub_eq_zero]
      refine GlobalTheory.eq_of_forall_sub_mem_ideal_pow J fun N => ?_
      obtain ⟨c', hc1, hc2⟩ := hQ N b hb x hx
      rwa [hι (hc.trans hc1.symm)]) F hF x hx
  rw [Ideal.bot_mul, Ideal.mem_bot, sub_eq_zero] at hc'
  rw [hc', hc]

private theorem pf_factor [DecidableEq X] (hι : Function.Injective ι)
    (hg : ∀ s, ι (g (MvPowerSeries.X s)) = t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (W.wt s)) * ξ (W.zA s))
    (J : Ideal B) [IsHausdorff J B] (hJ : ∀ s, g (MvPowerSeries.X s) ∈ J)
    (h0 : ∀ x ∈ W.gr 0, ∃ c, ι c = ξ x) :
    ∃ f : R →ₐ[O] B, (ι.comp f).comp W.A.val = act W.gr t ξ := by
  have key := pf_agree W ξ ι t g hι hg J hJ h0
  have hker : ∀ F ∈ RingHom.ker W.π, g F = 0 := by
    intro F hF
    refine pf_vanish W.wt J g hJ F fun j => hι ?_
    have h1 : W.π (wcomp O W.wt j F) = 0 := W.homog F hF j
    rw [key j _ (wcomp_mem O W.wt j F) 0 h1.symm, map_zero, map_zero, mul_zero]
  let f : R →ₐ[O] B := (Ideal.Quotient.liftₐ (RingHom.ker W.π) g hker).comp
    (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective W.surj).symm.toAlgHom
  have hf : ∀ F, f (W.π F) = g F := by
    intro F
    have : (Ideal.quotientKerAlgEquivOfSurjective W.surj).symm (W.π F) = Ideal.Quotient.mk _ F := by
      rw [AlgEquiv.symm_apply_eq]; rfl
    simp only [f, AlgHom.comp_apply, AlgEquiv.coe_algHom]
    erw [this]
    rfl
  refine ⟨f, AlgHom.ext fun x => ?_⟩
  induction x using DirectSum.Decomposition.inductionOn W.gr with
  | zero => rw [map_zero, map_zero]
  | add a b ha hb => rw [map_add, map_add, ha, hb]
  | @homogeneous j x =>
    obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := x
    rw [act_of_mem W.gr t ξ hx]
    obtain ⟨F, hF, hFx⟩ := hx
    have hFx' : (x : R) = W.π F := hFx.symm
    rw [← key j F hF x hFx', ← hf, ← hFx']
    rfl

end Core

theorem UpperTheory.V.Alg.points_finite :
    ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] (X : Type) [AddCommGroup X] [Module.Free ℤ X] [Module.Finite ℤ X] [DecidableEq X] (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (W : WPres O X R), (∀ s, W.z s ∈ maximalIdeal R) → ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] [IsAlgClosed K] [Algebra O K] [FaithfulSMul O K] [Algebra.IsAlgebraic O K] (ξ : W.A →ₐ[O] K),
      ((W.gr 0).map ξ.toLinearMap).FG →
      ∃ (t : Multiplicative X →* Kˣ) (Ξ : R →ₐ[O] K), Ξ.comp W.A.val = act W.gr t ξ ∧ Module.Finite O Ξ.range := by
  intro O _ _ _ _ X _ _ _ _ R _ _ _ W _ K _ _ _ _ _ ξ hfg
  obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := pf_abs O K
  obtain ⟨p, hp⟩ := IsDiscreteValuationRing.exists_irreducible O
  have hinj := FaithfulSMul.algebraMap_injective O K
  have hp0 : algebraMap O K p ≠ 0 := (map_ne_zero_iff _ hinj).2 hp.ne_zero
  have hlt : ∀ a : K, (a ≠ 0 → ¬ IsIntegral O a⁻¹) → w a < 1 := by
    intro a ha
    by_cases h0 : a = 0
    · simp [h0]
    · by_contra h
      refine ha h0 (hw _ ?_)
      rw [map_inv₀]
      exact inv_le_one_of_one_le₀ (not_lt.1 h)
  have hwp : w (algebraMap O K p) < 1 := hlt _ fun _ h => by
    haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O (integralClosure O K)) := inferInstance
    refine hp.not_isUnit (IsLocalHom.map_nonunit (f := algebraMap O (integralClosure O K)) p
      (IsUnit.of_mul_eq_one (⟨_, h⟩ : integralClosure O K) (Subtype.ext ?_)))
    exact mul_inv_cancel₀ hp0
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ : ∃ t : Multiplicative X →* Kˣ,
      ∀ s, w ((t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (W.wt s)) : K) * ξ (W.zA s)) < 1 := by
    letI : NormedField K := w.toNormedField
    exact carver W.σ X W.wt K (fun s => ξ (W.zA s)) ⟨_, hp0, hwp.ne⟩
      fun n hn h0 => hlt _ (pf_nonint W ξ n hn h0)
  set y : W.σ → K := fun s => (t (Multiplicative.ofAdd (W.wt s)) : K) * ξ (W.zA s) with hy
  have hc : ∀ s, ∃ n : ℕ, IsIntegral O (y s ^ n / algebraMap O K p) := fun s => by
    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := exists_pow_lt_of_lt_one (w.pos hp0) (ht s)
    refine ⟨n, hw _ ?_⟩
    rw [map_div₀, map_pow]
    exact div_le_one_of_le₀ hn.le (w.nonneg _)
  choose e he using hc
  obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := id hfg
  let G : Set K := (T : Set K) ∪ Set.range y ∪ Set.range fun s => y s ^ e s / algebraMap O K p
  have hG : G.Finite := (T.finite_toSet.union (Set.finite_range _)).union (Set.finite_range _)
  have hGi : ∀ x ∈ G, IsIntegral O x := by
    rintro x ((hx | ⟨s, rfl⟩) | ⟨s, rfl⟩)
    · refine IsIntegral.of_mem_of_fg ((SetLike.GradeZero.subalgebra W.gr).map ξ) hfg x ?_
      have : x ∈ (W.gr 0).map ξ.toLinearMap := hT ▸ Submodule.subset_span hx
      exact this
    · exact hw _ (ht s).le
    · exact he s
  let B : Subalgebra O K := Algebra.adjoin O G
  haveI : Module.Finite O B := Algebra.finite_adjoin_of_finite_of_isIntegral hG hGi
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing B := IsNoetherianRing.of_finite O B
  haveI := GlobalTheory.isAdicComplete_map_of_finite (maximalIdeal O) B
  let J : Ideal B := ((maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O B)).radical
  obtain ⟨N, hN⟩ := Ideal.exists_radical_pow_le_of_fg ((maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O B))
    (IsNoetherian.noetherian _)
  haveI : IsAdicComplete J B :=
    { toIsHausdorff := GlobalTheory.NilpotentEval.isHausdorff_of_pow_le hN
      toIsPrecomplete := GlobalTheory.NilpotentEval.isPrecomplete_of_pow_le hN Ideal.le_radical }
  let yB : W.σ → B := fun s => ⟨y s, Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl (Or.inr ⟨s, rfl⟩))⟩
  have hyB : ∀ s, yB s ∈ J := fun s => by
    refine ⟨e s, ?_⟩
    have : yB s ^ e s = algebraMap O B p *
        ⟨y s ^ e s / algebraMap O K p, Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inr ⟨s, rfl⟩)⟩ :=
      Subtype.ext (mul_div_cancel₀ _ hp0).symm
    rw [this]
    exact Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ (Ideal.mem_map_of_mem _ hp.not_isUnit)
  obtain ⟨f, hf⟩ := pf_factor W ξ B.val t (GlobalTheory.mvEvalAdic O J yB hyB) Subtype.val_injective
    (fun s => by rw [GlobalTheory.mvEvalAdic_X]; rfl) J (fun s => by rw [GlobalTheory.mvEvalAdic_X]; exact hyB s)
    (fun x hx => by
      have h1 : ξ x ∈ Submodule.span O (T : Set K) := hT ▸ Submodule.mem_map_of_mem hx
      have h2 : Submodule.span O (T : Set K) ≤ B.toSubmodule :=
        Submodule.span_le.2 fun a ha => Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl (Or.inl ha))
      exact ⟨⟨ξ x, h2 h1⟩, rfl⟩)
  refine ⟨t, B.val.comp f, hf, ?_⟩
  have hle : (B.val.comp f).range ≤ B := by
    rintro _ ⟨x, rfl⟩
    exact (f x).2
  exact Module.Finite.of_injective (Subalgebra.inclusion hle).toLinearMap (Subalgebra.inclusion_injective hle)


[UCE_SRC_ORDINARY_REPRESENTATIVE] Original all-entry-map ordinary representative, exact torus conjugacy and finite coefficient ring
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/WeightZeroAlgebraLeEntryAlgebra.lean#L276-L343
private theorem exists_range {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O]
    [Algebra O (PadicAlgCl l)] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O (PadicAlgCl l)] {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R]
    (Ξ : R →ₐ[O] PadicAlgCl l) (hf : Module.Finite O Ξ.range) :
    ∃ (A : Subalgebra O (PadicAlgCl l)) (h : IsDiscreteValuationRing A) (_ : Module.Finite O A) (f : R →ₐ[O] A),
      IsAdicComplete (@maximalIdeal A _ h.toIsLocalRing) A ∧ ∀ x, (f x : PadicAlgCl l) = Ξ x := by
  obtain ⟨n, p, hp⟩ := Module.Finite.exists_fin' O Ξ.range
  have hint : ∀ x : Ξ.range, ‖(x : PadicAlgCl l)‖ ≤ 1 := fun x =>
    (int_iff_norm _).1 (isIntegral_trans (A := O) _ ((Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral x).map Ξ.range.val))
  obtain ⟨A, hA1, hA2, hbA⟩ := exists_dvr (O := O) (fun i => (p (Pi.single i 1) : PadicAlgCl l)) fun i => hint _
  have hmem : ∀ x, Ξ x ∈ A := fun x => by
    obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hp ⟨Ξ x, x, rfl⟩
    have h1 : Ξ x = (p v : PadicAlgCl l) := (congrArg Subtype.val hv).symm
    rw [h1, LinearMap.pi_apply_eq_sum_univ, AddSubmonoidClass.coe_finset_sum]
    refine sum_mem fun i _ => A.smul_mem ?_ _
    convert hbA i using 3
    funext j
    simp [Pi.single_apply, eq_comm]
  haveI : Module.Finite ℤ_[l] A := Module.Finite.trans O A
  exact ⟨A, hA1, hA2, Ξ.codRestrict A hmem, (UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l A).1, fun _ => rfl⟩

private theorem push_eq {O Γ R K : Type} [CommRing O] [Group Γ] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [CommRing K] [Algebra O K]
    (u : Γ →* GG 3 R) (W : WPres O ℤ R) (hent : ∀ g a b, (u g).mat a b ∈ W.part (wt a b)) {T : Subalgebra O R}
    (hA : W.A ≤ T) (ξ : T →ₐ[O] K) (r : Γ →* GG 3 K) (hr : IsPush ξ u r) (t : Multiplicative ℤ →* Kˣ) (Ξ : R →ₐ[O] K)
    (hΞ : Ξ.comp W.A.val = act W.gr t (ξ.comp (Subalgebra.inclusion hA))) (A : Subalgebra O K) (f : R →ₐ[O] A)
    (hf : ∀ x, (f x : K) = Ξ x) :
    push A.val (push f u) = conjBy (tor _ (t (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1))) r := by
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext (Matrix.ext fun a b => ?_)) ?_
  · refine Eq.trans ?_ ((conjBy_tor r _).1 g a b).symm
    obtain ⟨h, e⟩ := (hr g).2 a b
    rw [e, ← map_zpow, ← ofAdd_zsmul, smul_eq_mul, mul_one]
    have h1 : Ξ ((u g).mat a b) = _ := (DFunLike.congr_fun hΞ ⟨_, W.part_le_A (hent g a b)⟩).trans
      (act_of_mem W.gr t _ (j := wt a b) (hent g a b))
    exact (hf _).trans h1
  · rw [(conjBy_tor r _).2.1, (hr g).1]
    rfl

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

open NumberField IsLocalRing Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Aut UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.exists_ordPt_of_point :
    Loc.LocalPublishedTheorems → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω), D.Marked g₁ → ∀ (U : D.ULoc) (S : D.Ring U g₁),
    FinA D g₁ → ∀ (B : ValuationSubring Ω), (∀ x, x ∈ B ↔ ‖D.j x‖ ≤ 1) → ∀ (ξ : entAlg c.O S.r.hom →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l) (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)), U3.IsPush ξ S.r.hom r →
      ∃ (A : Subalgebra c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) (_ : IsDiscreteValuationRing A) (_ : Module.Finite c.O A) (t : (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ) (r' : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A), OrdPt l D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B r' ∧ UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r' = conjBy (tor _ t) r := by
  intro hP Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D g₁ hg U S hfin B hB ξ r hr
  obtain ⟨hc, -, hl, hinj, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing c.O := PrincipalIdealRing.isNoetherianRing
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l) :=
    IsFractionRing.comap_isAlgebraic_iff.2 (inferInstance : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l))
  haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic c.O (PadicAlgCl l) := Algebra.IsAlgebraic.extendScalars hinj
  obtain ⟨W, hz, hs, hent⟩ := exists_pres S (Loc.locGood hP D.loc U).conj
  have hlS : (l : S.R) ∈ maximalIdeal S.R := fun h => by
    have h1 : IsUnit (algebraMap c.O S.R (l : c.O)) := by rwa [map_natCast]
    exact hl (IsLocalHom.map_nonunit _ h1)
  have h0 := part_zero_le _ hlS S.r.hom W hz hs (hfin U S)
  have hA : W.A ≤ entAlg c.O S.r.hom :=
    A_le W _ (fun i => span_le_ent _ _ (hs i)) fun x hx => weight_zero_algebra_le_entry_algebra _ (h0 x hx)
  haveI := hfin U S
  have hfg : ((W.gr 0).map (ξ.comp (Subalgebra.inclusion hA)).toLinearMap).FG := by
    refine ((Module.Finite.fg_top (R := c.O) (M := weightZeroSubalgebra c.O S.r.hom)).map
      (ξ.comp (Subalgebra.inclusion (weight_zero_algebra_le_entry_algebra S.r.hom))).toLinearMap).of_le ?_
    rintro _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨⟨x, h0 x hx⟩, trivial, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨t, Ξ, hΞ, hr'⟩ := Alg.points_finite c.O ℤ S.R W hz (PadicAlgCl l) _ hfg
  obtain ⟨A, hA1, hA2, f, hA3, hf⟩ := exists_range Ξ hr'
  haveI : IsLocalRing A := hA1.toIsLocalRing
  exact ⟨A, hA1, hA2, t (Multiplicative.ofAdd 1), U3.push f S.r.hom,
    ordPt_of_gd hP D g₁ hg U S B hB A f (loc_of_int f), push_eq _ W hent hA ξ r hr t Ξ hΞ A f hf⟩


[UCE_SRC_OPEN_IRREDUCIBLE] Full original all-open-subgroup irreducibility, including finite-index coordinate and residual arguments
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/MatIrrOpenGd.lean#L17-L382
private theorem f3 : ∀ i : Fin 3, i ≠ i + 1 ∧ i ≠ i + 2 ∧ ∀ k, k = i ∨ k = i + 1 ∨ k = i + 2 := by decide

private theorem single_mem (d : Fin 3 → K) (hd : Function.Injective d) (W : Submodule K (Fin 3 → K))
    (hW : ∀ v ∈ W, diagonal d *ᵥ v ∈ W) {v : Fin 3 → K} (hv : v ∈ W) {i : Fin 3} (hi : v i ≠ 0) :
    Pi.single i (1 : K) ∈ W :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.SingleMemSubmodule.single_mem d hd W hW hv hi

private theorem le_of_single {W W' : Submodule K (Fin 3 → K)} (h : ∀ v ∈ W, ∀ i, v i ≠ 0 → Pi.single i (1 : K) ∈ W') :
    W ≤ W' := fun v hv => by
  rw [pi_eq_sum_univ' v]
  refine W'.sum_mem fun i _ => ?_
  by_cases hi : v i = 0
  · rw [hi, zero_smul]; exact W'.zero_mem
  · exact W'.smul_mem _ (h v hv i hi)

set_option synthInstance.maxSize 1000 in
private theorem comb' : ∀ P : Fin 3 → Bool, (∃ i, P i = true) → (∃ i, ¬P i = true) → ∀ Q : Fin 3 → Bool, (∃ i, Q i = true) →
    (∃ i, ¬Q i = true) → (∃ i, P i = true ∧ ¬Q i = true) →
    (∃ i, P i = true ∧ ∀ j, j ≠ i → ¬P j = true) ∨ (∃ i, Q i = true ∧ ∀ j, j ≠ i → ¬Q j = true) ∨
      ∃ i, (P i = true ∧ Q i = true) ∧ ∀ j, j ≠ i → ¬(P j = true ∧ Q j = true) := by decide

private theorem comb : ∀ P Q : Fin 3 → Prop, (∃ i, P i) → (∃ i, ¬P i) → (∃ i, Q i) → (∃ i, ¬Q i) → (∃ i, P i ∧ ¬Q i) →
    (∃ i, P i ∧ ∀ j, j ≠ i → ¬P j) ∨ (∃ i, Q i ∧ ∀ j, j ≠ i → ¬Q j) ∨ ∃ i, (P i ∧ Q i) ∧ ∀ j, j ≠ i → ¬(P j ∧ Q j) := by
  intro P Q
  classical
  have := fun h h' => comb' (fun i => decide (P i)) h h' fun i => decide (Q i)
  simp only [decide_eq_true_eq] at this
  exact this

private theorem exists_line (H U : Subgroup G) (hUH : U ≤ H) [hn : U.Normal] (a : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)
    (hm : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ h ∈ H, a (g * h) = a g * a h) (h1 : a 1 = 1) {u : G} (hu : u ∈ U) (d : Fin 3 → K)
    (hd : Function.Injective d) (hud : a u = diagonal d) (hH : MatIrr H a) (hU : ¬MatIrr U a) :
    ∃ i, ∀ s ∈ U, ∀ j, j ≠ i → a s j i = 0 := by
  have key : ∀ W : Submodule K (Fin 3 → K), (∀ g ∈ U, ∀ v ∈ W, a g *ᵥ v ∈ W) →
      ∀ v ∈ W, ∀ i, v i ≠ 0 → Pi.single i (1 : K) ∈ W := fun W hW v hv i hi =>
    single_mem d hd W (fun v hv => hud ▸ hW u hu v hv) hv hi
  have fin : ∀ W : Submodule K (Fin 3 → K), (∀ g ∈ U, ∀ v ∈ W, a g *ᵥ v ∈ W) →
      (∃ i, Pi.single i (1 : K) ∈ W ∧ ∀ j, j ≠ i → ¬Pi.single j (1 : K) ∈ W) → ∃ i, ∀ s ∈ U, ∀ j, j ≠ i → a s j i = 0 :=
    fun W hW ⟨i, hi, hi'⟩ => ⟨i, fun s hs j hj => by
      by_contra hc
      exact hi' j hj (key W hW _ (hW s hs _ hi) j (by simpa using hc))⟩
  have ne1 : ∀ W : Submodule K (Fin 3 → K), (∀ g ∈ U, ∀ v ∈ W, a g *ᵥ v ∈ W) → W ≠ ⊥ →
      ∃ i, Pi.single i (1 : K) ∈ W := fun W hW hb => by
    obtain ⟨v, hv, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff W).1 hb
    obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := Function.ne_iff.1 h0
    exact ⟨i, key W hW v hv i hi⟩
  have ne2 : ∀ W : Submodule K (Fin 3 → K), W ≠ ⊤ → ∃ i, ¬Pi.single i (1 : K) ∈ W := fun W ht => by
    by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    exact ht (top_le_iff.1 (le_of_single fun _ _ i _ => hc i))
  unfold MatIrr at hU
  push Not at hU
  obtain ⟨W, hW, hb, ht⟩ := hU
  obtain ⟨g, hg, v, hv, hgv⟩ : ∃ g ∈ H, ∃ v ∈ W, a g *ᵥ v ∉ W := by
    by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    exact (hH W hc).elim hb ht
  have hinv : ∀ w, a g *ᵥ (a g⁻¹ *ᵥ w) = w := fun w => by
    rw [mulVec_mulVec, ← hm g hg _ (H.inv_mem hg), mul_inv_cancel, h1, one_mulVec]
  set W' := W.comap (mulVecLin (a g)) with hW'
  have hm' : ∀ w, w ∈ W' ↔ a g *ᵥ w ∈ W := fun _ => Iff.rfl
  have hs' : ∀ s ∈ U, ∀ w ∈ W', a s *ᵥ w ∈ W' := fun s hs w hw => by
    have h2 := hn.conj_mem s hs g
    rw [hm', mulVec_mulVec, ← hm g hg s (hUH hs), show g * s = g * s * g⁻¹ * g by group, hm _ (hUH h2) g hg,
      ← mulVec_mulVec]
    exact hW _ h2 _ hw
  have hb' : W' ≠ ⊥ := fun h => hb ((Submodule.eq_bot_iff W).2 fun w hw => by
    have : a g⁻¹ *ᵥ w ∈ W' := by rw [hm', hinv]; exact hw
    rw [h, Submodule.mem_bot] at this
    rw [← hinv w, this, mulVec_zero])
  have ht' : W' ≠ ⊤ := fun h => hgv ((hm' v).1 (h ▸ Submodule.mem_top))
  have hle : ∃ i, Pi.single i (1 : K) ∈ W ∧ ¬Pi.single i (1 : K) ∈ W' := by
    by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    exact hgv (le_of_single (W' := W') (fun v hv i hi => hc i (key W hW v hv i hi)) hv)
  rcases comb _ _ (ne1 W hW hb) (ne2 W ht) (ne1 W' hs' hb') (ne2 W' ht') hle with h | h | h
  · exact fin W hW h
  · exact fin W' hs' h
  · exact fin (W ⊓ W') (fun s hs w hw => ⟨hW s hs w hw.1, hs' s hs w hw.2⟩) h

private theorem coord {H : Subgroup G} {a : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K} (hH : MatIrr H a) (P : Fin 3 → Prop)
    (h : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ i j, P i → ¬P j → a g j i = 0) {i : Fin 3} (hi : P i) (j : Fin 3) : P j := by
  by_contra hj
  have := hH (Submodule.pi {j | ¬P j} fun _ => ⊥) fun g hg v hv j hj => by
    refine (Submodule.mem_bot K).2 ?_
    show ∑ i, a g j i * v i = 0
    refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i _ => ?_
    by_cases hi : P i
    · rw [h g hg i j hi hj, zero_mul]
    · rw [(Submodule.mem_bot K).1 (hv i hi), mul_zero]
  rcases this with h0 | h0
  · have h1 : Pi.single i (1 : K) ∈ Submodule.pi {j | ¬P j} fun _ => (⊥ : Submodule K K) := fun k hk =>
      (Submodule.mem_bot K).2 (Pi.single_eq_of_ne (fun e : k = i => hk (e ▸ hi)) _)
    rw [h0, Submodule.mem_bot] at h1
    simpa using congrFun h1 i
  · have h1 : (fun _ => (1 : K)) ∈ Submodule.pi {j | ¬P j} fun _ => (⊥ : Submodule K K) := h0 ▸ Submodule.mem_top
    simpa using h1 j hj

private theorem perm1 : ∀ p : Fin 3 → Fin 3, Function.Injective p → (∀ i, p i ≤ i) → ∀ i, p i = i := by decide

private theorem perm2 : ∀ p : Fin 3 → Fin 3, Function.Injective p → p 0 ≠ 0 → ∀ i, p i = i →
    ∀ q : Fin 3 → Fin 3, Function.Injective q → q i ≠ i → (∀ j, q j ≠ j) ∨ ∀ j, p (q j) ≠ j := by decide

private theorem core (H U : Subgroup G) (hUH : U ≤ H) [hn : U.Normal] (a : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)
    (hm : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ h ∈ H, a (g * h) = a g * a h) (h1 : a 1 = 1) {u : G} (hu : u ∈ U) (d : Fin 3 → K)
    (hd : Function.Injective d) (hud : a u = diagonal d) (hH : MatIrr H a) (hU : ¬MatIrr U a)
    {x : G} (hx : x ∈ H) (hxu : ∀ i j, j < i → a x i j = 0) : ∃ g ∈ H, ∀ n : ℕ, (a (g * x ^ n)).trace = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨i₀, hi₀⟩ := exists_line H U hUH a hm h1 hu d hd hud hH hU
  set L : Fin 3 → Prop := fun i => ∀ s ∈ U, ∀ j, j ≠ i → a s j i = 0 with hL
  have hc : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ i, L i → ∀ s ∈ U, ∃ c, ∀ k, (a s * a g) k i = a g k i * c := fun g hg i hi s hs => by
    have h2 := hn.conj_mem' s hs g
    refine ⟨a (g⁻¹ * s * g) i i, fun k => ?_⟩
    rw [← hm s (hUH hs) g hg, show s * g = g * (g⁻¹ * s * g) by group, hm g hg _ (hUH h2), mul_apply,
      Finset.sum_eq_single i (fun b _ hb => by rw [hi _ h2 b hb, mul_zero]) (by simp)]
  have hone : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ i, L i → ∀ j, a g j i ≠ 0 → ∀ k, k ≠ j → a g k i = 0 := fun g hg i hi j hj k hk => by
    by_contra h
    obtain ⟨c, e⟩ := hc g hg i hi u hu
    simp only [hud, diagonal_mul, mul_comm (d _)] at e
    exact hk (hd ((mul_left_cancel₀ h (e k)).trans (mul_left_cancel₀ hj (e j)).symm))
  have hall : ∀ j, L j := coord hH L (fun g hg i j hi hj => by
    by_contra h
    refine hj fun s hs k hk => ?_
    obtain ⟨c, e⟩ := hc g hg i hi s hs
    have e' := e k
    rw [hone g hg i hi j h k hk, zero_mul, mul_apply,
      Finset.sum_eq_single j (fun b _ hb => by rw [hone g hg i hi j h b hb, mul_zero]) (by simp)] at e'
    exact (mul_eq_zero.1 e').resolve_right h) hi₀
  have hex : ∀ g : G, ∀ i, ∃ j, g ∈ H → a g j i ≠ 0 := fun g i => by
    by_contra h
    push Not at h
    have hg := (h 0).1
    have e := congrFun (congrFun (hm _ (H.inv_mem hg) g hg) i) i
    rw [inv_mul_cancel, h1, mul_apply] at e
    simp [fun j => (h j).2] at e
  choose σ hσ using hex
  have hiff : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ i j, a g j i ≠ 0 → σ g i = j := fun g hg i j h => by
    by_contra h'
    exact hσ g i hg (hone g hg i (hall i) j h _ h')
  have hmul : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ h ∈ H, ∀ i, σ (g * h) i = σ g (σ h i) := fun g hg h hh i => by
    refine hiff _ (H.mul_mem hg hh) _ _ ?_
    rw [hm g hg h hh, mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single (σ h i)
      (fun b _ hb => by rw [hone h hh i (hall i) _ (hσ h i hh) b hb, mul_zero]) (by simp)]
    exact mul_ne_zero (hσ g _ hg) (hσ h i hh)
  have hσ1 : ∀ i, σ 1 i = i := fun i => hiff 1 H.one_mem i i (by simp [h1])
  have hinj : ∀ g ∈ H, Function.Injective (σ g) := fun g hg =>
    Function.LeftInverse.injective (g := σ g⁻¹) fun i => by
      rw [← hmul _ (H.inv_mem hg) g hg, inv_mul_cancel, hσ1]
  have hmv : ∀ i, ∃ g ∈ H, σ g i ≠ i := fun i => by
    by_contra h
    push Not at h
    have := coord hH (· = i) (fun g hg k j hk hj => by
      by_contra h'
      exact hj ((hiff g hg k j h').symm.trans (by rw [hk, h g hg])) ) rfl (i + 1)
    exact (f3 i).1 this.symm
  have hx1 := perm1 _ (hinj x hx) fun i => not_lt.1 fun h => hσ x i hx (hxu _ _ h)
  have hxn : ∀ (n : ℕ) i, σ (x ^ n) i = i := fun n i => by
    induction n with
    | zero => rw [pow_zero, hσ1]
    | succ n ih => rw [pow_succ, hmul _ (H.pow_mem hx n) x hx, hx1, ih]
  obtain ⟨g, hg, hder⟩ : ∃ g ∈ H, ∀ j, σ g j ≠ j := by
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hp0⟩ := hmv 0
    by_cases h : ∀ j, σ p j ≠ j
    · exact ⟨p, hp, h⟩
    push Not at h
    obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := h
    obtain ⟨q, hq, hqi⟩ := hmv i
    rcases perm2 _ (hinj p hp) hp0 i hi _ (hinj q hq) hqi with h | h
    · exact ⟨q, hq, h⟩
    · exact ⟨p * q, H.mul_mem hp hq, fun j => by rw [hmul p hp q hq]; exact h j⟩
  refine ⟨g, hg, fun n => Finset.sum_eq_zero fun i _ => ?_⟩
  by_contra h
  have := hiff _ (H.mul_mem hg (H.pow_mem hx n)) i i h
  rw [hmul g hg _ (H.pow_mem hx n), hxn] at this
  exact hder i this

private theorem irr_conj {H : Subgroup G} {a : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K} (hH : MatIrr H a)
    {M M' : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K} (h : M * M' = 1) (h' : M' * M = 1) : MatIrr H fun g => M' * a g * M := by
  intro W hW
  have e : ∀ w, M' *ᵥ (M *ᵥ w) = w := fun w => by rw [mulVec_mulVec, h', one_mulVec]
  have hm : ∀ w, w ∈ W.comap (mulVecLin M') ↔ M' *ᵥ w ∈ W := fun _ => Iff.rfl
  refine (hH (W.comap (mulVecLin M')) fun g hg v hv => ?_).imp (fun h0 => ?_) fun h0 => ?_
  · have := hW g hg _ ((hm v).1 hv)
    rw [mulVec_mulVec, Matrix.mul_assoc, h, Matrix.mul_one, ← mulVec_mulVec] at this
    exact this
  · refine (Submodule.eq_bot_iff W).2 fun w hw => ?_
    have : M *ᵥ w ∈ W.comap (mulVecLin M') := by rw [hm, e]; exact hw
    rw [h0, Submodule.mem_bot] at this
    rw [← e w, this, mulVec_zero]
  · refine top_le_iff.1 fun w _ => ?_
    rw [← e w, ← hm, h0]
    exact Submodule.mem_top

private theorem diagonalise (T : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) (hT : T.BlockTriangular id) (hd : Function.Injective T.diag) :
    ∃ n n' : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K, n * n' = 1 ∧ n' * n = 1 ∧ n.BlockTriangular id ∧ n'.BlockTriangular id ∧
      n' * T * n = diagonal T.diag := by
  have h1 : T 1 1 - T 0 0 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 fun h => absurd (hd h) (by decide)
  have h2 : T 2 2 - T 1 1 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 fun h => absurd (hd h) (by decide)
  have h3 : T 2 2 - T 0 0 ≠ 0 := sub_ne_zero.2 fun h => absurd (hd h) (by decide)
  have z1 : T 1 0 = 0 := hT (by decide)
  have z2 : T 2 0 = 0 := hT (by decide)
  have z3 : T 2 1 = 0 := hT (by decide)
  obtain ⟨p, hp⟩ : ∃ p, p * (T 1 1 - T 0 0) = T 0 1 := ⟨_, div_mul_cancel₀ _ h1⟩
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ : ∃ r, r * (T 2 2 - T 1 1) = T 1 2 := ⟨_, div_mul_cancel₀ _ h2⟩
  obtain ⟨q, hq⟩ : ∃ q, q * (T 2 2 - T 0 0) = T 0 2 + T 0 1 * r := ⟨_, div_mul_cancel₀ _ h3⟩
  have e : (!![1, -p, p * r - q; 0, 1, -r; 0, 0, 1] : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K) * !![1, p, q; 0, 1, r; 0, 0, 1] = 1 := by
    ext i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three]
    ring
  refine ⟨!![1, p, q; 0, 1, r; 0, 0, 1], !![1, -p, p * r - q; 0, 1, -r; 0, 0, 1], mul_eq_one_comm.1 e, e, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · intro i j hij
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij ⊢
  · intro i j hij
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp at hij ⊢
  · have : T * !![1, p, q; 0, 1, r; 0, 0, 1] = !![1, p, q; 0, 1, r; 0, 0, 1] * diagonal T.diag := by
      ext i j
      rw [mul_diagonal]
      fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, z1, z2, z3]
      · linear_combination -hp
      · linear_combination -hq
      · linear_combination -hr
    rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, this, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, e, Matrix.one_mul]

private theorem core' (H U : Subgroup G) (hUH : U ≤ H) [U.Normal] (a : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K)
    (hm : ∀ g ∈ H, ∀ h ∈ H, a (g * h) = a g * a h) (h1 : a 1 = 1) {M M' : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K}
    (h : M * M' = 1) (h' : M' * M = 1) {u : G} (hu : u ∈ U) (hut : (M' * a u * M).BlockTriangular id)
    (hd : Function.Injective (M' * a u * M).diag) (hH : MatIrr H a) (hU : ¬MatIrr U a)
    {x : G} (hx : x ∈ H) (hxu : (M' * a x * M).BlockTriangular id) : ∃ g ∈ H, ∀ n : ℕ, (a (g * x ^ n)).trace = 0 := by
  obtain ⟨n, n', e, e', t, t', hn⟩ := diagonalise _ hut hd
  have f : M * n * (n' * M') = 1 := by rw [Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc n, e, Matrix.one_mul, h]
  have f' : n' * M' * (M * n) = 1 := mul_eq_one_comm.1 f
  have as : ∀ X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) K, n' * M' * X * (M * n) = n' * (M' * X * M) * n := fun X => by
    simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
  obtain ⟨g, hg, hz⟩ := core H U hUH (fun g => n' * M' * a g * (M * n)) (fun g hg k hk => by
      rw [hm g hg k hk, Matrix.mul_assoc _ (M * n), ← Matrix.mul_assoc (M * n), ← Matrix.mul_assoc (M * n), f]
      simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc, Matrix.one_mul])
    (by rw [h1, Matrix.mul_one, f']) hu _ hd ((as _).trans hn) (irr_conj hH f f') (fun hc => hU (by
      have := irr_conj hc f' f
      convert this using 2 with g
      rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc (M * n), ← Matrix.mul_assoc (M * n), f, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, f, Matrix.mul_one]))
    hx (fun i j hij => by rw [as]; exact (t'.mul hxu).mul t hij)
  refine ⟨g, hg, fun k => ?_⟩
  have := hz k
  rwa [trace_mul_cycle, f, Matrix.one_mul] at this

end Abstract

private theorem roots {k : Type} [Field k] (a b : kˣ) (h : ∀ n : ℕ, ((a * b ^ n : kˣ) : k) + 1 + (((a * b ^ n)⁻¹ : kˣ) : k) = 0) :
    (b : k) ^ 2 - 1 = 0 := by
  have h' : ∀ n : ℕ, ((a : k) * b ^ n) ^ 2 + a * b ^ n + 1 = 0 := fun n => by
    have e := (a * b ^ n).mul_inv
    have e' := h n
    rw [Units.val_mul, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val] at e e'
    linear_combination ((a : k) * b ^ n) * e' - e
  by_contra hb
  have hb1 : (b : k) - 1 ≠ 0 := fun e => hb (by linear_combination ((b : k) + 1) * e)
  have e1 : (a : k) * b + a + 1 = 0 := mul_left_cancel₀ (mul_ne_zero a.ne_zero hb1) (by linear_combination h' 1 - h' 0)
  have e2 : (a : k) * b ^ 2 + a + 1 = 0 := mul_left_cancel₀ (mul_ne_zero a.ne_zero hb) (by linear_combination h' 2 - h' 0)
  exact mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero a.ne_zero b.ne_zero) hb1 (by linear_combination e2 - e1)

private theorem conj_mat {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (h : GL (Fin 3) R) (r : G →* GG 3 R) {g : G}
    (hg : (r g).right = 1) : (conjBy h r g).mat = (h : Matrix _ _ R) * (r g).mat * ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) R) : Matrix _ _ R) := by
  simp [conjBy, GG.mat, hg]

private theorem frame {O A K G : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra O A] [Field K]
    [Algebra O K] [FaithfulSMul O K] [Group G] (j : A →ₐ[O] K) (hj : Function.Injective j) (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Oˣ)
    (D : Subgroup G) (I : Subgroup D) (r' : G →* GG 3 A) (hpt : Red η Ψ r')
    (ht : CondT (η.comp D.subtype) (Ψ.comp D.subtype) I (r'.comp D.subtype))
    (hI : TypeI η (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.push j r')) (U : Subgroup G) [U.Normal]
    (hU : U ≤ η.ker) (x : D) (hx : η x = 1) (hx' : IsUnit ((Ψ x : O) ^ 2 - 1))
    (u : D) (hu : u ∈ I) (huU : u.1 ∈ U) (hu' : Ψ u ^ 2 ≠ 1) : MatIrr U (mats (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.push j r')) := by
  by_contra hc
  obtain ⟨h, -, htri⟩ := ht
  set P : GL (Fin 3) K := GeneralLinearGroup.map (j : A →+* K) h⁻¹ with hP
  have em : ∀ s, mats (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.push j r') s = (r' s).mat.map j := fun _ => rfl
  have key : ∀ s : D, η s = 1 → (P : Matrix _ _ K) * mats (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.push j r') s * ((P⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix _ _ K) =
      (conjBy h⁻¹ (r'.comp D.subtype) s).mat.map j := fun s hs => by
    rw [conj_mat h⁻¹ (r'.comp D.subtype) (g := s) ((hpt.mult _).trans hs), hP, ← map_inv, em]
    symm
    refine (Matrix.map_mul (f := (j : A →+* K))).trans ?_
    rw [Matrix.map_mul]
    rfl
  have tri : ∀ s : D, η s = 1 →
      ((P : Matrix _ _ K) * mats (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.push j r') s * ((P⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix _ _ K)).BlockTriangular id :=
    fun s hs i j hij => by
    rw [key s hs, map_apply, htri.tri s hs i j (by
      have : (j : ℕ) < i := hij
      simp only [wt, wt1]
      omega), map_zero]
  have hu1 : η u = 1 := hU huU
  have hinj : Function.Injective
      ((P : Matrix _ _ K) * mats (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.push j r') u * ((P⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) K) : Matrix _ _ K)).diag := by
    intro i j hij
    rw [diag_apply, diag_apply, key u hu1, map_apply, map_apply, htri.ine u hu hu1, htri.ine u hu hu1,
      AlgHom.commutes, AlgHom.commutes] at hij
    have e : Ψ u ^ wt1 i = Ψ u ^ wt1 j := Units.ext (FaithfulSMul.algebraMap_injective O K hij)
    generalize Ψ u = y at e hu'
    have h1 : y ≠ 1 := fun h => hu' (by rw [h, one_pow])
    have h2 : y ≠ y⁻¹ := fun h => hu' (by rw [sq, ← eq_inv_iff_mul_eq_one]; exact h)
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [wt1, h1, h2, h1.symm, h2.symm] at e ⊢
  obtain ⟨g, hg, hz⟩ := core' η.ker U hU (mats (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.push j r')) (fun a ha b _ => by
      have : (UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.push j r' a).right = 1 := (hpt.mult a).trans ha
      simp only [mats, GG.mat, map_mul, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, this, sgnAct_one, Units.val_mul])
    (by simp only [mats, GG.mat, map_one, SemidirectProduct.one_left, Units.val_one]) P.inv_mul P.mul_inv huU (tri u hu1) hinj
    hI hc (x := x.1) hx (tri x hx)
  let φ : O →+* ResidueField A := (residue A).comp (algebraMap O A)
  refine (hx'.map φ).ne_zero ?_
  rw [map_sub, map_pow, map_one]
  refine roots (Units.map φ.toMonoidHom (Ψ g)) (Units.map φ.toMonoidHom (Ψ x)) fun n => ?_
  have e := hz n
  rw [← map_pow, ← map_pow, ← map_mul, ← map_mul, ← map_inv]
  generalize g * x.1 ^ n = y at e
  have e1 : (r' y).mat.trace = 0 := by
    refine hj ?_
    rw [map_zero, ← e, trace, trace, map_sum]
    rfl
  have e2 : ∀ i, residue A ((r' y).mat i i) = φ ((r0 η Ψ y).mat i i) := fun i =>
    Ideal.Quotient.eq.2 (hpt.red y i i)
  have e3 := congrArg (residue A) e1
  rw [trace_fin_three, map_add, map_add, e2, e2, e2, map_zero] at e3
  simpa [GG.mat, tor_val, wt1] using e3

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

open NumberField IsLocalRing Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Aut UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.matIrr_open_gd :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω), D.Marked g₁ →
    ∀ (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ (A : Subalgebra c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) [IsDiscreteValuationRing A] [Module.Finite c.O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A), OrdPt l D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B r → TypeI D.η (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r) →
      ∀ V : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), IsOpen (V : Set (G ℚ Ω)) → V ≤ D.η.ker → MatIrr V (mats (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r)) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ D g₁ _ B hv A _ _ r hr hI U hU hUη
  have : IsGalois ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isGalois ℚ Ω
  have : CompactSpace (G ℚ Ω) := inferInstanceAs (CompactSpace (Ω ≃ₐ[ℚ] Ω))
  have : Finite (G ℚ Ω ⧸ U) := U.quotient_finite_of_isOpen hU
  have : U.FiniteIndex := Subgroup.finiteIndex_of_finite_quotient
  obtain ⟨hη, x, hx, hx'⟩ := (dat_facts D).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 B hv
  have hu : IsUnit (1 + (l : ℤ_[l])) := by
    simpa using isUnit_one_sub_self_of_mem_nonunits (-(l : ℤ_[l])) (by simpa using PadicInt.prime_p.not_unit)
  obtain ⟨σ, hσ, hσ'⟩ := GlobalTheory.CycInertia.cycZ_Ine_surjective Ω l B hv hu.unit
  set m := U.normalCore.index * Nat.card (ZMod N)ˣ with hm
  have hm0 : m ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero Subgroup.FiniteIndex.index_ne_zero Nat.card_pos.ne'
  have hσm : σ ^ m ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B := pow_mem hσ m
  have key := frame A.val Subtype.val_injective D.η D.Ψ _ _ r hr.red hr.ord hI U.normalCore
    (U.normalCore_le.trans hUη) ⟨x, Ine_le_Dec B hx⟩ (hη hx) hx' ⟨σ ^ m, Ine_le_Dec B hσm⟩
    (by rw [Subgroup.mem_subgroupOf]; exact hσm)
    (by
      show σ ^ m ∈ _
      rw [hm, pow_mul]
      exact pow_mem (Subgroup.pow_index_mem _ σ) _)
    (by
      show Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) (σ ^ m) ^ 2 ≠ 1
      have h1 : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ (σ ^ m) = 1 := by
        rw [Chr.dirG, MonoidHom.comp_apply, map_pow, hm, pow_mul', pow_card_eq_one', one_pow, map_one]
      rw [Chr.Psi, MonoidHom.mul_apply, h1, mul_one, MonoidHom.zpow_apply, zpow_natCast, map_pow, ← pow_mul,
        ← pow_mul]
      intro h
      have h2 := congrArg (fun y : (c.O)ˣ => ((y : c.O) : PadicAlgCl l)) h
      simp only [Base.chiO, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hσ', Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, Units.coe_map, IsUnit.unit_spec] at h2
      have h3 : (((1 + l) ^ (m * (e * 2)) : ℕ) : PadicAlgCl l) = (1 : ℕ) := by simpa using h2
      have h4 := Nat.cast_injective h3
      have := D.gd.e_pos
      have := hl.out.pos
      rw [Nat.pow_eq_one] at h4
      rcases h4 with h4 | h4
      · omega
      · exact mul_ne_zero hm0 (by omega) h4)
  exact fun W hW => key W fun g hg => hW g (U.normalCore_le hg)


[UCE_SRC_AUTOMORPHY_TRANSPORT] Actual semisimplicity, automorphic attachment, twist and outer-corner conjugacy transports
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/GdMain.lean#L14-L92
private theorem ss_conj {Γ k : Type} [Group Γ] [Field k] {n : ℕ} {ρ ρ' : Γ →* GL (Fin n) k} (h : GL (Fin n) k)
    (hh : ∀ g, ρ' g = h * ρ g * h⁻¹) (hρ : IsSSRep ρ) : IsSSRep ρ' := by
  let e : (Fin n → k) ≃ₗ[k] (Fin n → k) := Matrix.toLin'OfInv (M := (h : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k))
    (M' := ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k)) (Units.mul_inv h) (Units.inv_mul h)
  have he : ∀ v, e v = ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) k) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k) *ᵥ v := fun v => rfl
  have key : ∀ g v, e ((ρ' g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k) *ᵥ v) = (ρ g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k) *ᵥ e v := by
    intro g v
    rw [he, he, hh, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, Matrix.mulVec_mulVec, ← Units.val_mul, ← Units.val_mul]
    congr 2
    group
  intro W hW
  obtain ⟨W', hW', hc⟩ := hρ (W.map (e : (Fin n → k) →ₗ[k] (Fin n → k))) (by
    rintro g _ ⟨v, hv, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨_, hW g v hv, key g v⟩)
  refine ⟨W'.comap (e : (Fin n → k) →ₗ[k] (Fin n → k)), fun g v hv => ?_, ?_⟩
  · show e _ ∈ W'
    rw [key]
    exact hW' g _ hv
  · have := (Submodule.orderIsoMapComap e).symm.isCompl hc
    have h2 : IsCompl ((W.map (e : (Fin n → k) →ₗ[k] (Fin n → k))).comap (e : (Fin n → k) →ₗ[k] (Fin n → k)))
      (W'.comap (e : (Fin n → k) →ₗ[k] (Fin n → k))) := this
    rwa [Submodule.comap_map_eq_of_injective e.injective] at h2

private theorem autCsd_iso {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [IsCMField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] {l : ℕ}
    [Fact l.Prime] {n : ℕ} {ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ} {r r' : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)} (h : Af.RepIso r r')
    (H : Af.IsAutCsd ι r) : Af.IsAutCsd ι r' := by
  obtain ⟨π, h1, h2, h3, h4⟩ := H
  refine ⟨π, h1, h2, ss_conj h.choose h.choose_spec h3, h4.mono fun w ⟨B, hB, hB'⟩ => ⟨B, hB, hB'.of_iso h⟩⟩

private theorem tw_iso {Γ k : Type} [Group Γ] [Field k] {n : ℕ} {r r' : Γ →* GL (Fin n) k} (χ : Γ →* kˣ)
    (h : Af.RepIso r r') : Af.RepIso (Af.tw r χ) (Af.tw r' χ) := by
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := h
  refine ⟨g, fun s => Units.ext ?_⟩
  rw [Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, Af.tw_val, Af.tw_val, hg, Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, Matrix.mul_smul,
    Matrix.smul_mul]

private theorem conj_left {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (r : G →* GG 3 R) (h : GL (Fin 3) R) (g : G)
    (hg : (r g).right = 1) : (conjBy h r g).left = h * (r g).left * h⁻¹ := by
  have e : conjBy h r g = GG.ofGL h * r g * GG.ofGL h⁻¹ := by
    rw [map_inv]; rfl
  rw [e, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.left_inl,
    SemidirectProduct.left_inl, SemidirectProduct.right_inl, sgnAct_one, SemidirectProduct.mul_right,
    SemidirectProduct.right_inl, hg, mul_one, sgnAct_one]

open Matrix in
private theorem matIrr_conj {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] {m : ℕ} {U : Subgroup G}
    {a a' : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R} (h : GL (Fin m) R)
    (e : ∀ g ∈ U, a g = ((h⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) * a' g * h) (H : MatIrr U a) :
    MatIrr U a' :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.Qf5.matIrr_conj h e H

private theorem att_iso {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [Algebra ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : ℕ} {ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ}
    {f : Af.Newform k} {r r' : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)} (h : Af.RepIso r r') (H : f.Att ι r) : f.Att ι r' := by
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := h
  refine ⟨ss_conj g hg H.1, H.2.mono fun w hw A hA => ⟨fun s hs => ?_, fun σ hσ => ?_⟩⟩
  · rw [hg, (hw A hA).1 s hs, mul_one, mul_inv_cancel]
  · rw [← (hw A hA).2 σ hσ, hg, Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, Matrix.coe_units_inv, Matrix.charpoly_units_conj]

private def dg {R : Type} [CommRing R] (t : Rˣ) : GL (Fin 2) R where
  val := Matrix.diagonal ![(t : R), ((t⁻¹ : Rˣ) : R)]
  inv := Matrix.diagonal ![((t⁻¹ : Rˣ) : R), (t : R)]
  val_inv := by
    rw [Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal, ← Matrix.diagonal_one]; congr 1; funext i; fin_cases i <;> simp
  inv_val := by
    rw [Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal, ← Matrix.diagonal_one]; congr 1; funext i; fin_cases i <;> simp

private theorem corner_conj {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] (r : G →* GG 3 R) (t : Rˣ) (g : G) :
    corner (conjBy (tor R t) r g).mat =
      (dg t : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) R) * corner (r g).mat * (((dg t)⁻¹ : GL (Fin 2) R) : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) R) := by
  ext a b
  show _ = (Matrix.diagonal ![(t : R), ((t⁻¹ : Rˣ) : R)] * corner (r g).mat *
    Matrix.diagonal ![((t⁻¹ : Rˣ) : R), (t : R)] : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) R) a b
  rw [Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.diagonal_mul, corner, corner, Matrix.submatrix_apply, Matrix.submatrix_apply,
    (conjBy_tor r t).1, wt, zpow_sub, Units.val_mul, mul_right_comm]
  congr 1
  · congr 1
    fin_cases a <;> simp [wt1]
  · fin_cases b <;> simp [wt1]



[UCE_SRC_JOINT_INSTALLATION] Literal automorphy_rank_three_of_endomorphisms and its same-g1 installation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/GdMain.lean#L93-L129
theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.automorphy_rank_three_of_endomorphisms :
    Loc.LocalPublishedTheorems → GdEnd → AutomorphyUnramifiedCoefficients := by
  intro hL H Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ D _ ι
  obtain ⟨B, hB, -, -, -, hmem, -⟩ := dat_loc D
  obtain ⟨g₁, hM, hfin, h3, h2⟩ := H Ω K l c N e ψ D ι B hB
  have hA : FinA D g₁ := finA_of_end hL D g₁ hM B hmem hfin
  refine ⟨g₁, hM, hA, fun U S ξ r hr hp hI => ?_, fun U S ξ r hp hII h11 W hW => ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨A, _, _, t, r', ho, ht⟩ := exists_ordPt_of_point hL D g₁ hM U S hA B hmem ξ r hp
    have hl : ∀ s ∈ D.η.ker, (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r' s).left = tor _ t * (r s).left * (tor _ t)⁻¹ := fun s hs => by
      rw [ht, conj_left r _ _ ((hr s).trans hs)]
    have hI' : TypeI D.η (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r') := matIrr_conj (tor _ t) (fun s hs => by
      rw [mats, mats, GG.mat, GG.mat, hl s hs, ← Units.val_mul, ← Units.val_mul]
      congr 1
      group) hI
    have hm : HasMult D.η (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r') := ho.red.mult
    have hiso : Af.RepIso (D.rhoK r hr) (D.rhoK _ hm) :=
      ⟨tor _ t, fun s => hl _ (D.η_ker.ge (Base.galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω s))⟩
    have key := h3 A r' ho (matIrr_open_gd D g₁ hM B hB A r' ho hI') (D.rhoK _ hm) fun s => rfl
    exact autCsd_iso (tw_iso _ hiso.symm') key
  · obtain ⟨A, _, _, t, r', ho, ht⟩ := exists_ordPt_of_point hL D g₁ hM U S hA B hmem ξ r hp
    let W' : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l) := (MulAut.conj (dg t)).toMonoidHom.comp W
    have hW' : ∀ s, W' s = dg t * W s * (dg t)⁻¹ := fun s => rfl
    have hII' : TypeII D.η (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r') := by
      rw [ht]
      refine ⟨fun g hg a b hab => ?_, matIrr_conj (dg t) (fun s _ => ?_) hII.2⟩
      · rw [(conjBy_tor r t).1, hII.1 g hg a b hab, mul_zero]
      · rw [corner_conj]
        simp only [← Matrix.mul_assoc]
        rw [Units.inv_mul, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, Units.inv_mul, Matrix.mul_one]
    obtain ⟨f, hf⟩ := h2 A r' ho hII' (fun g hg => by
      apply Subtype.ext
      show (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r' g).mat 1 1 = 1
      rw [ht, (conjBy_tor r t).1, h11 g hg]
      simp [wt]) W' (fun g hg => by
      rw [ht, corner_conj, hW', Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, hW.1 g hg]) (by
      rw [hW', Units.val_mul, Units.val_mul, Matrix.trace_units_conj, hW.2])
    exact ⟨f, att_iso (tw_iso _ (Af.RepIso.symm' ⟨dg t, hW'⟩)) hf⟩


[UCE_SRC_FRAME_BOUNDARY] Original GalLink, SeedClause, HasFrame and GdFrameEx retained producer scopes
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/HostFrameEx.lean#L22-L79
structure GalLink (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ)
    (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] (Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)) where
  ab : IsAbelianGalois ℚ L
  gal : galOf ℚ L Ω = upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker
  unr : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L ≤ η.ker
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L ≤ η.ker
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L,
    barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1
  p : upl Ω L →* Φ.Γ
  p_cont : Continuous p
  p_surj : Function.Surjective p
  p_ker : p.ker = (Subgroup.normalClosure {s : upl Ω L | ∃ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧
    s.1 ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ∧ η s = 1 ∧ barChar O Ψ s = 1}).topologicalClosure
  del : ∀ s, Φ.del (p s) = η s
  eps : ∀ s, Φ.eps (p s) = cycZ ℚ Ω l s
  psi : ∀ s, Φ.Ψ (p s) = barChar O Ψ s
  c : ∃ h : σ ∈ upl Ω L, p ⟨σ, h⟩ = Φ.c
  g0 : ∃ h : g₁ ∈ upl Ω L, p ⟨g₁, h⟩ = Φ.g0
  pl : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω
  pl_conj : ∀ v, ∃ γ : G ℚ Ω, η γ = 1 ∧ pl v = γ • B
  pl_all : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃! v, ∃ u ∈ upl Ω L, B' = u • pl v
  j : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* upl Ω L
  j_cont : ∀ v, Continuous (j v)
  j_inj : ∀ v, Function.Injective (j v)
  j_range : ∀ v (s : upl Ω L), s ∈ (j v).range ↔ s.1 ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  j_loc : ∀ v g, Φ.loc v g = p (j v g)
  j_ine : ∀ v g, g ∈ Φ.Iv v ↔ (j v g).1 ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  dv : ∀ v, Φ.dv v = (upl Ω L).relIndex (Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v))
  mu : ∀ v, ∃ g, cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 (j v g) ≠ 1

def GalLink.p0 {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω} {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ}
    {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] {Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)} (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ)
    (s : G L Ω) : Φ.G0 :=
  have h : galEmb ℚ L Ω s ∈ upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker := k.gal ▸ galEmb_mem ℚ L Ω s
  ⟨k.p ⟨galEmb ℚ L Ω s, h.1⟩, (k.del _).trans h.2⟩

def SeedClause (c : Coef l) (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] : Prop :=
  ∃ (K : Type) (_ : Field K) (_ : NumberField K) (_ : Algebra K Ω) (N w : ℕ) (χ' η' θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (j : ℕ),
    Sd.FieldPairCharacterTripleConditions K L σ N w χ' η' θ j ∧ χ' ≠ η' ∧ ¬ l ∣ orderOf χ' ∧ (∀ s, (η' s).val = (((η s).val : ℤ) : Qbar l)) ∧
    (∀ s, ‖((cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ') s).val - (Ψ s).val.1‖ < 1) ∧ ¬ (l : ℤ) ∣ discr K

def HasFrame (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω)
    (M : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (L : Type) (_ : Field L) (_ : NumberField L) (_ : IsCMField L) (_ : Algebra L Ω) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω)
    (Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField c.O)) (C₀ : ℕ), Nonempty (GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) ∧ Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ ∧
      SeedClause c η Ψ σ L

def HostFrameEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l]
    (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), UnitaryThreeInertia.HostData l e ψ o₁ →
  ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ → ∀ B : ValuationSubring Ω, B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
    HasFrame c (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ B (l * N)

def GdFrameEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ)
    (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
    HasFrame c D.η D.Ψ D.cc B (l * N * (discr K).natAbs)


[UCE_SRC_GD_END_BOUNDARY] Revised ordinary-end installer retains frame, linked-end, descent and both rank-local inputs
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/ThresholdArguments/DenseThresholdCases/GdEndOf.lean#L22-L96
theorem UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.Dn.gdEnd_of_frame_linkedEnd_downCsdK_descent (H : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.HypothesesH') (h3 : Dn.Loc3) (hK3 : Dn.LocalConditionRankThreeEvenLevel) (hPot : Dn.PotTwo) (hIne : Dn.Ine2) :
    GdFrameEx → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Sym.Pl.LinkedEndEAlt → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.Dn.DownCsdKAlt → RankTwoAutomorphyDescendsFromAbelianCMToRat → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.RR.AutNewformA → Sd.TwC → Sd.AlgCyc → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.GdEndAlt := by
  intro hF hL hD hQ hN hT hC Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ aK l hl c N _ e ψ D ι B hB
  have hσ : Sd.IsCC D.cc := D.conj
  haveI : IsLocalHom (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O) := (dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.1
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField c.O) l := by
    rw [CharP.charP_iff_prime_eq_zero hl.out, ← map_natCast (residue c.O), residue_eq_zero_iff,
      mem_maximalIdeal, mem_nonunits_iff, ← map_natCast (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O)]
    intro hu
    exact PadicInt.prime_p.not_unit (isUnit_of_map_unit _ _ hu)
  obtain ⟨L, _, _, _, _, g₁, Φ, C₀, ⟨k⟩, ok, big, sc⟩ := hF Ω K l c N e ψ D B hB
  have hσ' : IsConjOm D.cc.toRingEquiv := hσ
  have hsq : D.cc * D.cc = 1 := by
    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hσ'
    exact AlgEquiv.ext fun x => j.injective (by
      have h1 : j (D.cc (D.cc x)) = starRingEnd ℂ (j (D.cc x)) := hj (D.cc x)
      have h2 : j (D.cc x) = starRingEnd ℂ (j x) := hj x
      rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, h1, h2, Complex.conj_conj, AlgEquiv.one_apply])
  have hΨ : Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) D.cc = 1 := by
    have h1 : cycZ ℚ Ω l D.cc = -1 := hσ'.cyc (E := ℚ)
    have h2 : chiO ℚ Ω l c.O D.cc = -1 := by
      show Units.map (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O).toMonoidHom (cycZ ℚ Ω l D.cc) = -1
      rw [h1]
      apply Units.ext
      simp
    have h3 : (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ D.cc : c.O) = (-1) ^ e := by
      rw [Chr.dirG, MonoidHom.comp_apply, (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).2.2.2.1 D.cc hσ', MulChar.coe_toUnitHom]
      exact D.gd.par
    apply Units.ext
    show ((chiO ℚ Ω l c.O D.cc ^ (e : ℤ) * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ D.cc : c.Oˣ) : c.O) = 1
    rw [h2, zpow_natCast, Units.val_mul, h3, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, Units.val_neg, Units.val_one, ← mul_pow]
    simp
  have hram : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ D.η.ker →
      B'.valuation ((l * N * (discr K).natAbs : ℕ) : Ω) < 1 := by
    intro B' h
    haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
    have hd : B'.valuation ((discr K : ℤ) : Ω) < 1 := by
      by_contra h4
      refine h fun s hs => ?_
      rw [D.η_ker]
      have key := GlobalTheory.UnramDiscr.embedding_eq B' h4 ((s : Ω →+* Ω).comp (algebraMap K Ω)) (algebraMap K Ω) (fun x => by
        refine hs.2 _ ((B'.valuation_le_one_iff _).mpr (GlobalTheory.UnramDiscr.mem_of_isIntegral B' ?_))
        exact (RingOfIntegers.isIntegral_coe x).map (algebraMap K Ω).toIntAlgHom)
      exact fun x => DFunLike.congr_fun key x
    have hd' : B'.valuation (((discr K).natAbs : ℕ) : Ω) < 1 := by
      rcases Int.natAbs_eq (discr K) with h1 | h1
      · rw [h1, Int.cast_natCast] at hd; exact hd
      · rw [h1, Int.cast_neg, Int.cast_natCast, Valuation.map_neg] at hd; exact hd
    rw [Nat.cast_mul, map_mul]
    exact lt_of_le_of_lt (mul_le_of_le_one_left' ((B'.valuation_le_one_iff _).2 (natCast_mem B' _))) hd'
  have hlM : l ∣ l * N * (discr K).natAbs := (dvd_mul_right l N).mul_right _
  obtain ⟨hfin, haut, ka, hka, haut2⟩ := hL Ω l ι c N e ψ D.gd.e_pos D.gd.N_even D.η D.cc g₁ B _ (dvd_mul_right _ _) hram L Φ C₀ k hB ok big sc
  haveI : IsAlgClosure L Ω := Th.Db.algClo L Ω
  have hLk : LinkHyp c N e D.η D.cc B (l * N * (discr K).natAbs) := ⟨D.gd.e_pos, dvd_mul_right _ _,
    mul_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero hl.out.ne_zero (NeZero.ne N)) (Int.natAbs_ne_zero.2 (discr_ne_zero K)), hram, hB, hσ⟩
  have h3' := h3 Ω l c N e ψ _ _ g₁ B _ hLk L Φ C₀ k ok
  have hK3' := hK3 Ω l c N e ψ D.gd.N_even _ _ g₁ B _ hLk L Φ C₀ k ok big sc
  have h2' := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.Dn.end2' (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.Dn.autEnd2_alt H hPot hIne hQ hN hT hC D k ok hlM hB hLk ι ka hka haut2)
  have h1' := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.Dn.autEnd3_alt hD k ok hlM hσ ι K
  have hk := D.η_ker
  have hq := D.gd.quad
  refine ⟨g₁, marks_of_link k ok hlM hsq hΨ, finEnd_of_finPt k ok hlM hsq hfin, ?_, h2'⟩
  have h0 := fun a b => h1' a b haut
  clear h1' h2' haut haut2
  generalize D.η = η₀ at h0 h3' hK3' hk ⊢
  have hker : ∀ [Algebra K Ω], Base.galOf ℚ K Ω = η₀.ker := by
    rw [hk]
    intro a
    ext s
    exact ⟨fun h => fix_iff hq a.algebraMap aK.algebraMap s h, fun h => fix_iff hq aK.algebraMap a.algebraMap s h⟩
  exact h0 hker fun A _ _ r hr hi _ _ _ ρ hρ => by
    haveI : IsAlgClosure K Ω := Th.Db.algClo K Ω
    have t := h3' A r hr K hker.symm ρ hρ
    exact ⟨t.1, t.2, hK3' A r hr hi K hker.symm ρ hρ⟩
end


[UCE_SRC_LINKED_BOUNDARY] Original revised linked-end route retains full ModUAlt and both actual rank-two and rank-three seed producers
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/ThresholdArguments/DenseThresholdCases/LinkedEndE.lean#L18-L68
theorem UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.linked_endomorphism_dense :
    GlobalTheory.Published.GlobalArtinReciprocity → UpperTheory.V.W.LocCFTAll → _root_.UpperTheory.V.W.ModUAlt → UpperTheory.V.W.LevEx → UpperTheory.V.W.LamEx → UpperTheory.V.W.ProModularRingExists → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Sym.UnitaryThreeAutomorphyDense → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Sd.Seed2Alt → UpperTheory.V.Sd.Twist → UpperTheory.V.Sd.AlgCyc → UpperTheory.V.Sd.AlgRes → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Sym.Pl.LinkedEndEAlt := by
  intro hA _ hMod hLev hLam hB0 hS3 hS2 hTw hCyc hRes Ω _ _ _ l _ ι c _ N _ e ψ he hev η σ g₁ B M hM hram L _ _ _ _ Φ C₀ k hB hok hbig hseed
  obtain ⟨K, _, _, _, N', w, χ', η', θ, j, hD, hne, hord, hη, hcong, hdisc⟩ := hseed
  haveI : IsAlgClosure L Ω :=
    { isAlgClosed := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
      isAlgebraic := haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic ℚ Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgebraic
        Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) L }
  have hM0 : M ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    obtain ⟨hσ, hc⟩ := k.c
    have h1 : σ ∈ η.ker := k.split ⊤ (by rw [← ValuationSubring.mem_nonunits_iff, Nat.cast_zero]; exact zero_mem _) ⟨by
      refine mem_Dec.2 (eq_top_iff.2 fun x _ => ?_)
      rw [ValuationSubring.mem_pointwise_smul_iff_inv_smul_mem]
      exact ValuationSubring.mem_top _, hσ⟩
    have h2 := k.del ⟨σ, hσ⟩
    rw [hc, hok.del_c] at h2
    have h3 : η σ = 1 := h1
    rw [h3] at h2
    exact absurd h2 (by decide)
  have H : UpperTheory.V.Pr.LinkHyp c N e η σ B M := ⟨he, hM, hM0, hram, hB, hD.cc⟩
  have hv : Nonempty Φ.V := by
    by_contra h
    rw [not_nonempty_iff] at h
    have := hbig.1
    simp [UpperTheory.V.Fr.Frame.d] at this
  obtain ⟨v⟩ := hv
  have hker : η.ker = galOf ℚ K Ω := by
    rw [← hD.η_ker]
    ext s
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, MonoidHom.mem_ker, ← Units.val_eq_one, ← Units.val_eq_one, hη s]
    exact_mod_cast Iff.rfl
  haveI := hD.imag
  obtain ⟨κ, hκ⟩ := hTw K Ω hD.quad l hok.l_odd hdisc ι σ hD.cc
  obtain ⟨hk3, hk2⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.kaOf hCyc hRes Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k
  obtain ⟨ka, hk2, hka⟩ := hk2 K hker κ hκ
  obtain ⟨c₁, hle, hL, hv3, hv2, f, hf⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Pr.coefEx l c L Ω _ _ hk3.alg.1 hk2.alg.1
  haveI := UpperTheory.V.Pr.coefIs l c₁
  obtain ⟨m₃, hm₃, -, r₃, -, s₃⟩ := hS3 K L Ω l ι σ N' w χ' η' θ j hD hne M hM0
    (by rw [mul_comm]; exact (Nat.mul_dvd_mul_left l hev.two_dvd).trans hM)
    (UpperTheory.V.Z.Sym.Pl.linkUnr Ω l _ η _ σ g₁ B M L Φ k) (UpperTheory.V.Z.Sym.Pl.chiUnr Ω l c N e ψ χ' j hord hcong M hM L) 2
  obtain ⟨m₂, hm₂, -, r₂, -, -, -, s₂⟩ := hS2 K L Ω l ι σ N' w χ' η' θ j hD κ ka hκ hka 2
  obtain ⟨hfin, a₃, a₂⟩ := _root_.UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.twoRuns_alt hA hMod hLam ι H v hL hk2 hk3
    (UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.cRealAv hLev hB0 Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 2 ka C₀ (Or.inl rfl) hok hbig hle hf hk2 hv2)
    (UpperTheory.Gal.projectedFormsSystemPart.cRealAv hLev hB0 Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 3 _ C₀ (Or.inr rfl) hok hbig hle hf hk3 hv3)
    (fun R₂ => _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.seedPtOf_alt hA Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 2 ka R₂ hk2 χ' j hord hcong m₂ hm₂ _ (by
      convert s₂ using 2 with i i i <;> fin_cases i <;> simp [UpperTheory.V.Fr.w]))
    (fun R₃ => _root_.UpperTheory.V.Pr.seedPtOf_alt hA Ω l ι c N e ψ η σ g₁ B M H L Φ k c₁ f 3 _ R₃ hk3 χ' j hord hcong m₃ hm₃ _ (by
      convert s₃ using 2 with i i i <;> fin_cases i <;> simp [UpperTheory.V.Fr.w]))
  exact ⟨UpperTheory.V.Pr.finPtDown l c c₁ f hle hf Φ 3 hfin, a₃, ka, hk2.alg, a₂⟩
end
